## 1370376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとはギルドのおやじが<line-break>受け付けてくれる。<line-break>早く登録を済ませろ。<end>"
EN: "The old fellow at the Guild will handle the rest. Get yourself registered, and be quick about it.<end>"
KO: "나머지는 길드 아저씨가 접수해 줄 거다. 빨리 등록을 끝내.<end>"

## 1370377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者登録かい？<line-break>あんたみたいに若い人は大歓迎だ。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょっと調べさせてもらうよ。<line-break>信用できる人物かどうか<line-break>確かめなくちゃならないんでな。<end>"
EN: "Here to register as an adventurer? Young people like you are always welcome. Let me run a quick check on you first. I need to make sure you're someone we can trust.<end>"
KO: "모험자 등록인가? 너처럼 젊은 사람은 대환영이지. 잠깐 조사 좀 할게. 믿을 수 있는 사람인지 확인해야 하거든.<end>"

## 1370378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず<line-break>犯罪登録当てはまらず。<line-break>　<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break>あんたも冒険者として<line-break>登録されたよ。<end>"
EN: "The illusion gauge didn't react, and there's no match in the criminal registry. Congratulations. You're now registered as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "환술계 수치도 없고 범죄 등록에도 해당하지 않는군. 축하해. 너도 모험자로 등록됐어.<end>"

## 1370379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<line-break>がんばりな！<end>"
EN: "Now get out there, take on plenty of work, and make a name for yourself. Good luck!<end>"
KO: "이제부터 팍팍 일해서 이름을 날려 줘. 힘내!<end>"

## 1370380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事をこなしながら、<line-break>強くなることだ。<line-break>弱いやつは邪魔なだけだからな。<line-break>仕事の選択は、お前に任せる。<line-break>では、町を出るときに合流しよう。<end>"
EN: "Take on jobs and grow stronger. Weaklings only get in the way. What work you choose is up to you. We'll meet up when you leave town.<end>"
KO: "일을 해내면서 강해져라. 약한 녀석은 방해만 되니까. 어떤 일을 고를지는 네게 맡긴다. 그럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하지.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>。<line-break>戻ったか。<line-break>ご苦労だった。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. You're back. Well done.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 돌아왔군. 수고했다.<end>"

## 1370387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何事だ？<end>"
EN: "W-What was that?<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 1370388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…父上、<line-break>今のが竜王の覚醒…<line-break>その第一声でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Father... Was what we just heard the first cry of the Dragon King's Awakening?<end>"
KO: "…아버님, 방금 것이 용왕의 각성… 그 첫 울음일까요?<end>"

## 1370389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ…。<line-break>この世が乱れるとき<line-break>竜王が覚醒すると言われておる。<line-break>つまり、<line-break>乱世の原因を除くべく<line-break>目覚めるというのだが…。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... It is said that the Dragon King awakens when the world falls into turmoil. In other words, it awakens to eliminate the cause of the upheaval, but...<end>"
KO: "음…. 세상이 어지러워질 때 용왕이 깨어난다고 전해진다. 즉 난세의 원인을 없애기 위해 깨어난다는 뜻인데….<end>"

## 1370390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この地は神器を守るための結界があり、<line-break>魔物は近寄れぬはず…。<line-break>それが先ほども森で遭遇いたしました。<line-break>このことも関係あるのでしょうか。<line-break>破壊神ウルグの復活の時だとしたら…。<end>"
EN: "A barrier protects the sacred artifact here. Monsters should not be able to approach... Yet we encountered one in the forest earlier. Could that be connected as well? If the time has come for Ulugh, God of Destruction, to return...<end>"
KO: "이 땅에는 신기를 지키는 결계가 있어 마물이 가까이 오지 못할 텐데…. 그런데 아까도 숲에서 마물을 만났습니다. 이것도 관계가 있는 걸까요. 파괴신 울그가 부활할 때라면….<end>"

## 1370391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしそうならば、ゆゆしき事態だ。<line-break>ロイ、あの森を調査するのだ。<end>"
EN: "If so, this is dire indeed. Roy, investigate that forest.<end>"
KO: "만약 그렇다면 큰일이다. 로이, 저 숲을 조사해라.<end>"

## 1370392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました、父上。<line-break><value:$28>も、<line-break>連れていってよろしいでしょうか。<end>"
EN: "Understood, Father. May I take <value:$28> with me?<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다, 아버님. <value:$28>도 데려가도 될까요?<end>"

## 1370393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならぬ。<line-break><value:$28>は未熟だ。<line-break>危険すぎる。<end>"
EN: "No. <value:$28> is still inexperienced. It is too dangerous.<end>"
KO: "안 된다. <value:$28>는 아직 미숙하다. 너무 위험해.<end>"

## 1370394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父上。もう、<value:$28>も<line-break>修行に出してもよい年頃。<line-break>私も一緒に参るのです。ご安心を。<end>"
EN: "Father, <value:$28> is old enough to set out for training. I'll be going too, so you needn't worry.<end>"
KO: "아버님. 이제 <value:$28>도 수행을 떠날 나이입니다. 저도 함께 가겠습니다. 안심하십시오.<end>"

## 1370395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ロイがそこまで言うならばよかろう。<line-break><value:$28>も行くがよい。<end>"
EN: "If you feel that strongly, Roy, very well. <value:$28>, you may go too.<end>"
KO: "…로이가 그렇게까지 말한다면 좋다. <value:$28>도 가거라.<end>"

## 1370396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前も、兄、ロイのように、<line-break>神器を守れるだけの力が必要だ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いずれお前も修行に出ることになろう。<line-break>これはその予行と思うがよい。<line-break>ロイとともに調査に行くのだ。<end>"
EN: "Like your brother Roy, you must become strong enough to protect the sacred artifact. In time, you too will set out to train. Consider this a trial run. Go investigate with Roy.<end>"
KO: "너도 형 로이처럼 신기를 지킬 만한 힘이 필요하다. 언젠가 너도 수련을 떠나게 될 거야. 이번 일을 그 예행연습이라 생각해. 로이와 함께 조사하러 가거라.<end>"

## 1370397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者は万事に備えることが肝心だ。<line-break>復活の真珠を持っていくとよい。<end>"
EN: "An adventurer must be prepared for anything. Take the Pearl of Revival with you.<end>"
KO: "모험자는 만사에 대비하는 것이 중요하다. 부활의 진주를 가져가도록 해.<end>"

## 1370398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私は村の広場で待っている。<line-break>用意ができたら来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'll wait in the village square. Come find me when you're ready.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나는 마을 광장에서 기다리겠다. 준비가 되면 와 줘.<end>"

## 1370399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>ソウルについてはロイから<line-break>教わったのか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Has Roy taught you about Souls?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 소울에 대해서는 로이에게 배웠나?<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教わった<end>"
EN: "I have.<end>"
KO: "배웠다<end>"

## 1370402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしい、では、<line-break>私がソウルについて語っておこう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then allow me to explain Souls.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 내가 소울에 대해 이야기해 두지.<end>"

## 1370403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルは、戦いのみならず、<line-break>人生においても、人の人たる証にして<line-break>最も重視すべきもの…。<end>"
EN: "A Soul is the mark of one's humanity, something to be valued above all else in battle and in life...<end>"
KO: "소울은 전투뿐만 아니라 인생에서도 인간이 인간임을 증명하는 것이며 가장 중시해야 할 것….<end>"

## 1370404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何から聞きたい？<end>"
EN: "What would you like to hear about?<end>"
KO: "뭐부터 듣고 싶어?<end>"

KO: "소울이란<end>"

KO: "소울과 스킬<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Magic and Elemental Power<end>"

JP: "すべて理解している<end>"

KO: "모두 이해하고 있다<end>"

## 1370411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルとは…<line-break>人の人たる証にして、<line-break>最も重視すべきもの。            <line-break>戦いにおいても、人生においても、<line-break>それは変わりない。<line-break>いわば、魂の色や形のようなものだ。<end>"
EN: "A Soul is the mark of one's humanity and the thing one should value above all else. This is as true in battle as it is in life. You might call it the color and shape of one's spirit.<end>"
KO: "소울이란… 사람이 사람임을 증명하는 것이며 가장 중시해야 할 것. 전투에서도 인생에서도 그건 변하지 않는다. 말하자면 혼의 색과 형태 같은 것이다.<end>"

## 1370412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルは、経験を積むことで<line-break>変化する性質を持つ。<line-break>ソウルの変化こそが人を形作るのだ。<end>"
EN: "A Soul changes as one gains experience. It is this transformation of the Soul that shapes a person.<end>"
KO: "소울은 경험을 쌓으면서 변화하는 성질이 있다. 소울의 변화야말로 사람을 만드는 것이다.<end>"

## 1370413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルを磨き上げるための<line-break>経験の蓄積を、<line-break>ソウルポイントと呼ぶ。<line-break>さまざまな経験をし、ソウルポイントを<line-break>ためることで、さらに優れたソウルを<line-break>宿せるのだ。<end>"
EN: "The experience you accumulate as you refine a Soul is measured in Soul Points. By gaining all kinds of experience and building up Soul Points, you can embody even better Souls.<end>"
KO: "소울을 단련하기 위해 쌓는 경험을 소울 포인트라고 한다. 여러 경험을 하며 소울 포인트를 모으면 더욱 뛰어난 소울을 깃들게 할 수 있지.<end>"

## 1370414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前のソウルも今はブルーフレアでしか<line-break>ないが、そのうちウォーロックに、<line-break>そしてニルヴァーナになろう。<line-break>人々に施す心を忘れるな。<line-break>さすれば、ソウルポイントは<line-break>着実にたまっていくはずだ。<end>"
EN: "For now, your Soul is only Blue Flare, but in time it may become Warlock, and then Nirvana. Never forget to be generous to others. Do so, and your Soul Points should steadily accumulate.<end>"
KO: "네 소울도 지금은 블루 플레어에 불과하지만 언젠가 워록으로, 그리고 니르바나가 될 거다. 사람들에게 베푸는 마음을 잊지 마라. 그러면 소울 포인트도 착실히 쌓일 거야.<end>"

## 1370415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルはおのれの魂の色や形…。<line-break>といえども、新たに得たソウルは<line-break>自らに宿さねば意味がないのだ。<line-break>新たなソウルを得、それに魅力を<line-break>感じたならば、まず宿す。<end>"
EN: "A Soul is the color and shape of one's own spirit... Even so, a newly acquired Soul is meaningless unless you embody it. When you gain a new Soul that appeals to you, embody it first.<end>"
KO: "소울은 자신의 영혼의 색과 형태…. 하지만 새로 얻은 소울은 자신에게 깃들게 하지 않으면 의미가 없다. 새로운 소울을 얻고 마음에 든다면 우선 깃들게 해라.<end>"

## 1370416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルを宿せば、ソウルがお前を導き、<line-break>お前を育ててくれるだろう。<line-break><line-break>例えば、ウォーロックを宿していれば、<line-break>魔道士として重要な<line-break>ＩＮＴやＭＩＮを伸ばしてくれる。<end>"
EN: "Once embodied, a Soul will guide you and foster your growth. For example, embodying Warlock will improve ＩＮＴ and ＭＩＮ, both vital to a mage.<end>"
KO: "소울을 깃들이면 소울이 너를 이끌고 성장시켜 줄 거야. 예를 들어 워록을 깃들이면 마도사에게 중요한 INT와 MIN을 높여 주지.<end>"

## 1370417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に、戦士系のグロウファイターなら<line-break>これがＳＴＲやＶＩＴとなる。<line-break>そういうことだ。<end>"
EN: "With a warrior Soul such as Grow Fighter, on the other hand, it will improve ＳＴＲ and ＶＩＴ. That is how it works.<end>"
KO: "반대로 전사계의 그로우 파이터라면 이것이 STR이나 VIT가 된다. 그런 뜻이야.<end>"

## 1370418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキルは、ソウルごとにある<line-break>特技や秘技のようなものだ。<line-break> <line-break>普通、スキルはソウルを宿すことにより<line-break>使えるようになる。<line-break>しかし、宿せるソウルはひとつのみ。<end>"
EN: "Skills are special techniques and secret arts associated with each Soul. Ordinarily, embodying a Soul allows you to use its Skills. However, you may embody only one Soul at a time.<end>"
KO: "스킬은 소울마다 있는 특기나 비기 같은 것이다. 보통 스킬은 소울을 깃들임으로써 쓸 수 있게 된다. 하지만 깃들일 수 있는 소울은 하나뿐이다.<end>"

## 1370419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ、そう言うであろうと思った。<line-break>宿していないソウルのスキルは<line-break>使うことができないのか…？<line-break>…できる。ソウルにソウルポイントが<line-break>あるように、スキルには<line-break>スキルポイントが存在する。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I thought you might ask: can you use the Skills of a Soul you are not embodying? You can. Just as Souls have Soul Points, Skills have Skill Points.<end>"
KO: "음, 그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 품고 있지 않은 소울의 스킬은 쓸 수 없는가…? …쓸 수 있다. 소울에 소울 포인트가 있듯 스킬에는 스킬 포인트가 존재한다.<end>"

## 1370420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのスキルポイントを消費して、<line-break>スキルだけを<line-break>身に付けられるのだ。<line-break>スキルポイントを使えば、<line-break>戦士が魔法を使うこともできるのだ。<end>"
EN: "By spending those Skill Points, you can learn the Skill itself. This even allows a warrior to use magic.<end>"
KO: "그 스킬 포인트를 소비해서 스킬만 익힐 수도 있다. 스킬 포인트를 쓰면 전사가 마법을 사용하는 것도 가능하지.<end>"

## 1370421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、魔法のことについても<line-break>語っておいたほうがよいだろう。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I should explain magic as well.<end>"
KO: "그래, 마법에 대해서도 이야기해두는 편이 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1370422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔法には、種類ごとに<line-break>それぞれ固有のレベルがある。<line-break> <line-break>そしてそのレベルは、<line-break>精霊力と密接な関係を持っている。<end>"
EN: "Each type of magic has its own level. That level is closely tied to elemental power.<end>"
KO: "마법에는 종류마다 고유한 레벨이 있다. 그리고 그 레벨은 정령력과 밀접한 관계가 있지.<end>"

## 1370423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、<value:$28>、<line-break>お前が水属性のスライムを<line-break>倒したとしよう。<line-break>すると、お前は水属性の精霊力を<line-break>得られるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Suppose, for example, that you defeated a Water-element Slime, <value:$28>. You would then gain Water elemental power.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 <value:$28>, 네가 물 속성 슬라임을 쓰러뜨렸다고 하자. 그러면 너는 물 속성 정령력을 얻게 된다.<end>"

## 1370424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊力が一定だけたまれば<line-break>精霊力レベルが上昇し、<line-break>それにつれて使える魔法も増える。<end>"
EN: "Once you accumulate enough elemental power, its level will rise, increasing the number of spells available to you.<end>"
KO: "정령력이 일정량 쌓이면 정령력 레벨이 오르고, 그에 따라 쓸 수 있는 마법도 늘어난다.<end>"

## 1370425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "水属性ならば、最初は１レベルの<line-break>アクアしか使えないが、<line-break>３レベルではポイズン、<line-break>４レベルではインビジブルを<line-break>覚えられるのだ。<end>"
EN: "For the Water element, you can initially use only the level-one spell Aqua. At level three you can learn Poison, and at level four, Invisible.<end>"
KO: "물 속성이라면 처음엔 레벨 1 아쿠아만 쓸 수 있지만 레벨 3엔 포이즌, 레벨 4엔 인비저블을 익힐 수 있다.<end>"

## 1370426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伸ばしたい属性の敵と<line-break>集中的に戦うのも、有効だろう。<end>"
EN: "Concentrating on enemies of the element you wish to develop may also prove effective.<end>"
KO: "키우고 싶은 속성의 적과 집중적으로 싸우는 것도 효과적이겠지.<end>"

## 1370427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にすべて理解しているのだな？<end>"
EN: "Are you certain you understand all of this?<end>"
KO: "정말 전부 이해하고 있는 거지?<end>"

## 1370428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説明してほしい<end>"
EN: "I'd like an explanation.<end>"
KO: "설명해 줬으면 해<end>"

## 1370429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "理解した<end>"
EN: "I understand.<end>"
KO: "이해했다<end>"

## 1370430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>お前自身にかかわることだ。<line-break>お前の言葉を信じよう。<line-break>ロイの元へ急ぐがよい…。<end>"
EN: "I see... This concerns you directly, so I'll take you at your word. Now hurry to Roy...<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 너 자신과 관련된 일이다. 네 말을 믿겠다. 로이에게 서둘러 가도록 해….<end>"

## 1370431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしい、わからぬことを尋ねるのは<line-break>恥ではないぞ。<line-break>わかるまで聞くといい。<end>"
EN: "Good. There is no shame in asking about what you do not understand. Ask until everything is clear.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 모르는 걸 묻는 건 부끄러운 일이 아니다. 알 때까지 물어보면 된다.<end>"

KO: "정말 전부 이해하고 있는 거지?<end>"

## 1370435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前自身にかかわることだ。<line-break>お前の言葉を信じよう。<line-break>ロイの元へ急ぐがよい…。<end>"
EN: "This concerns you personally. I will take you at your word. Make haste to Roy...<end>"

## 1370436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしい、わからぬことを尋ねるのは<line-break>恥ではないぞ。<line-break>わかるまで聞くといい。<end>"
EN: "Good. There is no shame in asking about what you do not understand. Keep asking until you do.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 모르는 걸 묻는 건 부끄러운 일이 아니다. 알 때까지 물어보면 된다.<end>"

## 1370438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを、ロイに渡してほしい。<line-break>ロイには苦労をかけているからな。<end>"
EN: "Please give this to Roy. I've placed a heavy burden on him.<end>"
KO: "이걸 로이에게 전해줘. 로이에겐 고생을 많이 시키고 있으니까.<end>"

## 1370439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前もロイと同じように、<line-break>神器の守護者となるべく<line-break>修行の旅に出る年になったのだな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You've reached the age when you, too, must set out on a journey of training to become a Guardian of the sacred artifact, as Roy did...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너도 로이처럼 신기의 수호자가 되기 위해 수행 여행을 떠날 나이가 되었구나….<end>"

## 1370440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、私らしくもない。<line-break>こんなことを言うようではいかんな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Heh. This isn't like me. I shouldn't speak this way.<end>"
KO: "후후, 나답지 않군. 이런 말을 해선 안 되겠어.<end>"

## 1370441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなるのだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Grow strong.<end>"
KO: "…강해져라.<end>"

## 1370442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3どうしたのだ。<line-break>ロイとともに村の外の森を<line-break>調べてくるがよい。<end>心配はいらぬ。<line-break>ロイがついているのだからな。<line-break>フフ…、私も親バカなことだ。<end>私はよい子供たちに恵まれたものだ。<line-break>万一、今ここで私が死んでも<line-break>神器のことは安心して任せられる。<line-break>万一の話だ。<line-break>そのような顔をするでない。<line-break>我が子よ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3What is it? Go with Roy and investigate the forest outside the village.<end>There's no need to worry. Roy will be with you. Heh... What a doting parent I am.<end>I have been blessed with fine children. Even if I were to die here and now, I could entrust the sacred artifact to you without fear. I said if. Don't look at me like that, my child.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3무슨 일이냐. 로이와 함께 마을 밖 숲을 조사하고 오거라.<end>걱정할 것 없다. 로이가 함께 있지 않느냐. 후후… 나도 자식 바보로군.<end>나는 좋은 아이들을 두었구나. 만일 지금 여기서 내가 죽더라도 신기의 일은 안심하고 맡길 수 있겠다. 만일의 이야기다. 그런 얼굴 하지 마라. 내 아이야.<end>"

## 1370447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、いわば冒険者たちの<line-break>事務所みてえなもんだよな。<line-break><line-break>ここで受けた依頼をこなして、<line-break>冒険者は日々のかてを<line-break>得るってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you could say it's like an office for adventurers. They make their living by taking on jobs here and seeing them through.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 말하자면 모험자들의 사무소 같은 거지. 여기서 받은 의뢰를 해내면서 모험자들은 하루하루 먹고사는 거야.<end>"

## 1370448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、根本的な質問だな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, going straight to the heart of it, are you?<end>"
KO: "오, 근본적인 질문이군.<end>"

## 1370449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "張り紙がしてありますね。<end>"
EN: "There's a notice posted here.<end>"
KO: "벽보가 붙어 있네요.<end>"

## 1370450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、何について説明しようか？<end>"
EN: "Now then, what shall I explain?<end>"
KO: "자, 무엇에 대해 설명할까?<end>"

## 1370451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、昨日のケガは大丈夫か？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hey. That wound from yesterday giving you any trouble?<end>"
KO: "어이, 어제 다친 곳은 괜찮아?<end>"

## 1370452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…にしても、あの怪物、いったい<line-break>どっから入り込んで来やがったのか。<line-break>　<line-break>平民地区が怪物に襲われてても<line-break>騎士団の連中、また出てこなかったな。<line-break>平民地区は俺らで守っていかないとな。<end>"
EN: "Still, where the hell did that monster get in from? The knights didn't show up again, even with the commoners' quarter under attack. Looks like we'll have to defend it ourselves.<end>"
KO: "…그런데 그 괴물, 대체 어디서 들어온 거야. 평민 지구가 괴물한테 습격당했는데 기사단 놈들은 또 안 나왔군. 평민 지구는 우리 손으로 지켜야겠어.<end>"

## 1370453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでお前、この街で保管されていた　　<line-break>聖杯が盗まれたって話は知ってっか？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "By the way, did you hear the Grail kept here in town was stolen?<end>"
KO: "그런데 너, 이 도시에서 보관하던 성배가 도둑맞았다는 얘기 알아?<end>"

## 1370454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、お前には話してなかったか。<line-break>実はな、昨日のモンスター騒ぎの間に、　　<line-break>何者かに聖杯が盗まれちまったんだ。<line-break>で、調べたところ、ちょうどそのころ<line-break>人の言葉をしゃべるゴブリンの３匹組が<line-break>うろついていたことがわかってな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, didn't I tell you? Someone stole the Grail during all that trouble with the monster yesterday. Turns out a group of three goblins who could speak the human tongue had been prowling around at about the same time.<end>"
KO: "아, 너한텐 아직 말 안 했던가. 사실 어제 몬스터 소동이 벌어진 사이 누군가 성배를 훔쳐 갔어. 조사해 보니 딱 그 무렵 사람 말을 하는 고블린 세 마리가 돌아다녔다는 걸 알아냈지.<end>"

## 1370455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉をしゃべるゴブリンなんて<line-break>今まで聞いたことがない。<line-break>な、怪しいと思うだろ？<end>"
EN: "I've never heard of goblins that could talk. Sounds suspicious, doesn't it?<end>"
KO: "말하는 고블린이라니 지금까지 들어 본 적도 없어. 수상하다고 생각하지?<end>"

## 1370456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスター騒ぎも落ち着いたし、<line-break>そろそろ、街の連中も聖杯がないことに<line-break>気づくだろう。となると大騒ぎだ。<end>"
EN: "Now that the monster trouble's died down, it won't be long before the townsfolk notice the Grail is missing. Then all hell will break loose.<end>"
KO: "몬스터 소동도 가라앉았고 슬슬 마을 사람들도 성배가 없다는 걸 눈치채겠지. 그러면 큰 소동이 날 거야.<end>"

## 1370458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、じゃ、ゴブリンどもの<line-break>居場所がわかり次第頼む。<line-break>　<line-break>それまで…そうだな、ひとつ、<line-break>頼みたいことがあるんだが、<line-break>聞いてくれねえか？<end>"
EN: "Good. I'll call on you as soon as I find out where those goblins are. Until then... let's see. There's one other thing I'd like you to do. Will you hear me out?<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 고블린 녀석들의 위치를 알게 되는 대로 부탁하지. 그때까지… 그래, 하나 부탁하고 싶은 게 있는데 들어 줄래?<end>"

## 1370459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まさか師匠、<line-break>あの恐ろしい仕事を…。<end>"
EN: "M-Master, you can't mean... that terrible job...<end>"
KO: "서, 설마 스승님, 그 무시무시한 일을….<end>"

## 1370460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるせえ！<line-break>お前らが行きたくねえって<line-break>言うのが悪いんじゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "Shut up! It's your own fault for refusing to go!<end>"
KO: "시끄러워! 너희가 가기 싫다고 한 게 잘못이잖아.<end>"

## 1370461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だって…。<end>"
EN: "B-But...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만….<end>"

## 1370462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやあ、実は、<line-break>酒場にツケがたまっていてな。<line-break>　<line-break>俺よりたくさんツケがたまってる<line-break>鍛冶屋の親父とワリカンして<line-break>払うって約束になってんだ。<end>"
EN: "Well, the truth is, I've run up quite a tab at the tavern. I promised to split the payment with the old blacksmith, who's run up an even bigger one than mine.<end>"
KO: "사실 술집 외상이 쌓여 있어서 말이지. 나보다 외상이 더 많은 대장간 아저씨와 반씩 내기로 약속했거든.<end>"

## 1370463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺って太っ腹だろ？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>で、すまねえが、こいつと<line-break>鍛冶屋の親父の分をあわせて、<line-break>酒場のフェルムに払ってきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Generous of me, eh? Anyway, sorry to ask, but combine this with the old blacksmith's share and use it to pay Ferme at the tavern.<end>"
KO: "나 꽤 통 크지? 그런데 미안하지만 이거랑 대장장이 아저씨 몫을 합쳐서 술집의 페름에게 가져다줘.<end>"

## 1370464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "駄賃は先に渡しとく。<line-break>お前も、冒険者の卵なら、<line-break>小銭よりこっちのが入用だろ？<end>"
EN: "I'll give you your payment up front. As a budding adventurer, you'll get more use out of this than a few coins, right?<end>"
KO: "품삯은 먼저 줄게. 너도 모험가 새내기라면 푼돈보다 이쪽이 더 필요하겠지?<end>"

## 1370465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、頼んだぜ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1370466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、昨日のケガは大丈夫か？<end>"
EN: "Hey. That wound from yesterday giving you any trouble?<end>"
KO: "어이, 어제 다친 곳은 괜찮아?<end>"

## 1370467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…にしても、あの怪物、いったい<line-break>どっから入り込んで来やがったのか。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>平民地区が怪物に襲われてても<line-break>騎士団の連中、また出てこなかったな。<line-break>平民地区は俺らで守っていかないとな。<end>"
EN: "Still, where the hell did that monster get in from? The knights didn't show up again, even with the commoners' quarter under attack. Looks like we'll have to defend it ourselves.<end>"
KO: "…그런데 그 괴물, 대체 어디서 들어온 거야. 평민 지구가 괴물한테 습격당했는데 기사단 놈들은 또 안 나왔군. 평민 지구는 우리 손으로 지켜야겠어.<end>"

## 1370468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでお前、この街で保管されていた　　<line-break>聖杯が盗まれたって話は知ってっか？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "By the way, did you hear the Grail kept here in town was stolen?<end>"
KO: "그런데 너, 이 도시에서 보관하던 성배가 도둑맞았다는 얘기 알아?<end>"

## 1370469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、お前には話してなかったか。<line-break>実はな、昨日のモンスター騒ぎの間に、　　<line-break>何者かに聖杯が盗まれちまったんだ。<line-break>で、調べたところ、ちょうどそのころ<line-break>人の言葉をしゃべるゴブリンの３匹組が<line-break>うろついていたことがわかってな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, didn't I tell you? Someone stole the Grail during all that trouble with the monster yesterday. Turns out a group of three goblins who could speak the human tongue had been prowling around at about the same time.<end>"
KO: "아, 너한텐 아직 말 안 했던가. 사실 어제 몬스터 소동이 벌어진 사이 누군가 성배를 훔쳐 갔어. 조사해 보니 딱 그 무렵 사람 말을 하는 고블린 세 마리가 돌아다녔다는 걸 알아냈지.<end>"

## 1370470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉をしゃべるゴブリンなんて　　　　　　<line-break>今まで聞いたことがない。<line-break>な、怪しいと思うだろ？<end>"
EN: "I've never heard of goblins that could talk. Sounds suspicious, doesn't it?<end>"
KO: "말하는 고블린이라니 지금까지 들어 본 적도 없어. 수상하다고 생각하지?<end>"

## 1370471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスター騒ぎも落ち着いたし、<line-break>そろそろ、街の連中も聖杯がないことに<line-break>気づくだろう。となると大騒ぎだ。<line-break>そうなる前に調査をしておきたくてな。<line-break>俺の読みじゃ、ゴブリンはゼグナ鉱山に<line-break>潜んでる。どうだ、行ってくれるか？<end>"
EN: "Now that the monster trouble's died down, it won't be long before the townsfolk notice the Grail is missing. Then all hell will break loose. I want to investigate before that happens. My guess is the goblins are hiding in Zegna Mine. What do you say? Will you go?<end>"
KO: "몬스터 소동도 진정됐고, 이제 슬슬 마을 사람들도 성배가 없다는 걸 눈치채겠지. 그러면 큰 소동이야. 그 전에 조사를 해 두고 싶어서 말이야. 내 생각엔 고블린들이 제그나 광산에 숨어 있어. 어때, 가 줄 수 있겠나?<end>"

## 1370475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。んじゃ、頼んだ。<line-break>ま、お前ひとりで十分だろうが…、　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>念のため、お前の幼なじみも<line-break>あとから行かせる。<line-break>仲よくやるんだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good. Then I'm counting on you. You can probably handle it alone, but just in case, I'll send your childhood friend after you. Try to get along.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 부탁한다. 뭐, 너 혼자서도 충분하겠지만… 혹시 모르니 네 소꿉친구도 뒤따라 보내겠다. 사이좋게 지내라.<end>"

## 1370476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昨日、アイリーンに助けられたこと、<line-break>まーだ、気にしてんのか？<line-break>だったら、ちょうどいいじゃねえか。<line-break>今日はお前がアイリーンを<line-break>助けてやんな。<line-break>それで貸し借りはなしだ。<end>"
EN: "Still bothered that Irene saved you yesterday? Then this works out perfectly. Today, you can save Irene. That'll make you even.<end>"
KO: "어제 아이린에게 도움받은 거, 아직도 신경 쓰이냐? 그럼 딱 좋잖아. 오늘은 네가 아이린을 도와줘. 그걸로 빚은 없는 거다.<end>"

## 1370477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いつも言ってるはずだぞ。<line-break>一番大切なのは仲間だってな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I've told you often enough: nothing matters more than your comrades.<end>"
KO: "…늘 말했을 텐데. 가장 중요한 건 동료라고.<end>"

## 1370478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、わかったら行ってこい。<line-break>念のため、薬を渡しておこう。<line-break>ケガをしたら、すぐに使うんだ。<end>"
EN: "Now that you understand, get going. Here, take some medicine just in case. If you get hurt, use it right away.<end>"
KO: "자, 알았으면 다녀와. 혹시 모르니 약을 줄게. 다치면 바로 써.<end>"

## 1370479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ああ。ついでだ。<line-break>悪いが、途中で酒場に寄って、<line-break>こいつをフェルムに渡してきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, one more thing. Sorry, but stop by the tavern on your way and give this to Ferme.<end>"
KO: "…아, 내친김에 하나 더. 미안하지만 가는 길에 술집에 들러 이걸 페름에게 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1370482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>か。<line-break><line-break><line-break>最近、あちこちで物騒な話が増えて<line-break>きているみたいなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Sounds like there's been more and more trouble all over the place lately.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 요즘 여기저기 험한 소문이 늘어난 것 같아.<end>"

## 1370483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "偉い人があてにならないんだから<line-break>俺たちで街を守らないとな。<end>"
EN: "We can't count on the bigwigs, so we'll have to protect the town ourselves.<end>"
KO: "높으신 분들이 못 미더우니 우리끼리라도 마을을 지켜야지.<end>"

## 1370484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前たちふたりなら<line-break>任せても問題ないだろう。<line-break>聖杯とゴブリンのこと頼んだぞ。<line-break>それと酒代のほうもな。<line-break>酒場なら街から出るときに前を通るし、<line-break>ついでだろ？　悪いな。<end>"
EN: "I can trust the two of you to handle it. Take care of the Grail and Goblin business, and my bar tab too. You'll pass the tavern on your way out of town anyway, right? Sorry to put you out.<end>"
KO: "너희 둘이라면 맡겨도 문제없겠지. 성배와 고블린 일 부탁한다. 그리고 술값도 말이야. 술집은 마을을 나갈 때 앞을 지나가니 겸사겸사잖아? 미안하다.<end>"

## 1370485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　どうしたんだ？<line-break>さっき、お前の幼なじみが来たから、<line-break>聖杯とゴブリンの話はしておいたぞ。<line-break>今ごろ、門の辺りで待ってるだろう。<line-break>早く行ってやんな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hm? What's wrong? Your childhood friend came by earlier, so I explained the Grail and Goblin business. Should be waiting near the gate by now. Better get going.<end>"
KO: "응? 왜 그래? 아까 네 소꿉친구가 왔길래 성배와 고블린 이야기는 해 뒀어. 지금쯤 문 근처에서 기다리고 있겠지. 빨리 가 봐.<end>"

## 1370486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、来たか、<value:$28>。<line-break>例のしゃべるゴブリンどもの<line-break>居場所の見当がついたぞ。<line-break>思ったとおりだ。<line-break>ゼグナ鉱山にいるらしい。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. I've got a lead on where those talking Goblins are hiding. Just as I thought. Seems they're in Zegna Mine.<end>"
KO: "오, 왔구나 <value:$28>. 그 말하는 고블린 녀석들의 위치를 알아냈어. 생각대로 제그나 광산에 있는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1370487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あそこは、とりあえず身を隠すのに<line-break>もってこいの場所だからな。<line-break>行ってくれるな？<end>"
EN: "It's the perfect place to lie low for a while. You'll go, won't you?<end>"
KO: "거긴 일단 몸을 숨기기 딱 좋은 곳이니까. 가 줄 거지?<end>"

## 1370488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。んじゃ、頼んだ。<line-break>ま、お前ひとりで十分だろうが…、<line-break>　<line-break>念のため、お前の幼なじみも<line-break>あとから行かせる。<line-break>仲よくやるんだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good. I'm counting on you. You should be more than enough on your own, but just to be safe, I'll send your childhood friend along later. Try to get along.<end>"

## 1370489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You're still bothered that Irene saved you yesterday? Then this works out perfectly. Today, you can save Irene. That'll make you even.<end>"

## 1370490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いつも言ってるはずだぞ。<line-break>一番大切なのは仲間だってな。<line-break>　<end>"

## 1370491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気合入ってんのは認めるがよ、<line-break>今のお前のウデじゃ、<line-break>無茶になりがちだ。<line-break>ま、逆に言や、気合の面では文句ナシ、<line-break>いいソウルを持ってやがるってこった。<end>"
EN: "I'll grant you've got plenty of spirit, but with your skills as they are, you're liable to push yourself too far. Put another way, though, no one can fault your drive. You've got a good Soul in you.<end>"
KO: "기합이 들어간 건 인정하지만 지금 네 실력으론 무리하기 쉽다. 반대로 말하면 기합만큼은 흠잡을 데 없고 좋은 소울을 지녔다는 거지.<end>"

## 1370493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頭でっかちになりがちな、<line-break>どっかのフヌケどもとは違ってな。<end>"
EN: "Unlike certain spineless fools who spend too much time in their own heads.<end>"
KO: "머리만 커지기 쉬운 어디의 얼빠진 녀석들과는 다르지.<end>"

## 1370494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしよけりゃ、<line-break>俺が少し知恵を授けてやろうと<line-break>思うんだが…。いらねえか？<end>"
EN: "If you'd like, I could teach you a thing or two. Not interested?<end>"
KO: "괜찮다면, 내가 조금 지혜를 알려 줄까하는데…. 필요 없어?<end>"

## 1370495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いする<end>"
EN: "I'd appreciate that.<end>"
KO: "부탁한다<end>"

## 1370496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はそれどころじゃない<end>"
EN: "I don't have time for that right now.<end>"
KO: "지금은 그럴 때가 아니야.<end>"

## 1370497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし来た！<line-break>んじゃ、どっから教えてやろうか？<line-break>オッシ道場じゃ、遠慮はタブーだぜ。<end>"
EN: "That's what I like to hear! So, where should we start? At the Ossi School, holding back is strictly taboo.<end>"
KO: "좋아! 그럼 어디부터 가르쳐 줄까? 오시 도장에선 사양하는 게 금지라고.<end>"

## 1370498

## 1370503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべて理解している<end>"
EN: "I understand everything.<end>"
KO: "모두 이해하고 있다<end>"

## 1370504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウル…そうだな、<line-break>とらえ方によっていろいろ言えるが、<line-break>魂の色や形のようなもんだな。<line-break>ソウルは冒険で経験を<line-break>積むことによって<line-break>磨きあげることができる。<end>"
EN: "A Soul... Well, there are plenty of ways to look at it, but you might call it the color and shape of your spirit. You can hone your Soul by gaining experience on your adventures.<end>"
KO: "소울… 그래, 보는 방식에 따라 여러 가지로 말할 수 있지만 영혼의 색이나 형태 같은 거다. 소울은 모험으로 경험을 쌓으며 갈고닦을 수 있어.<end>"

## 1370505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "磨き上げるというよりも、形を変える、<line-break>と言ったほうがいいとこもあるけどな。<end>"
EN: "Though sometimes it's more accurate to say you're reshaping it than honing it.<end>"
KO: "갈고닦는다기보다 형태를 바꾼다고 하는 편이 맞는 부분도 있지만 말이지.<end>"

## 1370506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルを磨きあげるための経験を、<line-break>冒険者たちは、ソウルポイントと<line-break>呼んでる。覚えておけよ。<line-break>ソウルポイントが一定までたまると、<line-break>さらに優れたソウルを<line-break>宿せるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Adventurers call the experience that hones a Soul “Soul Points.” Remember that. Once you've accumulated enough Soul Points, you can host an even better Soul.<end>"
KO: "소울을 갈고닦는 경험을 모험가들은 소울 포인트라고 불러. 기억해 둬. 소울 포인트가 일정량 쌓이면 더 뛰어난 소울을 품을 수 있어.<end>"

## 1370507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、グロウファイターだった者が、<line-break>ソウルポイントをためることで、<line-break>マイティブロウとなることができる。<end>"
EN: "For instance, someone with the Grow Fighter Soul can accumulate Soul Points and become a Mighty Blow.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 그로우 파이터였던 자가 소울 포인트를 모으면 마이티 블로가 될 수 있다.<end>"

## 1370508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてそのソウルポイントは、<line-break>依頼をこなしたりして、<line-break>人に尽くすことで得られるもんさ。<line-break>ひょっとするとお前も、<line-break>今回のお使いで少しばかりは<line-break>身についたんじゃねえか？<end>"
EN: "You earn those Soul Points by completing requests and helping people. You might have picked up a few from this last errand yourself.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 소울 포인트는 의뢰를 해내거나 남을 위해 힘쓰면 얻을 수 있는 거야. 어쩌면 너도 이번 심부름으로 조금쯤은 얻었을지도 모르겠는데?<end>"

## 1370509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、あとでさっそく試してみるといい。<line-break>グロウファイターくらいは<line-break>宿せるかもしれないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Give it a try later. You might already be able to host a Grow Fighter Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나중에 바로 시험해 봐. 그로우 파이터 정도는 깃들일 수 있을지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1370510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いたことがあるであります！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ソウルポイントで得た新しいソウルは、<line-break>ただ心の中に留めているだけでは<line-break>意味がねえってことであります！<end>"
EN: "I've heard about this, sir! A new Soul gained through Soul Points is useless if it merely remains within your heart, sir!<end>"
KO: "들은 적 있습니다! 소울 포인트로 얻은 새로운 소울은 마음속에 두기만 해서는 의미가 없다는 겁니다!<end>"

## 1370511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、重要なとこだ。<line-break>ソウルは、<line-break>宿してこそ初めて意味がある。<line-break>そうしないことにゃ、<line-break>剣を買ってサヤから抜かずに<line-break>敵と戦おうとするようなもんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Right. That's the important part. A Soul only means something once you host it. Otherwise, it's like buying a sword and trying to fight without ever drawing it from the scabbard.<end>"
KO: "그래, 중요한 부분이지. 소울은 깃들게 해야 비로소 의미가 있어. 그러지 않으면 검을 사 놓고 칼집에서 뽑지도 않은 채 적과 싸우려는 것과 다를 게 없지.<end>"

## 1370512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きちんとソウルを宿しゃ、<line-break>剣を振ることでウデのいい剣士が<line-break>育っていくように、<line-break>それぞれのソウルが<line-break>お前を成長させてくれる。<end>"
EN: "Host a Soul properly and each one will help you grow, just as wielding a sword develops you into a better swordsman.<end>"
KO: "제대로 소울을 품으면 검을 휘둘러 훌륭한 검사로 성장하듯, 각각의 소울이 너를 성장시켜 준다.<end>"

## 1370513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、グロウファイターのソウルを<line-break>宿しておけば、<line-break><line-break>レベルアップによって、<line-break>戦士として重要な<line-break>ＳＴＲとＶＩＴを伸ばしてくれるし、<end>"
EN: "For example, if you host a Grow Fighter Soul, leveling up will increase ＳＴＲ and ＶＩＴ, which are important for a warrior.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어, 그로우 파이터의 소울을깃들여 두면, 레벨업을 통해, 전사에게 중요한STR과 VIT를 올려 주고,<end>"

## 1370514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウォーロックを宿せば、<line-break>魔術師に重要なＩＮＴとＭＩＮを<line-break>伸ばしてくれるってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Host a Warlock, and it'll increase ＩＮＴ and ＭＩＮ, the attributes that matter to a mage.<end>"
KO: "워록을 품으면 마술사에게 중요한 INT와 MIN을 올려 주는 거지.<end>"

## 1370515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ためになるであります！<end>"
EN: "Most instructive, sir!<end>"
KO: "도움이 되는 내용입니다!<end>"

## 1370516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキルのことだな。<end>"
EN: "You want to know about Skills.<end>"
KO: "스킬 이야기군.<end>"

## 1370517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキルってのはだな、<line-break>そのソウル独自の特技や<line-break>必殺技みてえなもんだと思ってくれ。<line-break>基本的に、スキルはソウルを<line-break>宿すことによって使えるようになる。<end>"
EN: "Think of Skills as special techniques and finishing moves unique to a particular Soul. As a rule, you gain access to Skills by hosting the Soul they belong to.<end>"
KO: "스킬이라는 건 그 소울만의 특기나 필살기 같은 거라고 생각해. 기본적으로 스킬은 소울을 깃들게 하면 사용할 수 있게 돼.<end>"

## 1370518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば攻撃魔法を使いたいと思ったら、<line-break>まずはウォーロックを宿す他にはねえ。<line-break>ファイアやアクアなんかの魔法は、<line-break>ウォーロックを宿すことによって<line-break>身につくスキル、ロースペルに<line-break>含まれるからだ。<end>"
EN: "Say you want to use attack magic. First, you'll have to host a Warlock. Spells such as Fire and Aqua are part of the Low Spell Skill you learn by hosting one.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 공격 마법을 쓰고 싶다면 우선 워록을 깃들이게 하는 수밖에 없어. 파이어나 아쿠아 같은 마법은 워록을 깃들이면 익히는 스킬, 로 스펠에 포함되기 때문이지.<end>"

## 1370519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1370520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ先生の教えは、<line-break>新米の自分にも、<line-break>とてもためになるであります！<end>"
EN: "Master Ossi's teachings are most instructive, even for a novice such as myself, sir!<end>"
KO: "오시 선생님의 가르침은, 신참인 저에게도, 정말 큰 도움이 됩니다!<end>"

## 1370521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生、質問があるであります！<end>"
EN: "Master, I have a question, sir!<end>"
KO: "선생님, 질문이 있습니다!<end>"

## 1370522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、なんだ。<end>"
EN: "All right. What is it?<end>"
KO: "어, 왜.<end>"

## 1370523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔道士系のウォーロックを宿していては<line-break>剣での攻撃力は<line-break>あまり伸びないはずであります。<line-break>しかし、<line-break>剣では倒すのが難しい敵もおります。<end>"
EN: "If one hosts a mage-type Warlock, one's Attack Power with a sword should hardly increase, sir. Yet some enemies are difficult to defeat with a sword.<end>"
KO: "마도사 계통의 워록을 품고 있으면 검 공격력은 그다지 늘지 않을 겁니다. 하지만 검으로 쓰러뜨리기 어려운 적도 있습니다.<end>"

## 1370524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦士として成長しながら<line-break>魔法も使えるようになるには、<line-break>どうすればいいんでありますか？<end>"
EN: "How can one grow as a warrior while also learning to use magic, sir?<end>"
KO: "전사로 성장하면서 마법도 쓸 수 있게 되려면 어떻게 해야 합니까?<end>"

## 1370525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい質問だな。<end>"
EN: "Good question.<end>"
KO: "좋은 질문이네.<end>"

## 1370526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "腕きき冒険者なら、たいてい<line-break>ウォーロックを宿していなくても、<line-break>ロースペルくらいは使える。<line-break>こういう芸当ができるのは、<line-break>ソウルを宿さなくても、<line-break>スキルだけを獲得できるからだ。<end>"
EN: "Most seasoned adventurers can use something like Low Spell without hosting a Warlock. They can pull that off because it's possible to acquire the Skill itself without hosting the Soul.<end>"
KO: "실력 있는 모험가라면 대부분 워록을 깃들이지 않아도 로우 스펠 정도는 쓸 수 있어. 이런 게 가능한 건 소울을 깃들이지 않아도 스킬만 얻을 수 있기 때문이지.<end>"

## 1370527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルにソウルポイントがあるように、<line-break>スキルにはスキルポイントがある。<line-break>ま、言うなれば、実戦経験だな。<end>"
EN: "Just as Souls have Soul Points, Skills have Skill Points. You could call them practical combat experience.<end>"
KO: "소울에 소울 포인트가 있듯 스킬에는 스킬 포인트가 있다. 뭐, 말하자면 실전 경험이지.<end>"

## 1370528
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキルポイントを使って<line-break>スキルを獲得したら、<line-break>そいつはずっとお前のもんだ。<line-break>ギアを払って飯を食うのと一緒だな。<line-break>別のソウルに宿し替えても<line-break>そのスキルは続けて使えるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Once you spend Skill Points to acquire a Skill, it's yours for good. It's like paying Gea for a meal. Even if you switch to another Soul, you can keep using that Skill.<end>"
KO: "스킬 포인트를 써서 스킬을 얻으면 그건 계속 네 거야. 기어를 내고 밥을 먹는 것과 같지. 다른 소울로 바꿔 깃들여도 그 스킬은 계속 쓸 수 있어.<end>"

## 1370529
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキルポイントは戦闘に勝つとたまる。<line-break>強くなりゃ、何十ものスキルを<line-break>使いこなすことになるだろうよ。<end>"
EN: "You accumulate Skill Points by winning battles. Once you're strong enough, you'll be making use of dozens of Skills.<end>"
KO: "스킬 포인트는 전투에서 이기면 쌓여. 강해지면 수십 가지 스킬을 다루게 될 거다.<end>"

## 1370530
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ先生も得意としていた<line-break>タワーブレイブでもって、俺も、<line-break>隊の父親的存在になるであります！<end>"
EN: "With Tower Brave, Master Ossi's own specialty, I too shall become a father figure to the unit, sir!<end>"
KO: "오시 선생님도 특기로 삼았던 타워 브레이브로 저도 부대의 아버지 같은 존재가 되겠습니다!<end>"

## 1370531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつも言ってるが、まだお前は、<line-break>何もスキルのない<line-break>ブルーフレアのままだがな。<end>"
EN: "As I keep telling you, you're still a Blue Flare without a single Skill.<end>"
KO: "늘 말하지만, 아직 넌 아무 스킬도 없는 블루 플레어 그대로지만 말이지.<end>"

## 1370532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まいったであります…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you have me there, sir...<end>"
KO: "차, 참패했습니다….<end>"

## 1370533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺もロースペルくらいは使えるが、<line-break>魔法は奥が深いぜ。<line-break><line-break>エンシャントにゃ、<line-break>魔道アカデミーがあるくらいだ。<end>"
EN: "I can use Low Spell myself, but there's a lot more to magic. Ancient even has an entire Magic Academy devoted to it.<end>"
KO: "나도 로우 스펠 정도는 쓸 수 있지만 마법은 깊이가 있어. 엔샨트에는 마도 아카데미까지 있으니까.<end>"

## 1370534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じロースペルでも<line-break>覚えやすいのとそうじゃねえのがある。<line-break>ま、レベルがあるってことだな。<line-break>順序を追って話すとだな…<line-break>世の中には精霊力ってのがあって、<line-break>火、水、土、風の４属性がある。<end>"
EN: "Even among Low Spells, some are easier to learn than others. In other words, they have levels. To start at the beginning, there's a force in the world called elemental power, divided into four elements: fire, water, earth, and wind.<end>"
KO: "같은 로 스펠이라도 배우기 쉬운 것과 어려운 게 있어. 뭐, 레벨이 있다는 거지. 순서대로 말하자면… 세상에는 정령력이라는 게 있고 불, 물, 땅, 바람의 네 속성이 있다.<end>"

## 1370535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "各精霊力は、<line-break>倒した敵の属性によって<line-break>たまっていく。<line-break>例えば、<line-break>風属性のタイガーワスプを倒せば、<line-break>風属性の精霊力ポイントがたまる。<end>"
EN: "Your elemental power builds according to the element of the enemies you defeat. For example, defeating a Wind-element Tiger Wasp earns you Wind elemental power points.<end>"
KO: "각 정령력은 쓰러뜨린 적의 속성에 따라 쌓인다. 예를 들어 풍속성 타이거 와스프를 쓰러뜨리면 풍속성 정령력 포인트가 쌓이지.<end>"

## 1370536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊力ポイントをためていくと、<line-break>精霊力レベルが上がる。<line-break>すると、使える魔法が増えていくんだ。<end>"
EN: "As you accumulate elemental power points, your elemental power level rises. That gives you access to more spells.<end>"
KO: "정령력 포인트를 모으면 정령력 레벨이 올라가. 그러면 사용할 수 있는 마법이 늘어나지.<end>"

## 1370537
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風属性の魔法で話をするぜ？<line-break>最初はゲイルしか使えない。<line-break>だが、精霊力レベルをあげるとだな…<line-break>風の精霊力レベル３でサイレント、<line-break>風の精霊力レベル４ではエスケープを<line-break>使えるようになる。<end>"
EN: "Let's use Wind magic as an example. At first, you can only use Gale. Raise your elemental power level, though, and you'll unlock Silent at Wind elemental power level 3, then Escape at level 4.<end>"
KO: "바람 속성 마법으로 설명해 줄게. 처음에는 게일밖에 못 써. 하지만 정령력 레벨을 올리면… 바람 정령력 레벨 3에서 사일런트, 레벨 4에서는 이스케이프를 쓸 수 있게 된다.<end>"

## 1370538
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伸ばしたい属性の敵と<line-break>集中的に戦うのも<line-break>いい方法かもしれないな。<end>"
EN: "Focusing on enemies of the element you want to strengthen might be a good approach.<end>"
KO: "키우고 싶은 속성의 적과 집중적으로 싸우는 것도 좋은 방법일지 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1370539
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break>もう、いいんだな？<end>"
EN: "I see. You're sure you're all set?<end>"
KO: "그런가. 이제 괜찮은 거지?<end>"

## 1370540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自信がないのでもういちど最初から聞きたい<end>"
EN: "I'm not sure I've got it, so I'd like to hear it again from the beginning.<end>"
KO: "자신이 없으니 처음부터 다시 듣고 싶다<end>"

## 1370541
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫<end>"
EN: "I'm good.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아<end>"

## 1370542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。とにかく場数を踏むこった。<line-break>実戦に勝る訓練はない。<line-break>忘れんなよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. The main thing is to get plenty of experience. There's no better training than real combat. Don't forget that.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 어쨌든 경험을 많이 쌓아라. 실전보다 좋은 훈련은 없어. 잊지 마.<end>"

## 1370543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、行ってきな。<line-break>応援してるぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Off you go, then. I'm rooting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 다녀와. 응원할게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1370544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫<end>"
EN: "Okay.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아<end>"

## 1370545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただいま帰りました。<end>"
EN: "I'm back.<end>"
KO: "다녀왔습니다.<end>"

## 1370546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ギルドのご主人さん。<line-break>いらっしゃいませ、こんにちは。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Guildmaster! Welcome. Good afternoon.<end>"
KO: "아, 길드 주인님. 어서 오세요, 안녕하세요.<end>"

## 1370547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんちは、フェルムちゃん。<line-break>今日もよく働いてて偉いな。<end>"
EN: "Afternoon, Ferme. Hard at work again today, I see. Good for you.<end>"
KO: "안녕, 페름. 오늘도 열심히 일하네. 기특해.<end>"

## 1370548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、お前さんは、<line-break><value:$28>じゃないか。<end>"
EN: "And if it isn't <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "게다가 너, <value:$28>잖아.<end>"

## 1370549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシさんから聞いたぜ、<line-break>ゴブリン退治に協力するらしいな。<line-break> <line-break>お前もようやく冒険者として <line-break>出発ってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Ossi told me you're helping with the goblin hunt. So you're finally setting out as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "오시 씨에게 들었어. 고블린 퇴치에 협력한다며. 너도 드디어 모험자로 출발하는 거군.<end>"

## 1370550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…となると、当然ギルドも<line-break>無関係じゃなくなってくるって<line-break>ことだな。<end>"
EN: "In that case, the Guild naturally has a part to play as well.<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 당연히 길드도 무관하지 않게 된다는 거군.<end>"

## 1370551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい、冒険者ギルドについて、<line-break>何か聞いておきたいことはあるかい？<end>"
EN: "Anything you'd like to know about the Adventurers' Guild?<end>"
KO: "어때, 모험자 길드에 대해 뭔가 물어보고 싶은 게 있나?<end>"

## 1370552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、やめてください！<end>"
EN: "P-please stop!<end>"
KO: "그, 그만해 주세요!<end>"

## 1370554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困ります、お客さん。<line-break>い、いや…！　触らないで。<end>"
EN: "Please, don't do this. N-no! Don't touch me!<end>"
KO: "곤란합니다, 손님. 아, 아니…! 만지지 마세요.<end>"

## 1370555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様とふたりで愛の冒険に旅立つ。<line-break>それが君の運命なんだ！<end>"
EN: "You and I shall embark together on an adventure of love. It is your destiny!<end>"
KO: "이 몸과 둘이 사랑의 모험을 떠난다. 그게 네 운명이야!<end>"

## 1370556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん！<line-break>助けてください！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Please help me!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님! 도와주세요!<end>"

## 1370557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このお客さん、酔っぱらってて…。<end>"
EN: "This customer is drunk, and...<end>"
KO: "이 손님, 취해 있어서….<end>"

## 1370558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様ぁ！<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break>俺様の愛の伝説のわき役にしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you?! Perfect timing. I'll make you a supporting player in my legend of love!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 네놈! 마침 잘됐다. 나의 사랑의 전설에 조연으로 만들어 주마!<end>"

## 1370559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こんなはずは…。<line-break>ええい、まだだ！<line-break>俺様の無敵伝説はこれから始まるんだ！<end>"
EN: "Th-this wasn't supposed to happen... Bah, I'm not finished yet! The legend of my invincibility has only just begun!<end>"
KO: "이, 이럴 리가…. 젠장, 아직이다! 이 몸의 무적 전설은 이제부터 시작이야!<end>"

## 1370560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございますっ！<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>ケガはありませんか？<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much! <value:$28>, are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "감사합니다! <value:$28> 씨, 다친 데는 없으세요?<end>"

## 1370561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすが道場で鍛えているだけあって、<line-break><value:$28>さんは強いですね。<end>"
EN: "All that training at the dojo has certainly paid off. You're very strong, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "역시 도장에서 단련한 만큼 <value:$28>씨는 강하군요.<end>"

## 1370562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見ていてくれたか？<line-break>これが俺様の愛の…<line-break>あふれんばかりにほとばしる愛の力！！<end>"
EN: "Did you see that? This is the power of my love... the overflowing, unstoppable power of my love!!<end>"
KO: "보고 있었나? 이것이 바로 나님의 사랑의… 넘쳐흐를 듯 솟구치는 사랑의 힘!!<end>"

## 1370563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恐れずに飛び込んでくるがいい。<line-break>俺様の愛の翼は、<line-break>いつでも君を包み込むぜ！<end>"
EN: "Come to me without fear. The wings of my love will always enfold you!<end>"
KO: "두려워 말고 뛰어들어 와. 이 몸의 사랑의 날개는 언제든 너를 감싸 줄 테니까!<end>"

## 1370564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫ですかっ？<line-break><value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "A-are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "괘, 괜찮으세요? <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1370565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい…私なんかのために。<line-break>…ケガまでさせてしまって…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... You even got hurt for someone like me.<end>"
KO: "미안해요… 저 같은 사람 때문에. …다치기까지 하게 해서…<end>"

## 1370566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい？<line-break>あ、オッシさんのお酒のお代ですね。<line-break>毎度、ありがとうございましたー。<end>"
EN: "Yes? Oh, you're paying for Ossi's drinks. Thank you, and come again!<end>"
KO: "네? 아, 오시 씨 술값이군요. 매번 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1370567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "앗, <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 1370568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>先ほどはありがとうございました。<line-break>…あの、このあと…お暇ですか？<end>"
EN: "Um... Thank you for what you did earlier. I was wondering... are you free after this?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 아까는 감사합니다. …저, 이 뒤에… 시간 있으세요?<end>"

## 1370569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ごめんなさい。そうですよね。<line-break>…アイリーンさんがいますもんね。<end>"
EN: "S-sorry. Of course. There's Irene, after all.<end>"
KO: "죄, 죄송해요. 그렇죠. …아이린 씨가 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1370570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>私は仕事に戻ります。<line-break>たまには酒場に遊びにきてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, I'd better get back to work. Do come by the tavern once in a while.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>님. 저는 일하러 돌아갈게요. 가끔 술집에도 놀러 오세요.<end>"

## 1370571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者ギルドとは？<end>"

## 1370572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "No, I understand it well enough.<end>"

## 1370575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者として登録してくれれば<line-break>お前も明日から自由な冒険者さ。<line-break>胸躍らせる冒険がお前を待ってるぞ。<line-break>そうしたら、仕事も面倒見させてもらうぜ。<line-break>だが、まずは引き受けてる用事を<line-break>片付けちまいな。<end>"
EN: "Register as an adventurer, and starting tomorrow you'll be free to pursue whatever adventures you like. Thrilling adventures will be waiting for you, and I'll help you find work. But first, finish the business you've already taken on.<end>"
KO: "모험자로 등록하면 너도 내일부터 자유로운 모험자다. 가슴 뛰는 모험이 널 기다리고 있어. 그러면 일도 내가 챙겨 주지. 하지만 우선 지금 맡은 용무부터 끝내고 와.<end>"

## 1370576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、ピンからキリまで<line-break>いろいろある。<line-break><line-break>届け物みたいな簡単なものから、<line-break>恐ろしいバケモンを退治してくれって<line-break>いうような、命がけのものまでな。<end>"
EN: "There's all sorts, from one end of the scale to the other. Some are as simple as making a delivery. Others have you risking your life to slay some fearsome monster.<end>"
KO: "그래, 별의별 게 다 있어. 배달 같은 간단한 일부터 무시무시한 괴물을 퇴치해 달라는 목숨 건 일까지 말이지.<end>"

## 1370577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一応、ギルドのほうでも、<line-break>冒険者に適当な仕事かどうか<line-break>アドバイスをしてやってる。<line-break>依頼を引き受けるようになったら、<line-break>あんまり無茶はするんじゃねえぜ。<end>"
EN: "The Guild will advise you on whether a job is suitable for you. Once you start taking requests, don't go doing anything too reckless.<end>"
KO: "길드에서도 모험자에게 맞는 일인지 조언해 주고 있어. 의뢰를 받기 시작해도 너무 무리하지는 마.<end>"

## 1370578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、冒険者になる気に<line-break>なったのかい。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Decided to become an adventurer, have you?<end>"
KO: "오, 모험자가 될 마음이 생긴 거야?<end>"

## 1370579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな、自分が確実にこなせる<line-break>仕事を引き受けることと、あとは、<line-break>日数をちゃんと考えることかな。<line-break>それさえうまくできれば、<line-break>冒険者として通用するだろう。<end>"
EN: "Let's see... Only take jobs you know you can finish, and pay close attention to how many days you have. If you can manage that, you should do well enough as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군, 자기가 확실히 해낼 수 있는 일을 맡는 것과 날짜를 제대로 생각하는 거겠지. 그것만 잘하면 모험자로 통할 거야.<end>"

## 1370580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もういいのかい？<end>"
EN: "Is that all?<end>"
KO: "이제 됐어?<end>"

## 1370581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう一度説明が聞きたい<end>"
EN: "I'd like to hear the explanation again.<end>"
KO: "한 번 더 설명을 듣고 싶다<end>"

## 1370582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫<end>"
EN: "I'm all set.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아<end>"

## 1370583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめることなどできん！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>未来の勇者の俺様の熱くたかぶる愛は！<line-break>誰にも…、そう、<line-break>この俺様にすら止めることはできん！<end>"

## 1370593
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様ぁ！<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break>俺様の愛の伝説のわき役にしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you?! Perfect timing. I'll make you a supporting player in my legend of love!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 네놈! 마침 잘됐다. 나의 사랑의 전설에 조연으로 만들어 주마!<end>"

## 1370594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何と…何と美しい！<line-break>その瞳の輝き、頬のなめらかさ…<line-break>まさに未来の勇者の妻にふさわしい！<line-break>いや、そのためだけに生まれてきた<line-break>逸材といって過言ではない！<end>"
EN: "H-how... how beautiful! The sparkle in your eyes, the smoothness of your cheeks... You are truly worthy to be the future Hero's wife! No, it would be no exaggeration to call you an exceptional woman born for that very purpose!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고… 이 얼마나 아름다운가! 그 눈동자의 빛, 매끈한 뺨… 그야말로 미래의 용사의 아내로 딱이군! 아니, 오직 그걸 위해 태어났다고 해도 과언이 아닌 인재다!<end>"

## 1370595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、探したっすぅ。<line-break>実は、たった今、聖杯を盗んだ<line-break>ゴブリンたちの居場所が判明したっす！<line-break>道場で待つっす！<line-break>すぐ来てほしいっす！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I've been looking for you! We just found out where the goblins who stole the Grail are hiding! I'll wait for you at the dojo, so come right away!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 찾았슴다. 사실 방금 성배를 훔친 고블린들의 위치를 알아냈슴다! 도장에서 기다리겠슴다! 바로 와 주셨으면 함다!<end>"

## 1370607

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370610

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370611
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370612
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…、いい名だ。<line-break>オッシのとこで教わってるのか。<line-break>ふうん…、あいつんとこで…<line-break>おっと悪い、俺はゼネテスだ。<end>"

## 1370613
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気楽な冒険者…<line-break>ま、あんたにとっちゃ、<line-break>反面教師ってとこかな。<end>"

## 1370614
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははははっ！　<line-break>言うねえ、ゼネさん。<line-break>なあ、<value:$28>とやら。<line-break>わからないことがあったら、<line-break>このゼネさんに何でも聞くといいぜ。<line-break>こう見えてもゼネさんは…<end>"
EN: "Hah hah hah! That's a good one, Xenetes. Listen, <value:$28>, was it? If there's anything you don't understand, just ask Xenetes here. He may not look it, but...<end>"

## 1370617
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml

## 1370620

## 1370621
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ、一口には言えないがな、<line-break>どういう人間になりたいか、<line-break>あんたの意思が何より大事だな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. There's no simple answer, but what matters most is what kind of person you want to become.<end>"
KO: "음, 한마디로 말할 순 없지만 어떤 사람이 되고 싶은지, 네 의지가 무엇보다 중요하겠지.<end>"

## 1370622
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣から魔法、ひととおり何でも<line-break>できるようになりたいのか？<line-break><line-break>逆に、ひとつの能力を<line-break>比類なきまでに高めたいのか？<end>"
EN: "Do you want to learn a bit of everything, from swordplay to magic? Or would you rather hone one ability until none can rival you?<end>"
KO: "검부터 마법까지 두루 능숙해지고 싶은가? 아니면 하나의 능력을 비할 데 없이 끌어올리고 싶은가?<end>"

## 1370623
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どちらにせよ、<line-break>それはお前さんの自由なのさ。<line-break><line-break>あんただったら、<line-break>どっちを目指すつもりだい？<end>"
EN: "Either way, the choice is yours. Which path would you take?<end>"
KO: "어느 쪽이든 그건 네 자유야. 너라면 어느 쪽을 목표로 하겠어?<end>"

## 1370624

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "弱点のない成長<end>"

KO: "약점 없는 성장<end>"

## 1370625
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一芸に秀でた成長<end>"
EN: "Specialized growth<end>"
KO: "한 분야에 뛰어난 성장<end>"

## 1370626
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどな。スキがなけりゃ、<line-break>敵にあわせて戦い方を変えることが<line-break>できるってわけだ。<line-break>時には戦士に、時には魔道士に…<line-break>確かに、パーティにとって、<line-break>便利な人材になるだろう。<end>"
EN: "I see. If you have no gaps in your skills, you can change how you fight to suit the enemy. A warrior one moment, a mage the next... You'd certainly be useful to any party.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 빈틈만 없다면 적에 맞춰 싸우는 방식을 바꿀 수 있다는 거네. 때로는 전사로, 때로는 마도사로… 확실히 파티에 유용한 인재가 되겠어.<end>"

## 1370627
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、<line-break>その何でもできるところが、<line-break>お前さんの弱点にもなるぜ。<line-break>万能でいようとするためには、<line-break>すべての能力を<line-break>平等に伸ばさにゃならない。<end>"
EN: "But being able to do everything can also be your weakness. If you want to remain well-rounded, you'll have to develop every ability equally.<end>"
KO: "하지만 뭐든 할 수 있다는 점이 네 약점이 되기도 해. 만능이 되려면 모든 능력을 고르게 키워야 하거든.<end>"

## 1370628
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけ人より多くの努力を<line-break>しなきゃならないってことだからな。<line-break>　<line-break>…ま、何を強いとするのか、<line-break>それを決めるのもお前さん自身だ。<end>"
EN: "That means putting in more effort than other people. Still, you're the one who decides what it means to be strong.<end>"
KO: "그만큼 남보다 더 많이 노력해야 한다는 뜻이니까. …뭐, 무엇을 강함이라 여길지는 네가 정할 일이야.<end>"

## 1370629
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんの主は、他でもない<line-break>お前さん自身だということ…<line-break>それが一番大事なんじゃないかね。<end>"
EN: "You answer to no one but yourself. Perhaps that's the most important thing of all.<end>"
KO: "네 주인은 다름 아닌 너 자신이라는 것…. 그게 가장 중요한 것 아닐까.<end>"

## 1370630
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かたよってもいいから、<line-break>その道では一番になって<line-break>やろうってんだな。<line-break>巨人も驚く堅牢な戦士か、<line-break>悪魔も道をあける大魔法使いか…。<end>"
EN: "So you'd rather specialize and aim to be the best on your chosen path. A stalwart warrior who could astonish even a giant, or a great mage before whom even demons give way...<end>"
KO: "치우쳐도 좋으니 그 길에서 최고가 되겠다는 거군. 거인도 놀랄 견고한 전사인가, 악마도 길을 비킬 대마법사인가….<end>"

## 1370631
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そうなるには多くの誘惑に<line-break>勝てなくちゃな。<line-break>　<line-break>進むべき道に迷ったり、<line-break>引き返してる暇はねえぜ。<end>"
EN: "But to reach that height, you'll have to resist plenty of temptations. There's no time to lose your way or turn back.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그러려면 많은 유혹을 이겨 내야 해. 갈 길을 헤매거나 되돌아볼 시간은 없어.<end>"

## 1370632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ま、何を強いとするのか、<line-break>それを決めるのもお前さん自身だ。<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんの主は、他でもない<line-break>お前さん自身だということ…<line-break>それが一番大事なんじゃないかね。<end>"
EN: "Still, you're the one who decides what it means to be strong. You answer to no one but yourself. Perhaps that's the most important thing of all.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 무엇을 강함이라 할지 정하는 것도 결국 자네 자신이야. 자네의 주인은 다름 아닌 자네 자신이라는 것…. 그게 가장 중요하지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1370633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うまい戦い方？　簡単なこった。<line-break>敵に弱点をつかれないようにして、<line-break>逆に敵の弱点をつけばいい。<line-break>必ず敵には弱点がある。<line-break>武器での攻撃か、魔法か？<line-break>火か水か、土か風か？<end>"
EN: "How do you fight well? Simple. Don't let the enemy exploit your weaknesses; exploit the enemy's instead. Every enemy has one. Is it vulnerable to weapons or magic? Fire or water? Earth or wind?<end>"
KO: "잘 싸우는 법? 간단하지. 적에게 약점을 찔리지 않게 하고 반대로 적의 약점을 찌르면 돼. 적에게는 반드시 약점이 있다. 무기 공격인가, 마법인가? 불인가 물인가, 땅인가 바람인가?<end>"

## 1370634
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを見極めるのと同時に、<line-break>自分が何をすべきかを<line-break>考える必要があるだろうな。<line-break>ひ弱な魔道士に前に出られたって<line-break>盾にもならねえし、逆に、戦士が<line-break>引っ込むのもナンセンスだ。<end>"
EN: "While you're working that out, you also need to think about what your own role should be. A frail mage won't make much of a shield on the front line, and a warrior hanging back makes no sense either.<end>"
KO: "그걸 파악하는 동시에 자신이 뭘 해야 할지도 생각해야겠지. 허약한 마도사가 앞으로 나와 봐야 방패도 못 되고, 반대로 전사가 뒤로 빠지는 것도 말이 안 돼.<end>"

## 1370635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間と協力して、<line-break>最大限に自分の能力をいかす。<line-break>これだな。<line-break>仲間？　ははは、ま、<line-break>そのうちできるだろうよ。<end>"
EN: "Work together with your companions and make the most of your own abilities. That's the key. Companions? Ha ha ha. Well, I'm sure you'll find some eventually.<end>"
KO: "동료와 협력해서 자기 능력을 최대한 살린다. 이거지. 동료? 하하하, 뭐, 그중에 생기겠지.<end>"

## 1370636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、ま<line-break>これで質問は終わりってことで<line-break>いいかい、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Well then, are we done with the questions, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 뭐, 이걸로 질문은 끝인 걸로 해도 되겠지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1370637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自信がないのでもういちど最初から聞きたい<end>"
EN: "I'm not sure, so I'd like to hear it again from the beginning.<end>"
KO: "자신이 없으니 처음부터 다시 듣고 싶다<end>"

## 1370638
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I'm all set.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아<end>"

## 1370639
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっはっは、こりゃ頼もしいな。<end>"
EN: "Hah hah hah! That's reassuring.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 이거 든든하군.<end>"

## 1370640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、<line-break>鍛冶屋やギルドをのぞいてみるのも<line-break>ついでにお勧めしておくぜ。<line-break>どこでも歓迎してくれるだろうし、<line-break>お前さんも得るものが多いだろう。<line-break>ま、そんなとこだ。<end>"
EN: "One more thing: I'd recommend stopping by the smithy and the Guild. You should be welcome at either, and you'll learn plenty. Well, that's about it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 대장간이나 길드도 한번 들러 보라고 권해 두지. 어디든 환영해 줄 테고 너도 얻는 게 많을 거야. 뭐, 그런 정도다.<end>"

## 1370641

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370642
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、がんばってな。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>男くせえこことは違って、<line-break>みんな、フェルムちゃんの笑顔を見に、<line-break>向こうの酒場に通ってんだからさ。<end>"

KO: "그래, 힘내라. 남자 냄새 나는 여기와 달리 다들 페름의 웃는 얼굴을 보러 저쪽 술집에 다니니까.<end>"

EN: ""

## 1370644
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前も冒険者の卵なら、<line-break>フェルムをしっかり守ってやるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "If you're an adventurer in the making, make sure you keep Ferme safe.<end>"
KO: "너도 모험자 새내기라면 페름을 잘 지켜 줘야 한다.<end>"

## 1370645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近は、物騒だからな。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>俺が見たわけじゃねえが、<line-break>やたら全開な変質者が<line-break>横行してるって言うしよ。<end>"
EN: "It's dangerous out there these days. I haven't seen anything myself, but they say some exhibitionist has been roaming around with everything on display.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 흉흉하잖아. 내가 직접 본 건 아니지만, 너무 훤히 드러내고 다니는 변태가 돌아다닌다더군.<end>"

## 1370646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<line-break>戻りましょう、<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we head back, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아가죠, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1370647
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1370648
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（早く師匠のところに<line-break>　向かわなければ…。）<end>"
EN: "(I have to hurry to my master's...)<end>"
KO: "(빨리 스승님께 가야 해….)<end>"

## 1370649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<line-break><value:$28>さん…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨….<end>"

## 1370650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ、<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>いたじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right! You're here, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "맞아, <value:$28> 씨가 있었잖아.<end>"

## 1370651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、実は私、これから<line-break>酒場の用事でお使いに<line-break>行かなければならないんです。<line-break>この先のスラムにある<line-break>酒場なのですが…。<end>"
EN: "Well, I actually have to run an errand for the tavern. I need to go to a tavern in the Slums up ahead...<end>"
KO: "저기, 사실 저는 지금부터 술집 심부름을 가야 합니다. 이 앞 빈민가에 있는 술집인데요….<end>"

## 1370652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、そうですね。<line-break>お酒を飲まれない<line-break><value:$28>さんには<line-break>あんまり馴染みが<line-break>ないかもしれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, that's true. Since you don't drink, <value:$28>, you may not know the place.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그렇네요. 술을 마시지 않는 <value:$28> 씨에겐 그다지 익숙하지 않을지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1370653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スラムには、私が働いている<line-break>この酒場とは別にもう一軒、<line-break>酒場があるんです。<line-break>今日はそこへ届けものを<line-break>しなくちゃならないんですが…<line-break>スラムだけあって、ちょっと怖くて。<end>"
EN: "There's another tavern in the Slums besides the one where I work. I have to deliver something there today, but... well, the Slums frighten me a little.<end>"
KO: "슬럼에는 제가 일하는 이 술집 말고도 한 군데 술집이 더 있어요. 오늘은 거기에 물건을 배달해야 하는데… 슬럼이라 조금 무서워서요.<end>"

## 1370654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、このロストールでも、<line-break>女の人を狙って付きまとってくる<line-break>変質者が出るらしいんです。<line-break><value:$28>さんは、<line-break>冒険者志望でしたよね？<end>"
EN: "They say there's been a pervert stalking women here in Rostorl lately. You were hoping to become an adventurer, weren't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "최근 로스톨에서도 여자를 노리고 따라다니는 변태가 나타난대요. <value:$28> 씨는 모험자 지망이었죠?<end>"

## 1370655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、<line-break>スラムの酒場まで、<line-break>一緒に行ってください。<end>"
EN: "Please, come with me as far as the tavern in the Slums.<end>"
KO: "부탁입니다, 슬럼가의 술집까지 함께 가주세요.<end>"

## 1370656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった！<line-break>じゃ、よろしくお願いします！<end>"
EN: "What a relief! Thank you―I'm counting on you!<end>"
KO: "다행이다! 그럼 잘 부탁드립니다!<end>"

## 1370657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうだったんですか。<line-break>オッシ先生と鍛冶屋さんで<line-break>酒代をワリカンに…。<line-break>ふふふ、確かに鍛冶屋さんも、<line-break>オッシ先生に負けないくらい<line-break>よくお飲みになりますからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so Master Ossi and the blacksmith are splitting the drinks bill... Hee hee. The blacksmith certainly drinks every bit as much as Master Ossi.<end>"
KO: "아, 그랬군요. 오시 선생님과 대장장이가 술값을 반씩…. 후후, 확실히 대장장이도 오시 선생님 못지않게 술을 잘 드시니까요.<end>"

## 1370658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃまず、<line-break>鍛冶屋さんに寄りましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>ご存知かもしれませんが、<line-break>この街の鍛冶屋さんは、<line-break>道具屋さんの地下にありますよ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's stop by the blacksmith's first. You may already know this, but the town smithy is beneath the item shop.<end>"
KO: "그럼 먼저 대장간에 들르죠. 아실지도 모르지만 이 도시 대장간은 도구점 지하에 있습니다.<end>"

## 1370659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやじ、剣が古くなってきたから　　<line-break>新しいのを買いたいんだ。<line-break>いい剣はないか？<end>"
EN: "Old man, my sword's getting worn out, so I want to buy a new one. Got any good swords?<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 검이 낡아 와서 새 걸 사고 싶어. 좋은 검 없어?<end>"

## 1370660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、新米冒険者だね？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're a novice adventurer, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "당신, 신참 모험자지?<end>"

## 1370661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？<line-break>なんで新米だってわかったんだ？　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? How did you know I was a novice?<end>"
KO: "어? 내가 신참인 걸 어떻게 알았지?<end>"

## 1370662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器はな、新しく買うよりも、<line-break>強化していった方が強くなるのさ。　<line-break>だから、そう思ったわけだ。<end>"
EN: "A weapon will grow stronger if you keep improving it instead of replacing it. That's what gave you away.<end>"
KO: "무기는 새로 사는 것보다 강화해 나가는 편이 더 강해져. 그래서 그렇게 생각한 거야.<end>"

## 1370663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なるほど。<line-break>それなら、強化してくれないか。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "R-right, I see. In that case, could you improve mine?<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇군. 그렇다면 강화해 주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1370664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器の強化には、錬剛石が必要だが、<line-break>持ってるかい？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You'll need Tempering Stone to improve a weapon. Do you have any?<end>"
KO: "무기 강화에는 연강석이 필요한데, 가지고 있나?<end>"

## 1370665
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "錬剛石？　いや、持ってないな。　　<line-break>その錬剛石って、どうすれば<line-break>手に入るんだ？<end>"
EN: "Tempering Stone? No, I don't have any. How do I get it?<end>"
KO: "연강석? 아니, 없는데. 그 연강석은 어떻게 하면 구할 수 있어?<end>"

## 1370667
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、錬剛石がとれる山を　　　　<line-break>見つけたら、何度か足を<line-break>運んでみるのがいいだろうな。<end>"
EN: "So if you find a mountain where Tempering Stone can be gathered, you'd do well to go back a few times.<end>"
KO: "그러니 연강석이 나는 산을 찾으면 몇 번쯤 찾아가 보는 게 좋겠지.<end>"

## 1370668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<line-break>冒険者は何ごとも冒険から　　　　　<line-break>ってことだな。<line-break>それじゃあ、おやじ。<line-break>錬剛石を手に入れたら、また来るよ。<end>"
EN: "I see. For an adventurer, everything starts with an adventure. All right, old man. I'll come back once I've found some Tempering Stone.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 모험자는 뭐든 모험에서 시작한다는 거네. 그럼 아저씨, 연강석을 손에 넣으면 다시 올게.<end>"

## 1370669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！<line-break>ギアもいるから、貯めとけよ。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Right! You'll need Gea too, so save some up.<end>"
KO: "좋아! 기어도 필요하니까 모아 둬.<end>"

## 1370670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰だって最初は新米だからな。<line-break>知ってる奴が、教えてやらんとな。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、高いとこから低いとこへ水が<line-break>流れてくのと同じ道理だわな。<line-break>はっはっは。<end>"
EN: "Everyone starts out a novice. Those who know ought to teach those who don't. It's only natural―like water flowing downhill. Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "누구나 처음에는 신참이니까. 아는 사람이 가르쳐 줘야지. 높은 곳에서 낮은 곳으로 물이 흐르는 것과 같은 이치야. 하하하.<end>"

## 1370671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。<end>"
EN: "Hello.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요.<end>"

## 1370672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっ、フェルムちゃんじゃないか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それに、お前さんは<line-break>オッシんとこの若いのだったな。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't little Ferme. And you're Ossi's young apprentice, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "오, 페름 아니냐. 그리고 넌 오시 밑의 젊은이였지.<end>"

## 1370673
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…그래서 무슨 일이야? 대장장이 일인가?<end>"

## 1370674
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、お前さんも、<line-break>鍛冶屋の仕事について<line-break>聞きにきたのかい？<end>"
EN: "So you've come to learn about a blacksmith's work too?<end>"
KO: "그럼 자네도 대장장이 일에 대해 물으러 온 건가?<end>"

## 1370675
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がはは、遠慮すんなって！<line-break>俺がその気じゃない時に来られても、<line-break>教えてやらねえぜ。<line-break>それに、覚えといてくれた方が、<line-break>仕事を頼まれる側としても<line-break>話が早いってもんだ。<end>"
EN: "Gahaha! Don't be shy! If you came when I wasn't in the mood, I wouldn't teach you a thing. Besides, if you know how it works, it'll make things easier for me when you ask me to do a job.<end>"
KO: "가하하, 사양하지 마! 내가 내킬 때 찾아오지 않으면 가르쳐 주지 않을 거라고. 게다가 기억해 두는 편이 일을 맡기는 쪽에서도 얘기가 빠르지.<end>"

## 1370676
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　留守中。すぐに戻ります。　　　<end>"

KO: "부재중. 곧 돌아옵니다.<end>"

## 1370677
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
