## 1370678
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370679
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370680
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 왜 여관에? 아직 용무가 끝나지 않았어요.<end>"

## 1370681
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370682
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370683
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370684
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "疲れたときはいつでも来るといい。<line-break>王宮気分…とはいかないが、<line-break> <line-break>うまい家庭料理と<line-break>ふかふかのベッドで歓迎するよ。<end>"

## 1370685
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとう。<end>"

## 1370687

## 1370688

## 1370692
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……いえ、なんでもないです。<line-break>それじゃ、中にはいりましょうか。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "...No, it's nothing. Shall we go inside, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "……아니, 아무것도 아니에요. 그럼 안으로 들어갈까요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1370693
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは私が働いている酒場です。<line-break>スラムの酒場で事を済ませないと、<line-break>戻れません。<line-break>行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "This is the tavern where I work. We can't come back until we've finished our business at the tavern in the Slums. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "여기는 제가 일하는 술집입니다. 슬럼가의 술집에서 일을 마치지 않으면 돌아갈 수 없습니다. 갑시다.<end>"

## 1370694
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の杖の修理代、<line-break>安くしてくれるなら、<line-break>考えてもいいですよ。<end>"
EN: "I might consider it if you'll give me a discount on repairing my staff.<end>"
KO: "제 지팡이 수리비를 싸게 해 주신다면 생각해 볼게요.<end>"

## 1370695
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うおっ、神様のしもべが俺の足元を！<line-break><end>天空神様へのお布施ってやつですかい？<line-break><end>"
EN: "Whoa! A servant of God at my feet!<end>Is this what you call an offering to the Sky God?<end>"
KO: "우왓, 신의 종이 내 발밑에!<end>천공신님께 바치는 헌금 같은 겁니까?<end>"

## 1370696
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、冗談ですよ、冗談。<line-break>で、精霊レベルのことですね？<line-break>　<line-break>装備品の精霊レベルを変更する仕事は、<line-break>本来、鍛冶屋ではなく、<line-break>神と人とをつなぐ神殿の領分なのです。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm only joking. Now, you were asking about Spirit Levels, yes? Changing the Spirit Level of equipment is properly the province of the temple, which serves as a bridge between gods and mortals, rather than the blacksmith.<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담이에요. 농담. 그래서 정령 레벨 이야기죠? 장비의 정령 레벨을 바꾸는 일은 원래 대장장이가 아니라 신과 인간을 잇는 신전의 영역입니다.<end>"

## 1370697
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールには神殿がないからな。<line-break>ちなみにこの方はたまたま用事があって<line-break>エンシャントからいらっしゃってんだ。<end>"
EN: "There isn't a temple in Rostorl. This priest just happens to be visiting from Ancient on other business.<end>"
KO: "로스톨엔 신전이 없으니까. 참고로 이분은 마침 볼일이 있어서 엔샨트에서 오신 거야.<end>"

## 1370698
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊レベルとは、すなわち、<line-break>火や水といった精霊の力を<line-break>武器や防具に宿すことを意味します。<line-break>　<line-break>精霊の属性については<line-break>もうご存知ですか？<end>"
EN: "Spirit Level means imbuing weapons and armor with the power of spirits such as fire and water. Are you already familiar with Spirit Attributes?<end>"
KO: "정령 레벨이란 곧 불이나 물 같은 정령의 힘을 무기나 방어구에 깃들이는 것을 뜻합니다. 정령의 속성은 이미 알고 계십니까?<end>"

## 1370699
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちにとっても、<line-break>いいかせぎになるしな。<end>"
EN: "And it's good business for us, too.<end>"
KO: "우리한테도 좋은 벌이가 되니까.<end>"

## 1370700
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははは！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "하하하!<end>"

## 1370701
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊レベルのことか。<line-break>確かにそれも装備品まわりの<line-break>ことではあるが、ちょいと…<line-break>それは鍛冶屋じゃなくて、<line-break>教会の仕事なんでな。<end>"
EN: "Spirit Levels, eh? True, that does have to do with equipment, but... it's church work, not a blacksmith's.<end>"
KO: "정령 레벨 말인가. 확실히 장비와 관련된 일이긴 하지만 그건 대장간이 아니라 교회 쪽 일이야.<end>"

## 1370702
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火、水、土、風…<line-break>この四種類が精霊の属性です。<line-break>　<line-break>聖母神がお作りになったこの四属性が<line-break>互いに干渉しあい、循環することで<line-break>世界は成り立っています。<end>"
EN: "Fire, water, earth, and wind: these are the four Spirit Attributes. The world exists through the interplay and circulation of these four attributes, created by the Mother Goddess.<end>"
KO: "불, 물, 땅, 바람…. 이 네 종류가 정령의 속성입니다. 성모신께서 만드신 이 네 속성이 서로 간섭하고 순환함으로써 세계는 이루어져 있습니다.<end>"

## 1370703
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火は水に強く、水は土に強く、<line-break>土は風に、風は火に強い。<line-break>　<line-break>おわかりでしょう？<line-break>どれが圧倒的に優位ということはなく、<line-break>うまくバランスをとっているのです。<end>"
EN: "Fire is strong against water, water against earth, earth against wind, and wind against fire. You see? None holds an overwhelming advantage; together, they maintain a careful balance.<end>"
KO: "불은 물에 강하고, 물은 흙에 강하며, 흙은 바람에, 바람은 불에 강합니다. 아시겠죠? 어느 하나가 압도적으로 우세한 게 아니라 절묘하게 균형을 이루고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1370704
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "装備の精霊レベルをあげるということは<line-break>そのまま、この四属性の性質を<line-break>盛り込むことになるのです。<line-break>例えば、あなたが火属性の剣を持ち、<line-break>火属性の鎧を身につけたとしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Raising a piece of equipment's Spirit Level imbues it with the nature of one of those four attributes. Suppose, for example, that you wield a Fire-element sword and wear Fire-element armor.<end>"
KO: "장비의 정령 레벨을 높인다는 건 곧 이 네 속성의 성질을 더 강하게 담는다는 뜻입니다. 예를 들어 당신이 불 속성 검을 들고 불 속성 갑옷을 입었다고 해 보죠.<end>"

## 1370706
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらに、この精霊の力は、<line-break>装備品ごとに上限が定まってはいますが、<line-break>レベルとして向上させることができます。<line-break>同じ火の属性の剣でも、<line-break>１レベルと３レベルでは<line-break>大きくその威力が変わってくる。<end>"
EN: "This spiritual power can also be raised in levels, though every piece of equipment has its own limit. Even between two Fire-element swords, the difference in power between level one and level three is considerable.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이 정령의 힘은 장비마다 상한이 정해져 있기는 하지만, 레벨로 향상시킬 수 있습니다. 같은 불 속성의 검이라도 1레벨과 3레벨은 위력이 크게 달라집니다.<end>"

## 1370707
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、同時に、忘れないでください。<line-break>精霊レベルが高いということは、<line-break>それだけ純粋に近いという証。<line-break>火属性の剣の攻撃では、<line-break>同じ火属性の敵を<line-break>傷つけることは難しく…<end>"
EN: "But there is something else you must remember. A higher Spirit Level is a sign of greater elemental purity. A Fire-element sword will have difficulty harming a Fire-element enemy...<end>"
KO: "하지만 동시에 잊지 마세요. 정령 레벨이 높다는 건 그만큼 순수한 속성에 가깝다는 증거입니다. 불 속성 검의 공격으로 같은 불 속성 적을 상처 입히기는 어려워지고…<end>"

## 1370708
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火属性の鎧は、風属性の攻撃を、<line-break>時に倍にもして受けてしまうことが<line-break>あるということを…。<end>"
EN: "...and Fire-element armor may sometimes cause you to take as much as twice the damage from a Wind-element attack.<end>"
KO: "불 속성 갑옷은 바람 속성 공격을 때로는 두 배로 받아 버릴 수도 있다는 걸….<end>"

## 1370709
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふうむ、さすが神官様は説明がうまい。<line-break>口下手な俺なんかが<line-break>でしゃばらなくて良かったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. A priest certainly knows how to explain things. Good thing a poor talker like me didn't butt in.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 역시 신관님은 설명을 잘하는군. 말주변 없는 내가 괜히 나서지 않길 잘했어.<end>"

## 1370710
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "装備に精霊の力を宿したくなったら、<line-break>神殿へいらっしゃい。<line-break>お待ちしてますよ。<end>"
EN: "If you ever wish to imbue your equipment with the power of spirits, come to the temple. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "장비에 정령의 힘을 깃들게 하고 싶다면 신전으로 오세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 1370711
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほっほう、そいつはたいしたもんだ。<line-break>改めて俺に仕事を頼むときも、<line-break>迷わずに済むな。<line-break>じゃ、説明はこの辺で<line-break>終わりにしちまって大丈夫かい？<end>"
EN: "Well, that's impressive. Now you won't get mixed up next time you come to me for work. So, can we wrap up the explanation here?<end>"
KO: "호오, 그건 대단하군. 다음에 내게 일을 부탁할 때도 망설일 필요 없겠어. 그럼 설명은 이쯤에서 끝내도 괜찮겠나?<end>"

## 1370712
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "네<end>"

## 1370713
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1370714
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　オッシの奴が？<line-break>酒場のツケを、<line-break>俺のと奴のとでワリカンに？<line-break>そうかー！　思い出したぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Ossi said that? Split the tavern tab between the two of us? Oh! Now I remember!<end>"
KO: "응? 오시 녀석이? 술집 외상을 나랑 자기 거랑 반씩 내기로 했다고? 아! 생각났다.<end>"

## 1370715
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前、あいつと飲んでて、<line-break>何か約束したような気になってたのは、<line-break>そのことだったか！<line-break>くっ、あいつのが飲んでるってのに<line-break>ワリカンとは…。オッシの野郎、<line-break>こっちがあぜんとするほど飲むんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "So that's what I had a vague feeling Ossi and I had agreed to when we were drinking the other day! Damn it, Ossi drank more than I did, and we're still splitting it down the middle? That bastard can drink enough to leave you speechless.<end>"
KO: "지난번에 그 녀석이랑 마시다가 뭔가 약속한 것 같았던 게 그거였구나! 젠장, 그 녀석이 더 마셨는데도 더치페이라니…. 오시 녀석, 이쪽이 질릴 정도로 마셔 댄다고.<end>"

## 1370716
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おまけにその酒癖ときちゃあ…。<line-break>ま、約束したなら仕方がねえな。<line-break>頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "And don't get me started on what Ossi's like when drunk... Well, a promise is a promise. Take care of it for me.<end>"
KO: "게다가 그 술버릇까지…. 뭐, 약속했다면 어쩔 수 없지. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1370717
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いな。<line-break>よし、じゃ、あんたには、<line-break>駄賃といったらなんだが…<end>"
EN: "Sorry about that. Here, let me give you something for your trouble...<end>"
KO: "미안하군. 좋아, 그럼 자네에겐 품삯이라 하기엔 뭐하지만….<end>"

## 1370718
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつを持ってきな。<end>"
EN: "Take this with you.<end>"
KO: "이걸 가져와.<end>"

## 1370719
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何って、錬剛石だよ、錬剛石！<line-break>そいつがありゃ、お前さんの装備も<line-break>少しはマシになるだろ。<line-break>頑張れよ、ヒヨッコ。<line-break>お前さんが装備を鍛えに来るのを<line-break>楽しみに待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? It's a Tempering Stone. A Tempering Stone! With it, you should be able to make your equipment a little better. Keep at it, rookie. I'll look forward to seeing you when you come by to get your equipment strengthened.<end>"
KO: "뭐긴, 연강석이지 연강석! 그게 있으면 네 장비도 조금은 나아질 거야. 힘내라, 풋내기. 네가 장비를 단련하러 오는 걸 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 1370720
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、あたしたち行きますね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>またお店で待ってますので<line-break>よろしくおねがいしますね。<end>"
EN: "Well, we'll be going now. We'll be waiting for you back at the shop, so please stop by.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저희는 가 볼게요. 또 가게에서 기다리고 있을 테니 잘 부탁드려요.<end>"

## 1370721
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>スラムの酒場まで<line-break>連れて行ってくださいね。<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please take us to the tavern in the Slums. We're counting on you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 슬럼가 술집까지 데려다 주세요. 부탁드릴게요.<end>"

## 1370722
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒代はその後で<line-break>マスターに渡してあげて<line-break>くださいね。<end>"
EN: "After that, please give the tavern keeper the money for the drinks.<end>"
KO: "술값은 그 뒤에 마스터에게 건네 주세요.<end>"

## 1370725
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>さっき宿屋でフェルムと<line-break>一緒だったの見たんだけど…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I saw you with Ferme at the inn earlier...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 아까 여관에서 페름과 함께 있던 걸 봤는데….<end>"

## 1370726
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか…。<line-break>宿屋で話を聞いてたのね。<line-break><line-break>別に気にしてたわけじゃないからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh... So you were listening at the inn. Not that I cared or anything.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나…. 여관에서 이야기를 듣고 있었네. 딱히 신경 쓴 건 아니니까.<end>"

## 1370728
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく先生の道場に<line-break>早く向かってあげて。<end>"
EN: "Just hurry over to Master Ossi's dojo.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 선생님 도장으로 빨리 가 줘.<end>"

## 1370729
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、アイリーンさん<line-break>こんにちは。<end>"
EN: "Oh, hello, Irene.<end>"
KO: "아, 아이린 씨. 안녕하세요.<end>"

## 1370730
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "二人でどうしたの？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>もしかしてデート？<end>"
EN: "What are you two up to? Don't tell me you're on a date?<end>"
KO: "둘이서 뭐 해? 혹시 데이트?<end>"

## 1370731
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談よ。<line-break>ムキになるなんて子供ねぇ。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. How childish, getting so worked up.<end>"
KO: "농담이야. 발끈하다니 어린애 같네.<end>"

## 1370732
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、私たちまだ用事が<line-break>ありますからこれで失礼しますね。<end>"
EN: "Um... we still have something to take care of, so if you'll excuse us.<end>"
KO: "저기… 저희 아직 볼일이 있어서 이만 실례할게요.<end>"

## 1370750
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こう言うと難しそうだが、<line-break>要はウォーロックを宿しさえすりゃ、<line-break><line-break>ロースペルが使えるってこった。<end>"
EN: "That may sound complicated, but the point is simple: host a Warlock, and you can use Low Spell.<end>"
KO: "어렵게 들리지만, 요컨대 워록을 깃들게 하면 로 스펠을 쓸 수 있다는 거야.<end>"

## 1370751
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "오시 끝<end>"

## 1380000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ、ボリューム５開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "오시 볼륨 5 시작<end>"

## 1380001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。　　　　　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>今晩はこの宿にお泊りなのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Are you staying at this inn tonight?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. 오늘 밤은 이 여관에 묵으시나요?<end>"

## 1380002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、私は、復興団の活動で<line-break>立ち寄ったのです。<line-break>少しお話ししていっても構いませんか？<line-break><value:$28>様には、<line-break>聞いていただきたいことが<line-break>たくさんあるのです…。<end>"
EN: "No, I'm only here on business for the Restoration League. Would you mind if we talked for a while? There is so much I want you to hear, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 저는 부흥단 활동 때문에 들렀습니다. 잠시 이야기하고 가도 될까요? <value:$28> 님께 들려드리고 싶은 이야기가 많아요….<end>"

## 1380003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break><value:$28>様にだから　　　　　　<line-break>お話しするのですが…<line-break>今回のクーデターは、実は私、<line-break>どこか現実味がないんです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I can tell you this because it's you, but somehow, this coup still doesn't feel real to me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. <value:$28>님이라서 말씀드리는 건데… 이번 쿠데타가 사실 저는 어딘가 현실감이 없어요.<end>"

## 1380004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には、主権を手にしようとされた　<line-break>レムオン様や、立場を危うくされた<line-break>貴族の方々よりも、<line-break>むしろクーデターで生活を狂わされた<line-break>市井の人々の気持ちの方が<line-break>よくわかる気がするのです…。<end>"
EN: "I feel I understand the ordinary people whose lives were thrown into turmoil by the coup better than I understand Lemghon, who sought to seize sovereignty, or the nobles whose positions were threatened...<end>"
KO: "저는 주권을 손에 넣으려 하신 레무온 님이나 처지가 위태로워진 귀족분들보다, 오히려 쿠데타로 삶이 엉망이 된 평범한 사람들의 마음을 더 잘 이해할 수 있을 것 같습니다….<end>"

## 1380005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "視力を失って、長年王宮というものと<line-break>縁遠くして生きてきた<line-break>せいかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps it is because I lost my sight and have lived for so many years with little connection to the royal palace.<end>"
KO: "시력을 잃고 오랫동안 왕궁과 거리를 두고 살아온 탓인지도 모르겠습니다.<end>"

## 1380006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>人はどうしてそこまで、権力などという<line-break>物騒なものを欲しがるのでしょうか？<line-break>みなが安らかに暮らせることだけを、<line-break>すなおに願えないのはなぜ…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, why do people crave something as dangerous as power so badly? Why can't people simply wish for everyone to live in peace?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 사람들은 어째서 그렇게까지 권력 같은 위험한 것을 원하는 걸까요? 모두가 편안히 살기만을 순수하게 바랄 수 없는 건 왜일까요…?<end>"

## 1380007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私は、そういう思いから、<line-break>復興団の創立を思い立ったのです。<line-break> <line-break>多くの方のお力を借りました。<line-break>今でも、復興団は彼らによって<line-break>支えられています。<end>"
EN: "It was those thoughts that led me to found the Restoration League. Many people lent me their aid, and the League still rests on their support today.<end>"
KO: "…저는 그런 생각에서 부흥단 창립을 결심했습니다. 많은 분의 힘을 빌렸습니다. 지금도 부흥단은 그분들이 지탱해 주고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして彼らは、余さず、<line-break>市井の生活を知っている人々でも<line-break>あります。<line-break>誰かが誰かを治めるのではなく、<line-break>すべての人々が、<line-break>おのおの自分の主であるような…<end>"
EN: "Every one of them also knows what life is like for ordinary people. I envision a world where no one rules over anyone else, and all people are their own masters...<end>"
KO: "그리고 그들은 빠짐없이 서민의 삶을 아는 사람들이기도 합니다. 누군가가 누군가를 다스리는 게 아니라 모든 사람이 각자 자신의 주인이 되는…<end>"

## 1380009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだうまくは言えないのですが、<line-break>そんな世の中を目指して、<line-break>復興団は活動しています。<end>"
EN: "I still cannot express it very well, but that is the kind of world the Restoration League is working toward.<end>"
KO: "아직 잘 설명할 수는 없지만, 그런 세상을 목표로 부흥단은 활동하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うれしく思います、<value:$28>様。<line-break>これからもアトレイアを暖かく<line-break>見守っていてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad, <value:$28>. Please continue to look kindly on Atleia.<end>"
KO: "기쁘게 생각합니다, <value:$28>님. 앞으로도 아트레이아를 따뜻하게 지켜봐 주세요.<end>"

## 1380011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>わざわざ呼び出して、すまぬ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Forgive me for summoning you here.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 일부러 불러내서 미안하군.<end>"

## 1380012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主にどうしても礼が言いたくてな。<end>"
EN: "There was something I simply had to thank you for.<end>"
KO: "그대에게 꼭 감사 인사를 하고 싶어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1380013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>我が娘、イークレムンの命を<line-break>救ってくれたこと、心より感謝いたす。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you saved the life of my daughter, Iiklmn. You have my heartfelt thanks.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 내 딸 이크렘의 목숨을 구해 준 것, 진심으로 감사한다.<end>"

## 1380014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやいや<line-break>わしひとりでは<line-break>娘を救えんかったじゃろうて。<end>"
EN: "No, no. I could never have saved my daughter on my own.<end>"
KO: "아니 아니, 나 혼자서는 딸을 구하지 못했을 게야.<end>"

## 1380015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に感謝しとる。<end>"
EN: "I truly am grateful.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사하고 있네.<end>"

## 1380016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは…昔…。<line-break>愛する人を守れなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Long ago... I failed to protect someone I loved.<end>"
KO: "나는… 예전에…. 사랑하는 사람을 지키지 못했다.<end>"

## 1380017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの時ばかりは…<line-break>わしも死にたくなった。<end>"
EN: "That was the one time... I wanted to die as well.<end>"
KO: "그때만큼은… 나도 죽고 싶었지.<end>"

## 1380018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がいなければ…、<line-break>わしはまた…<end>"
EN: "If it hadn't been for you, I would have... again...<end>"
KO: "네가 없었더라면… 나는 또….<end>"

## 1380019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主には感謝してもしきれぬな。<end>"
EN: "I can never thank you enough.<end>"
KO: "자네에겐 아무리 감사해도 모자라겠군.<end>"

## 1380020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうはいかん。<line-break>このままではわしの気持ちが<line-break>収まらぬ。<end>"
EN: "No, that will not do. I cannot let the matter rest here.<end>"
KO: "그럴 순 없지. 이대로는 내 마음이 풀리지 않아.<end>"

## 1380021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、感謝の意味を込めて…<line-break>酒席を設けた。<end>"
EN: "So, as a token of my gratitude... I have arranged a little drinking party.<end>"
KO: "그래서 감사의 뜻을 담아… 술자리를 마련했다.<end>"

## 1380022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、受けぬと言わんじゃろうな？<end>"
EN: "Surely you do not mean to refuse?<end>"
KO: "설마 받지 않겠다고 하진 않겠지?<end>"

## 1380023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハ、<line-break>無理に飲まんでもよい。<line-break>わしにつきあってくれれば十分じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! You need not force yourself to drink. Keeping me company will be enough.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 억지로 마실 필요는 없다. 나와 함께 있어 주기만 하면 충분하다.<end>"

## 1380024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、そうと決まれば善は急げじゃ。<line-break>さっそく、酒場に繰り出そうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good, then it is settled. No time like the present―let us be off to the tavern!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 정했으면 쇠뿔도 단김에 빼야지. 당장 술집으로 가자꾸나.<end>"

## 1380025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>見てください、<line-break>かわいい猫さんがいるんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>. Look, there's such a sweet little cat here.<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28> 씨. 보세요, 귀여운 고양이가 있어요.<end>"

## 1380026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "に…にぁぁああ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>………………<end>"
EN: "M-mrraaaow...<end>"
KO: "냐… 냐아아아. ………………<end>"

## 1380027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたんですか？<line-break>怖い顔して。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Why the fierce look?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러세요? 무서운 표정이네요.<end>"

## 1380028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ごろごろ、ごろにゃーん<end>"
EN: "P-purr, purr... purrr-meow!<end>"
KO: "고, 고로롱, 고로냥~<end>"

## 1380029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、よしよし。<line-break>猫さんはいい子ですねぇ。<end>"
EN: "There, there. What a good kitty you are.<end>"
KO: "그래그래. 고양이 착하지.<end>"

## 1380030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "にゃ…。　　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>………………<end>"
EN: "Meow...<end>"
KO: "냐…. ………………<end>"

## 1380031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたの？<line-break>私の顔に何かついてる？<end>"
EN: "What is it? Is there something on my face?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 내 얼굴에 뭐 묻었어?<end>"

## 1380032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…この猫さん、<line-break>何か言いたげに見えませんか？<line-break> <line-break>何かこう、猫さんの瞳に、<line-break>訴えかけてくるものを<line-break>感じるような…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... doesn't this kitty look as if it wants to say something? There's something in its eyes, almost as if it's trying to tell me something...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 이 고양이, 뭔가 말하고 싶은 것 같지 않나요? 눈빛에서 뭔가 호소하는 느낌이….<end>"

## 1380033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…な、なーん。<end>"
EN: "M-mrrow...<end>"
KO: "…나, 냥.<end>"

## 1380034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんね、私、猫さんの言葉は<line-break>わからないの。<line-break> <line-break>ミズチがそうだったみたいに、いつか<line-break>わかるようになったらいいのに。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I don't understand what cats say. I wish I could understand you someday, the way I did Mizuchi.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 난 고양이 말을 못 알아들어. 미즈치처럼 언젠가는 알아들을 수 있게 되면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 1380035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしたら…きっと、猫さんとも<line-break>いいお友達になれると思うのだけれど。<end>"
EN: "Then... I'm sure the kitty and I could become good friends.<end>"
KO: "그러면… 분명 고양이 씨와도 좋은 친구가 될 수 있을 것 같은데.<end>"

## 1380036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン様。<line-break>そろそろ、戻りますよ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn. It's nearly time to return.<end>"
KO: "이크레문 님. 슬슬 돌아가시죠.<end>"

## 1380037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官長様です。<line-break>楽しい時はあっという間に<line-break>過ぎ去ってしまうものですね。<end>"
EN: "It's the High Priest. Happy moments always pass so quickly, don't they?<end>"
KO: "신관장님이십니다. 즐거운 시간은 순식간에 지나가 버리는 법이군요.<end>"

## 1380038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>またお会いできる時を楽しみに<line-break>しています。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, I look forward to seeing you again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28> 씨, 다시 만날 날을 기대하고 있겠습니다.<end>"

## 1380039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫さんも、ね。<end>"
EN: "And you too, kitty.<end>"
KO: "고양이도, 응.<end>"

## 1380040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだよ。<end>ケリュネイアに付き合ってて<line-break>すきをついて自由行動してんだよ。<line-break>悪いか？<end>"
EN: "W-what?<end>I'm with Ceryneia, and I saw my chance to slip off on my own. What's wrong with that?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야.<end>케류네이아랑 어울리다가틈을 봐서 자유행동 중인 거야. 불만이냐?<end>"

## 1380041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺の勝手だろ。<line-break>昔のことを謝ろうとして、<line-break>うまくいかなかっただけだ。<line-break>あいつ、さすがに巫女だけあって、<line-break>ちっとヒヤッとしたけどよ…<line-break>謝るってことは、難しいもんだ。<end>"
EN: "It's none of your business. I only tried to apologize for what happened before, and it didn't work out. She really is a priestess. Gave me a bit of a scare there... Apologizing isn't easy.<end>"
KO: "…내 맘이잖아. 옛날 일을 사과하려다가 잘 안 됐을 뿐이야. 그 녀석, 역시 무녀라 그런지 조금 서늘하긴 했지만…. 사과라는 건 참 어렵네.<end>"

## 1380042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを…あんな風にされちゃ、<line-break>なおさら言い出しづれえじゃねえか。<line-break> <line-break>いいお友達、だと？<line-break>ちっ、このネモ様に向かって、<line-break>あ、あんなこと言いやがって…。<end>"
EN: "And after she treated me like that... how was I supposed to bring it up? “Good friends,” she says? Tch. Saying a-a thing like that to the great Nemo...<end>"
KO: "그걸… 그런 식으로 해 버리면 더 말 꺼내기 어렵잖아. 좋은 친구라고? 쳇, 이 네모 님한테 그, 그런 말을 하다니….<end>"

## 1380043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人なんてよ、自分より強い奴なんて<line-break>めったにいねえって立場だからな。<line-break>例えいたとして、そいつに倒されても…<line-break>ちょっとばかし眠りゃ元通り…。<line-break>あとに残される奴の気持ちなんざ<line-break>考える必要すらなかったのさ。<end>"
EN: "When you're a demon, you hardly ever meet anyone stronger than you. Even if you do and they defeat you... a little sleep puts you right again. I never even had to think about how the ones left behind might feel.<end>"
KO: "마인이라는 건 자기보다 강한 놈이 거의 없는 입장이잖아. 설령 있다 해도 그 녀석에게 쓰러져 조금 자고 나면 원래대로 돌아오지…. 남겨지는 사람의 마음 따위 생각할 필요조차 없었던 거야.<end>"

## 1380044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫なんて弱っちいもんになってみて、<line-break>不覚ながら、俺はそういうことに<line-break>気が回るようになっちまった。<line-break>も、もちろん、あのオトコオンナに<line-break>感謝するなんてことは、<line-break>空が落ちてくるよりありえねえがよ。<end>"
EN: "Now that I've been turned into a weak little thing like a cat, I've started thinking about that sort of thing, much as it pains me to admit it. N-not that I'd ever thank that she-man. The sky would fall first.<end>"
KO: "고양이 같은 약해빠진 것이 되어 보니, 나도 모르게 그런 데까지 신경 쓰게 됐지. 무, 물론 그 남자 같은 여자한테 감사한다는 건 하늘이 무너지는 것보다도 있을 수 없는 일이지만 말이야.<end>"

## 1380045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、そろそろ、戻らねぇと。<line-break>ケリュネイアにチクられでもしたら、<line-break>一大事だからな。<line-break>じゃあな。あんまり夜更かしすんなよ。<end>"
EN: "Oops, I'd better get back. If Ceryneia tattles on me, I'll be in real trouble. See you. Don't stay up too late.<end>"
KO: "이런, 슬슬 돌아가야겠군. 케류네이아에게 일러바치기라도 하면 큰일이니까. 그럼 잘 가. 너무 늦게까지 깨어 있진 마.<end>"

## 1380046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、誰かと思えば、<line-break><value:$28>ではないか。<line-break>どうした、眠れんのか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you, <value:$28>. What is it? Can't you sleep?<end>"
KO: "오, 누군가 했더니 <value:$28> 아닌가. 왜 그래, 잠이 안 오나?<end>"

## 1380047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>わしは今宵、<line-break>星を眺めに出てきたのじゃよ。<end>"
EN: "I see... I came out to gaze at the stars tonight.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 나는 오늘 밤 별을 보러 나왔네.<end>"

## 1380048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "星は、刻々夜空を流れ続けとる。　<line-break>詳しい者でなければ、望み通りの<line-break>星空を見ることはかなわん。<line-break>ま、見てのとおり、わしは<line-break>占星術者でも学者でもないがの、<line-break>それでも、こうして見上げていれば、<end>"
EN: "The stars move ceaselessly across the night sky. Unless you know them well, you cannot see the sky you hope for. As you can see, I am neither an astrologer nor a scholar. Still, I kept looking up like this because...<end>"
KO: "별은 시시각각 밤하늘을 흘러가고 있어. 자세히 아는 자가 아니면 원하는 별하늘을 보는 건 어렵지. 뭐, 보다시피 난 점성술사도 학자도 아니지만 그래도 이렇게 올려다보고 있으면….<end>"

## 1380049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひょっとしたら現れてくれるのでは<line-break>ないかと思ったんじゃ。<line-break><line-break>聖母神ティラの元へと帰るために、<line-break>一途空を駆けている、<line-break>イズキヤルめの魂がのう…。<end>"
EN: "I thought perhaps it might appear―the soul of that rascal Izkiyal, racing through the heavens with but one purpose: to return to the Mother Goddess Tira...<end>"
KO: "혹시 나타나 줄지도 모른다고 생각했지. 성모신 티라에게 돌아가기 위해 한결같이 하늘을 달리는 이즈키얄의 영혼이 말이야….<end>"

## 1380050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死者の魂が星となり輝く…。<line-break>これは、わしの故郷に<line-break>古く言い伝わっておる話じゃ。<line-break>伝承を生んだ古代の民は、<line-break>星空を見上げ、それほど死んでいった<line-break>者が多いのだと考えたのじゃろうか。<end>"
EN: "The souls of the dead become stars and shine in the heavens... It is an ancient tale from my homeland. I wonder if the people of old who gave rise to it looked up at the night sky and thought that so many had died.<end>"
KO: "죽은 자의 영혼이 별이 되어 빛난다…. 이건 내 고향에 오래전부터 전해 내려오는 이야기다. 그 전승을 만든 고대 사람들은 별하늘을 보며 그만큼 죽은 사람이 많았다고 생각한 걸까.<end>"

## 1380051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このまま乱世が続けば、いずれ夜空は<line-break>星で埋めつくされるのではないか…<line-break>そんなガラにもない不安すら湧きよる。<line-break>しかし、おぬしも聞いたじゃろう？<line-break>イズキヤルは、奴の姉妹どもの命を、<line-break>わしらにゆだねたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "If this age of war goes on, perhaps one day the night sky will be filled with stars... I even find myself harboring fears that hardly suit me. But you heard what Izkiyal asked of us, did you not? The lives of Izkiyal's sisters were left in our hands.<end>"
KO: "이대로 난세가 이어지면 언젠가 밤하늘이 별로 가득 차지 않을까… 나답지 않은 불안까지 드는군. 하지만 자네도 들었겠지? 이즈키얄는 자기 자매들의 목숨을 우리에게 맡겼다네.<end>"

## 1380052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らがイズキヤルと同様に<line-break>心を開いてくれるのであれば、<line-break>わしも悩みはせぬ。<line-break>しかし、その保証はどこにもない…。<line-break>もし、手遅れだったとしたら、<line-break>おぬしはどうするつもりじゃ？<end>"
EN: "If they open their hearts to us as Izkiyal did, I shall have no cause for concern. But there is no guarantee they will... If it is already too late, what will you do?<end>"
KO: "그들이 이즈키얄처럼 마음을 열어 준다면 나도 고민하지 않을 걸세. 하지만 그 보장은 어디에도 없어…. 만약 이미 늦었다면 자넨 어떻게 할 셈인가?<end>"

## 1380053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力で立ち向かう<end>"
EN: "Use force against them<end>"
KO: "힘으로 맞선다<end>"

## 1380054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あくまで説き伏せる<end>"
EN: "Keep trying to reason with them<end>"
KO: "끝까지 설득한다<end>"

## 1380055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。無念じゃが、<line-break>それしかないじゃろうな。　　<line-break><line-break>わしらはともかく、無防備な<line-break>旅人なぞを襲わせてはならぬ。<line-break>その前に…そうなる前に…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Bitter as it is, that seems our only choice. Never mind us; we cannot allow them to attack defenseless travelers. Before that... before it comes to that...<end>"
KO: "음. 유감이지만 그것밖엔 없겠지. 우리야 그렇다 쳐도 무방비한 여행자까지 습격당하게 해서는 안 된다. 그 전에… 그렇게 되기 전에….<end>"

## 1380056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おぬしがそのような心を<line-break>持ってくれたことを、<line-break>わしはうれしく思うぞ。　　　　　<line-break>しかしな、あのイズキヤルにさえ、<line-break>かなり手を焼いたものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I am glad you feel that way. But even Izkiyal was quite a handful.<end>"
KO: "네가 그런 마음을 가져 준 것을 나는 기쁘게 생각한다. 하지만 그 이즈키얄조차 상대하기 꽤 힘들었지.<end>"

## 1380057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "牙を剥かれ、死にかけたこともあった。<line-break>それでもうまく行ったのは、<line-break>ただひとえに奴自身の改心による。<line-break>もし、向こうが聞き入れないとすれば、<line-break>その時は…やむをえん…。<end>"
EN: "Izkiyal bared those fangs at us, and we nearly died. Things ended well only because Izkiyal had a change of heart. If the others refuse to listen, then... there will be no other choice...<end>"
KO: "이를 드러내고 달려들어 죽을 뻔한 적도 있었다. 그래도 잘 풀린 건 오직 녀석 스스로 마음을 고쳐먹었기 때문이야. 만약 상대가 듣지 않는다면 그때는… 어쩔 수 없지….<end>"

## 1380058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…すまん。<line-break>とんだ顔を見せてしまった。<line-break>じゃが、気遣いは一切無用じゃぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ngh... forgive me. I've shown you a sorry sight. But don't trouble yourself over me.<end>"
KO: "큭… 미안하네. 꼴사나운 모습을 보여 버렸군. 하지만 신경 써 줄 필요는 전혀 없네.<end>"

## 1380059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むしろ、頼む。奴らに対する時には、<line-break>必ずわしを連れて行ってくれ。<line-break>引導が必要な時は引導を、<line-break>祈りが必要な時には祈りを、<line-break>奴らにささげてやれるじゃろう…。<end>"
EN: "Rather, I have a favor to ask. When the time comes to confront them, you must take me with you. If they need last rites, I can offer them last rites; if they need prayers, I can offer them prayers...<end>"
KO: "오히려 부탁하마. 녀석들을 상대할 때는 반드시 나를 데려가 다오. 인도가 필요할 때는 인도를, 기도가 필요할 때는 기도를 녀석들에게 바쳐 줄 수 있을 테니….<end>"

## 1380060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…こう言うと、<line-break>いかにも決まり文句の<line-break>ようになってしまうがな、<line-break>じき来る頃だと思っておったぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "This may sound terribly trite, but I thought you would be arriving about now, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…이렇게 말하면 뻔한 대사 같지만, 슬슬 올 때가 됐다고 생각하고 있었다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を不思議そうな顔をしておる。<line-break>わらわはエアぞ。<line-break>そなたがここに来るのは先刻承知じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Why the puzzled look? I am Er. I knew full well that you would come here.<end>"
KO: "뭘 그리 이상하다는 얼굴을 하느냐. 나는 에아다. 그대가 이곳에 올 것은 이미 알고 있었다.<end>"

## 1380063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それゆえ、そなたに会おうと、<line-break>わらわはここで待っておったのじゃ。<line-break>何も、驚くことでもあるまい。<end>"
EN: "That is why I waited here to meet you. Surely there is nothing so surprising about that.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그대를 만나려고 내가 여기서 기다리고 있었던 것이다. 딱히 놀랄 일도 아니지.<end>"

## 1380064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>そなたは、わらわの持つ<line-break>予知の力をどう感ずる？<line-break>そなたも、未来を見ることが<line-break>できたらよいと思うか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. What do you think of my power to foresee the future? Do you wish that you could see it as well?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 그대는 내가 가진 예지의 힘을 어떻게 생각하느냐? 그대도 미래를 볼 수 있으면 좋겠다고 생각하느냐?<end>"

## 1380065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見たい<end>"
EN: "I'd like to see the future.<end>"
KO: "보고 싶어<end>"

## 1380066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見たくない<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't want to see the future.<end>"
KO: "보고 싶지 않아<end>"

## 1380067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、フフフフ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "…후후, 후후후후.<end>"

## 1380068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ、すまぬ。<line-break>いや、わらわも無駄なことを<line-break>したものだと思ってな。<line-break>…尋ねる必要なぞ、<line-break>これっぽっちもなかったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, forgive me. I was merely thinking what a pointless thing I had done. There was not the slightest need to ask you.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나, 미안해. 나도 쓸데없는 짓을 했다고 생각해서 말이지. …물을 필요 따윈 조금도 없었어.<end>"

## 1380069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわには、あらかじめ、<line-break>そなたの答えることが<line-break>わかっておるのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "After all, I already knew what your answer would be...<end>"
KO: "짐은 애초에 네가 뭐라고 대답할지 알고 있으니 말이다….<end>"

## 1380070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、そなたといえども、<line-break>未来については、とりわけ奇抜な考えは<line-break>持っておらぬのだな…。<line-break>少し、勉強になった。<end>"
EN: "I see. So even you hold no particularly unusual views on the future. I have learned something.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 자네라도 미래에 대해서는 특별히 기발한 생각을 가진 건 아니군…. 조금 배웠다.<end>"

## 1380071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先が見えてしまうというのは、<line-break>何とも味気ないことなのじゃ。<line-break>何にも、感動せぬようになるのでな…。<line-break>見たくない時は見ないという風に<line-break>できればよいが、<line-break>そう都合よくもできてはおらん…。<end>"
EN: "Seeing what lies ahead makes life dreadfully dull. In time, nothing moves you at all... If only I could choose not to look when I did not wish to see, but the power is not so obliging.<end>"
KO: "앞일이 보인다는 건 참 재미없는 일이지. 무엇에도 감동하지 않게 되거든…. 보고 싶지 않을 때는 보지 않게 할 수 있으면 좋겠지만 그렇게 편리하게 되어 있지도 않아….<end>"

## 1380072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、今宵の出会いが、<line-break>思いがけぬものだったとしたなら…<line-break>あるいは、わらわはそこに…<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "If, for instance, our meeting tonight had been unexpected... perhaps then I...<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 오늘 밤의 만남이 뜻밖의 것이었다면… 어쩌면 나는 그곳에서… ……………….<end>"

## 1380073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "翔王様が、わらわに向けて<line-break>風を送っておる…。<line-break>そろそろ神殿へ戻らねば。<line-break>短い逢瀬だったが、<line-break>不思議と気分が晴れた。<line-break>礼を言うぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The Soaring King is sending the wind my way... It is nearly time I returned to the temple. Our rendezvous was brief, yet somehow it has lifted my spirits. You have my thanks, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "상왕님께서 나를 향해 바람을 보내고 계시는구나…. 슬슬 신전으로 돌아가야겠다. 짧은 만남이었지만 이상하게 기분이 맑아졌구나. 고맙다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばじゃ。また会おう。<end>"
EN: "Farewell. We shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "잘 가게. 또 만나세.<end>"

## 1380075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、フフフフ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "…후후, 후후후후.<end>"

## 1380076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ、すまぬ。<line-break>いや、わらわも無駄なことを<line-break>したものだと思ってな。<line-break>…尋ねる必要なぞ、<line-break>これっぽっちもなかったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, forgive me. I was merely thinking what a pointless thing I had done. There was not the slightest need to ask you.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나, 미안해. 나도 쓸데없는 짓을 했다고 생각해서 말이지. …물을 필요 따윈 조금도 없었어.<end>"

## 1380077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわには、あらかじめ、<line-break>そなたの答えることが<line-break>わかっておるのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "After all, I already knew what your answer would be...<end>"
KO: "짐은 애초에 네가 뭐라고 대답할지 알고 있으니 말이다….<end>"

## 1380078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、こうして<line-break>そなたの口からそれを聞くと、<line-break>また違った驚きがあるな。<line-break>…そうか、そなたは未来など<line-break>見たくはないと申すのだな？<end>"
EN: "And yet, hearing you say it yourself brings a different sort of surprise. So, you truly have no wish to see the future?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이렇게 네 입으로 그 말을 들으니 또 다른 놀라움이 있군. …그런가, 너는 미래 따위 보고 싶지 않다는 말이로군?<end>"

## 1380079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先が見えてしまうというのは、<line-break>何とも味気ないことなのじゃ。<line-break>何にも、感動せぬようになるのでな…。<line-break>見たくない時は見ないという風に<line-break>できればよいが、<line-break>そう都合よくもできてはおらん…。<end>"
EN: "Seeing what lies ahead makes life dreadfully dull. In time, nothing moves you at all... If only I could choose not to look when I did not wish to see, but the power is not so obliging.<end>"
KO: "앞일이 보인다는 건 참 재미없는 일이지. 무엇에도 감동하지 않게 되거든…. 보고 싶지 않을 때는 보지 않게 할 수 있으면 좋겠지만 그렇게 편리하게 되어 있지도 않아….<end>"

## 1380080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、今宵の出会いが、<line-break>思いがけぬものだったとしたなら…<line-break>あるいは、わらわはそこに…<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "If, for instance, our meeting tonight had been unexpected... perhaps then I...<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 오늘 밤의 만남이 뜻밖의 것이었다면… 어쩌면 나는 그곳에서… ……………….<end>"

## 1380081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（夜風の気持ちいい晩だ…。<line-break>　少し、散歩でもしようか）<end>"
EN: "(What a pleasant night breeze... Perhaps I'll take a little walk.)<end>"
KO: "(밤바람이 기분 좋은 밤이다…. 조금 산책이라도 할까.)<end>"

## 1380082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばじゃ。また会おう。<end>"
EN: "Farewell. We shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "잘 가게. 또 만나세.<end>"

## 1380083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んっ…。<end>"
EN: "Nnh...<end>"
KO: "응….<end>"

## 1380084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、誰かと思ったら、<line-break><value:$28>じゃない。　　　<line-break><line-break>ねえ、こっち来ない？<line-break>ボク、ちょうど<value:$28>と<line-break>話したいことがあったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I was wondering who that was. It's you, <value:$28>. Hey, want to come over here? There's something I've been wanting to talk to you about, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 누군가 했더니 <value:$28>잖아. 있지, 이쪽으로 안 올래? 마침 <value:$28>랑 얘기하고 싶은 게 있었어.<end>"

## 1380085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…静かな夜だね。　　　　　　　<line-break><line-break><line-break><value:$28>、キミはきっと<line-break>不思議に思ってるはずだよね。<end>"
EN: "It's a quiet night... There's something you've probably been wondering about, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…조용한 밤이네. <value:$28>, 넌 분명 이상하게 생각하고 있겠지.<end>"

## 1380086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何って、ほら、ボクが突然、<line-break>ゴメン！　って、<line-break>いなくなっちゃうことについてさ。<end>"
EN: "What? You know, how I suddenly say, “Sorry!” and disappear.<end>"
KO: "뭐냐니, 있잖아. 내가 갑자기 '미안!' 하고 사라져 버리는 일 말이야.<end>"

## 1380087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあこれからでっかい依頼に<line-break>チャレンジするぞ！　って時でも、<line-break>その時がくれば、<line-break>ボクはいなくなっちゃう。<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Even when we're all set to take on some huge job, once the time comes, I disappear.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 큰 의뢰에 도전한다! 하는 순간에도 그때가 오면 나는 사라져 버려.<end>"

## 1380088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…それとも、案外そんなに<line-break>気にしてなかったりして？<end>"
EN: "Oh... Or maybe it didn't really bother you that much?<end>"
KO: "아… 아니면 의외로 그렇게까지 신경 쓰지 않는 건가?<end>"

## 1380089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にしていた<end>"
EN: "It bothered me<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰고 있었다<end>"

## 1380090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にしてなかった<end>"
EN: "It didn't bother me<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰지 않았어<end>"

## 1380091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだよね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ボクから仲間にして欲しいって<line-break>言っておきながら、急にゴメン！　<line-break>っていなくなるわけなんだもん。<end>"
EN: "Right... I was the one who asked to join you, and then I suddenly say, “Sorry!” and disappear.<end>"
KO: "…그렇지. 내가 먼저 동료가 되고 싶다고 해 놓고 갑자기 미안! 하고 사라져 버렸으니까.<end>"

## 1380092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントにゴメンって思ってる。<line-break>ホントにホントだよ！<line-break>　<line-break>ボクだってホントは<line-break><value:$28>やみんなと<line-break>冒険したいんだ…。<end>"
EN: "I really am sorry. I mean it―I really do! The truth is, I want to go adventuring with you and everyone else, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "정말 미안하다고 생각해. 진짜야! 나도 사실은 <value:$28>이랑 모두와 함께 모험하고 싶어….<end>"

## 1380093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見たことない山や海、世界の不思議や、<line-break>いろんな国々の、たくさんの人たちと<line-break>触れ合ってみたいんだ！<line-break>だから…だからね…<end>"
EN: "I want to see mountains and seas I've never seen before, discover the wonders of the world, and meet all kinds of people from all kinds of countries! So... so...<end>"
KO: "본 적 없는 산과 바다, 세상의 신비와 여러 나라의 수많은 사람들을 만나 보고 싶어! 그래서… 그래서 말이야….<end>"

## 1380094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…待っててくれるよね、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "You'll wait for me, won't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…기다려 줄 거지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1380095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キミは優しいね、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんなボクに腹を立てたりしないで、<line-break>仲間として見てくれるんだもの。<line-break>ボク、頑張るよ！<line-break>これからもよろしくね、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "You're so kind, <value:$28>. You don't get angry with me for any of this. You still see me as one of you. I'll do my best! I hope you'll still have me, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "넌 다정하구나, <value:$28>. 이런 나한테 화내지도 않고 동료로 봐 주다니. 나 열심히 할게! 앞으로도 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1380096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうなんだ。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ちょっとびっくりしたけど、<line-break><value:$28><line-break>らしいといえば、らしいかもね。<end>"
EN: "O-oh. I see. That caught me a little off guard, but then again, I suppose it is like you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇구나. 조금 놀랐지만, <value:$28>답다고 하면 또 그런 것 같네.<end>"

## 1380097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>がどれだけすごい<line-break>冒険者かは、前に依頼したボクが<line-break>一番良く知ってる。<end>"
EN: "I've hired you before, <value:$28>, so I know better than anyone what an amazing adventurer you are.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 얼마나 대단한 모험자인지는 전에 의뢰했던 내가 가장 잘 알아.<end>"

## 1380098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の腕があれば、<line-break>引く手あまたになって当然だもん…。<line-break><line-break>ふふ、ゴメン、<line-break>何を話したかったのか、<line-break>わかんなくなっちゃったよ…。<end>"
EN: "With your skills, <value:$28>, it's only natural that everyone would want you... Hehe, sorry. I've forgotten what I wanted to talk about...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 정도 실력이면 여기저기서 찾는 게 당연하잖아…. 후후, 미안. 무슨 말을 하려던 건지 모르겠어….<end>"

## 1380099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "明日も早いんだったよね。<line-break>頑張らなくっちゃ！<line-break><line-break>おやすみ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Tomorrow's another early morning, isn't it? I'd better do my best! Good night, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "내일도 일찍 일어나야 했지. 힘내야겠다! 잘 자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ、<line-break><value:$28>じゃん。<end>"
EN: "Oh! It's <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>잖아.<end>"

## 1380101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まーた、<line-break>間の悪いところに来ちゃうんだな～。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you caught me at a bad time again.<end>"
KO: "또 참 타이밍 안 좋을 때 왔네~.<end>"

## 1380102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をしてたかって？<end>"
EN: "What was I doing, you ask?<end>"
KO: "뭘 하고 있었냐고?<end>"

## 1380103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まっ、いっか。<line-break>別に隠すことでもないし。<end>"
EN: "Oh, well. It's not as if I need to hide it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐나. 딱히 숨길 일도 아니고.<end>"

## 1380104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペウダのバカのとこから、<line-break>女の子助けたの憶えてる？<line-break>あの子から、手紙が来ててねー。<line-break>宝石、ありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげさまで、母と一緒に<line-break>身を隠すことができました、ってね。<end>"
EN: "Remember rescuing that girl from that idiot Peuda? She sent me a letter. It said, “Thank you for the jewel. Thanks to you, my mother and I were able to go into hiding.”<end>"
KO: "페우다 그 바보한테서 여자애 구해 준 거 기억나? 그 아이한테 편지가 왔어. 보석 고마웠습니다, 덕분에 엄마와 함께 몸을 숨길 수 있었습니다, 라고.<end>"

## 1380105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に気にしなくていいのにねー。<end>"
EN: "She really didn't need to worry about it.<end>"
KO: "딱히 신경 쓰지 않아도 되는데 말이야.<end>"

## 1380106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あれのおかげで、<line-break>色々手に入ったしねー。 <end>"
EN: "Besides, I got all sorts of things out of it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 덕분에 이것저것 손에 넣었으니까.<end>"

## 1380107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はいはい、詮索はそこまで。<line-break>あんまり、夜更かししてると<line-break>明日がきついやーね。<end>"
EN: "All right, that's enough prying. Stay up too late and tomorrow's going to be rough.<end>"
KO: "자자, 캐묻는 건 거기까지. 너무 늦게까지 깨어 있으면 내일 힘들어.<end>"

## 1380108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、寝る準備、寝る準備！<line-break>着替えるから、男は部屋を出る！<end>"
EN: "Now, time to get ready for bed! I'm changing, so men out of the room!<end>"
KO: "자, 잘 준비, 잘 준비! 갈아입을 테니까 남자는 방에서 나가!<end>"

## 1380109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…。<line-break>ほんと鈍いわねー。<line-break>あれじゃ、アイリーンも苦労するわ…。<end>"
EN: "Honestly... How can anyone be that dense? Irene's got her work cut out for her...<end>"
KO: "하아…. 정말 둔하네. 저래서야 아이린도 고생하겠어….<end>"

## 1380110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの宝石が無かったら、この手紙も<line-break>もらえなかったかもしれないしー、<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>と<line-break>旅をするなんてことにも<line-break>ならなかったかもしれない。 <end>"
EN: "Without that jewel, I might not have received this letter, and I might not have ended up traveling with <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 보석이 없었다면 이 편지도 못 받았을지 모르고, <value:$28>와 여행하게 되는 일도 없었을지 몰라.<end>"

## 1380111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんなわけで、<line-break>十分対価はもらったような<line-break>気がするってわけ。<end>"
EN: "So I suppose I've been repaid well enough for it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 이유로 충분한 대가는 받은 것 같다는 거야.<end>"

## 1380112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、明日も早いし、<line-break>そろそろ寝ましょうかねー。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>も<line-break>あんまし、夜更かししちゃ<line-break>ダメよん。<end>"
EN: "Well, we've got an early start tomorrow, so I think it's about time for bed. Don't you stay up too late either, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 내일도 일찍 일어나야 하니 슬슬 자자. <value:$28>도 너무 늦게까지 깨어 있지 마.<end>"

## 1380113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃねー。<end>"
EN: "See you.<end>"
KO: "또 봐.<end>"

## 1380114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっ。<line-break>静かに。<line-break>黙って、こちらへ来て…。<end>"
EN: "Shh. Keep quiet. Come this way, and don't say a word...<end>"
KO: "쉿. 조용히. 말하지 말고 이쪽으로 와….<end>"

## 1380115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくいらしてくれました。<line-break>解放軍へ、ようこそ。<line-break> <line-break>といっても、細々と存続しているのが<line-break>やっとのこの組織…今となっては、<line-break>軍という名すら大仰かもしれない…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad you came. Welcome to the Liberation Army. Though we're barely managing to keep this organization alive... Perhaps calling ourselves an army is rather grandiose now.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 주셨습니다. 해방군에 오신 걸 환영합니다. …라고 해도 간신히 명맥만 이어 가는 조직이라 지금은 군이라는 이름조차 거창할지도 모르겠군요….<end>"

## 1380117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大商人の娘として育ち、欲したものを<line-break>何でもすぐに手に入れて当たり前という<line-break>生活をしてきた私の人生のうち、<line-break>今ほど、望んだものが手に入らず、<line-break>もがき苦しんだことなんて、<line-break>他にはないわ…。<end>"
EN: "I grew up the daughter of a wealthy merchant, accustomed to getting anything I wanted at once. Never in my life have I struggled and suffered so much, unable to obtain what I longed for...<end>"
KO: "대상인의 딸로 자라 갖고 싶은 건 뭐든 금방 손에 넣는 게 당연한 삶을 살아온 나지만, 지금처럼 원하는 것을 손에 넣지 못해 몸부림치며 괴로워한 적은 없었어….<end>"

## 1380118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そうやってもがいても、<line-break>結局、解放軍が望むものは…。<end>"
EN: "But no matter how I struggle, in the end, what the Liberation Army seeks...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇게 발버둥쳐도 결국 해방군이 바라는 것은….<end>"

## 1380119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それでもいいと思う。<line-break>あなたに救い出されたあの日から、<line-break>私は、そう考えられるようになったの。<line-break>今こうして生きている私には、<line-break>まだ十分可能性が残っている…。<line-break>晴れ晴れと、そう思えるようになった。<end>"
EN: "But even so, I think that's all right. Ever since the day you rescued me, I've been able to see it that way. I'm alive, and there are still so many possibilities open to me... Now I can believe that and feel truly hopeful.<end>"
KO: "그래도 난 괜찮다고 생각해. 네가 날 구해 준 그날부터 그렇게 생각할 수 있게 됐어. 지금 이렇게 살아 있는 내겐 아직 충분한 가능성이 남아 있어…. 이제는 후련하게 그렇게 생각할 수 있어.<end>"

## 1380120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何の解放も成し遂げていなかったと<line-break>思っていた私の解放軍…。<line-break> <line-break>けれどそれは、真っ先に、<line-break>私の心を解き放ってくれたのね…。<end>"
EN: "I thought my Liberation Army had never liberated anyone... But the first heart it set free was mine.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 해방하지 못했다고 생각했던 나의 해방군…. 하지만 그건 가장 먼저 내 마음을 해방해 주었구나….<end>"

## 1380121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度でもあなたに言いたい。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "I want to tell you again and again. Thank you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "몇 번이고 당신에게 말하고 싶어요. 고마워요, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1380122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…？<line-break>こんな夜遅くに、どうしたの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...? What are you doing up so late?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…? 이렇게 늦은 밤에 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 1380123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう。なかなか寝つけないのは、<line-break>私だけじゃなかったのね。<line-break>なんだか、少しうれしいわ。<end>"
EN: "I see. So I'm not the only one who can't get to sleep. I'm strangely glad.<end>"
KO: "…그래. 좀처럼 잠들지 못한 게 나만은 아니었구나. 왠지 조금 기뻐.<end>"

## 1380124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "嫌な汗にまみれてるわけでもない。<line-break>闇のように粘つく悪夢に<line-break>さいなまれているわけでもない…。<line-break>眠れない夜なのに、<line-break>こんなに気分がいいなんて、<line-break>ちょっと不思議な感じね。<end>"
EN: "I'm not drenched in a cold sweat. I'm not being tormented by nightmares that cling like darkness... It's strange to feel this good on a sleepless night.<end>"
KO: "싫은 땀에 젖어 있는 것도 아니고, 어둠처럼 끈적한 악몽에 시달리는 것도 아니야…. 잠 못 드는 밤인데 이렇게 기분이 좋다니 좀 신기한 느낌이네.<end>"

## 1380125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の中には、長いこと、<line-break>マゴスという化物が棲みついていた。<line-break><line-break>その、マゴスのいた部分がぽっかり<line-break>空虚な穴になってしまったようで、<line-break>少し、とまどう時があるの。<end>"
EN: "For a long time, a monster called Magos lived inside me. Now it sometimes feels as though the place it occupied has become a gaping void, and I don't quite know what to make of it.<end>"
KO: "내 안에는 오랫동안 마고스라는 괴물이 자리 잡고 있었어. 그 마고스가 있던 부분이 텅 빈 구멍이 된 것 같아서 가끔 당황스러울 때가 있어.<end>"

## 1380126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今晩みたいに、それが気になって<line-break>眠れないこともある…。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes, like tonight, it weighs on my mind and keeps me awake...<end>"
KO: "오늘 밤처럼 그게 신경 쓰여 잠들지 못할 때도 있어….<end>"

## 1380127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫、心配はしないで。<line-break>それとも、私がそんなに<line-break>頼りなさそうに見える？<end>"
EN: "I'm all right. Don't worry. Or do I really seem so helpless to you?<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, 걱정하지 마. 아니면 내가 그렇게못 미더워 보여?<end>"

## 1380128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな感じを味わってるのなんて、<line-break>世界中でも私だけかもしれないわね。<line-break>　<line-break>空虚だけれど、怖くはない。<line-break>胸の中を吹き過ぎる夜風が<line-break>涼しいような、そんな感じ…。<end>"
EN: "I may be the only person in the world who feels this way. There's an emptiness, but it isn't frightening. It's like a cool night breeze passing through my chest...<end>"
KO: "이런 느낌을 맛보는 건 세상에서 나뿐일지도 모르겠네. 공허하지만 무섭진 않아. 가슴속을 스쳐 가는 밤바람이 서늘한 것 같은, 그런 느낌….<end>"

## 1380129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の私は、その空洞が近い内に<line-break>埋まることを確信している…。<line-break>これが、人間本来の強さなのかしら。<end>"
EN: "Now I'm certain that void will soon be filled... I wonder if this is the strength human beings possess by nature.<end>"
KO: "지금의 나는 그 공허가 머지않아 채워질 거라고 확신하고 있어…. 이게 인간 본래의 강함인 걸까.<end>"

## 1380130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスの空洞を満たすのは、私。<line-break>それが満たされて初めて、私は、<line-break>ひとりの完全な私になるのね…。<end>"
EN: "I am the one who will fill the void Magos left behind. Only when it is filled will I become my own complete self...<end>"
KO: "마고스의 공허를 채우는 건 나. 그게 채워져야 비로소 나는 하나의 완전한 내가 되는 거구나….<end>"

## 1380131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがいてくれなかったら、<line-break>こんな感じを味わうことなんて、<line-break>生涯なかったかもしれない…。<end>"
EN: "If you hadn't been there for me, I might have gone my whole life without ever knowing this feeling...<end>"
KO: "당신이 없었다면 이런 기분을 느끼는 일은 평생 없었을지도 몰라….<end>"

## 1380132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。生涯…なんて重い言葉を<line-break>持ち出したら、困ってしまうわよね。<line-break>フフフ…。<line-break>さあ、あなたはもう眠らなくては。<line-break>パーティのリーダーが寝不足では、<line-break>みんな力を出せなくなってしまうわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes... Bringing up something as weighty as my whole life must make you uncomfortable. Heh heh... Now, you need to get some sleep. If the party's leader is exhausted, no one will be able to do their best.<end>"
KO: "그래. 평생… 같은 무거운 말을 꺼내면 곤란하겠지. 후후후…. 자, 이제 넌 자야 해. 파티 리더가 잠을 못 자면 모두가 힘을 내지 못하잖아.<end>"

## 1380133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私なら大丈夫。エンシャントの<line-break>政庁では、机についたまま夜明かし<line-break>することなんて、ざらにあったもの。<end>"
EN: "I'll be fine. At the Ancient Government Hall, I often spent the whole night at my desk.<end>"
KO: "난 괜찮아. 엔샨트 정청에서는 책상에 앉은 채 밤을 새운 적도 흔했거든.<end>"

## 1380134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう少し夜風に吹かれて、<line-break>気分が落ち着いたら部屋へ戻るわ。<end>"
EN: "I'll stay out in the night breeze a little longer. Once I've settled down, I'll go back to my room.<end>"
KO: "조금 더 밤바람을 쐬고 마음이 진정되면 방으로 돌아갈게.<end>"

## 1380135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>おやすみなさい、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Good night, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "고마워. 잘 자, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1380136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break><end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…叔母貴と一杯やってたのさ。<end>"
EN: "...I was having a drink with my aunt.<end>"
KO: "…숙모랑 한잔하고 있었지.<end>"

## 1380138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴も結構イケる口でね。<line-break>叔母貴の作った肴をつまみながら<line-break>色々、話したもんさ。<end>"
EN: "She could hold her liquor, too. We'd drink, nibble on the food she'd made, and talk about all sorts of things.<end>"
KO: "숙모도 술을 꽤 잘해서 말이지. 숙모가 만든 안주를 먹으며 이런저런 이야기를 많이 했어.<end>"

## 1380139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴は…<line-break>いい人間とはいえなかったかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "My aunt... may not have been a good person.<end>"
KO: "숙모는… 좋은 사람이라고는 할 수 없었을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1380140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "政敵を葬ったことなど何度もある。<line-break>配下の者を捨て駒に使ったこともある。<line-break>邪魔な国を滅ぼしたこともある。<end>"
EN: "She disposed of political enemies more than once. She used those under her command as expendable pawns. She even destroyed a country that stood in her way.<end>"
KO: "정적을 없앤 적도 여러 번 있다. 부하를 버리는 말처럼 쓴 적도 있고, 방해되는 나라를 멸망시킨 적도 있다.<end>"

## 1380141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恨まれて当然だ。<line-break>悪く言われても無理はない。<end>"
EN: "People had every right to hate her. You couldn't blame them for speaking ill of her.<end>"
KO: "원망받아도 당연해. 나쁘게 말해도 무리는 아니지.<end>"

## 1380142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも…<line-break>俺にとっては、やっぱり優しい<line-break>…いい女だったよ。<end>"
EN: "Even so... she was always kind to me. She was a good woman.<end>"
KO: "그래도… 내겐 역시 상냥한… 좋은 여자였어.<end>"

## 1380143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…<line-break>お前さんも付き合ってくれるのかい？<end>"
EN: "Oh... You'll have a drink with me too?<end>"
KO: "그런가… 자네도 함께해 주겠나?<end>"

## 1380144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、すまねぇが、<line-break>もうちっと、酒とつまみを頼むわ。<end>"
EN: "Then, sorry to trouble you, but could you get a little more liquor and something to eat?<end>"
KO: "그럼 미안하지만 술이랑 안주 좀 더 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1380145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴…<line-break>心中しそこねちまったが…<line-break>まだ、そっちには行けねぇみたいだ。<line-break>でっかい土産話持っていくから…<line-break>もうちょっとだけ…<line-break>待っといてくれや…な。<end>"
EN: "Auntie... I didn't manage to die with you, but it looks like I can't join you just yet. I'll bring one hell of a story with me when I do, so... wait for me a little longer... all right?<end>"
KO: "숙모… 같이 죽진 못했지만… 아직 그쪽으로 갈 때는 아닌 모양이야. 엄청난 선물 이야기를 들고 갈 테니까… 조금만 더… 기다려 줘… 응.<end>"

## 1380146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、姉ちゃんか…。<line-break>夜更かししてると、お肌に悪いぜ。<line-break>もともと大したモンじゃねえんだから…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you, Sis... Staying up late is bad for your skin. Not that it was much to look at in the first place...<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 누나였나…. 밤늦게까지 안 자면 피부에 안 좋아. 원래도 대단한 건 아닌데….<end>"

## 1380147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し頭が悪くなった。<line-break>チャカのＩＮＴが１下がった。<end>"
EN: "Chaka got a little stupider. Chaka's ＩＮＴ dropped by 1.<end>"
KO: "차카는 조금 머리가 나빠졌다. 차카의 INT가 1 내려갔다.<end>"

## 1380148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し打たれ強くなった。<line-break>チャカのＶＩＴが１上がった。<end>"
EN: "Chaka got a little tougher. Chaka's ＶＩＴ rose by 1.<end>"
KO: "차카는 조금 더 맷집이 좋아졌다. 차카의 VIT가 1 올랐다.<end>"

## 1380149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なあ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>やっぱ、姉ちゃんは、<line-break>姉ちゃんだよな？<end>"
EN: "Hey, Sis... You're still you, right?<end>"
KO: "…있잖아, 누나. 역시 누나는 누나지?<end>"

## 1380150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことしみじみ思うなんて、　<line-break>自分でもヘンだと思うけど、<line-break>姉ちゃんは、俺が俺だから…<line-break>俺が姉ちゃんの弟だから、<line-break>こうして俺をしかってくれるんだよな？<end>"
EN: "I know it's strange to get all serious about it, but you scold me like this because I'm me... because I'm your little brother, right?<end>"
KO: "이런 걸 새삼 생각하는 나도 이상하지만, 누나는 내가 나이기 때문에… 내가 누나의 동생이기 때문에 이렇게 나를 혼내 주는 거지?<end>"

## 1380151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよな。<line-break>ちょっと、安心したよ。<line-break>へへへ…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, of course. That's a relief. Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 조금 안심했어. 헤헤….<end>"

## 1380152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "降ってわいたような成り行きで<line-break>姉ちゃんが貴族になっちゃって、　<line-break>俺、ちょっと心配だったんだよ。<line-break>おかげでボルボラは倒せたし、<line-break>俺たちの村も、もっと豊かになれる<line-break>見込みが見えてきた気がする。<end>"
EN: "You suddenly ended up a noble through a crazy twist of fate, and I was a little worried. It did let us defeat Bolbora, though, and now it feels like our village might have a chance to become more prosperous.<end>"
KO: "갑자기 일이 그렇게 돼서 누나가 귀족이 되어 버리니까 나도 좀 걱정했어. 덕분에 볼보라도 쓰러뜨렸고 우리 마을도 더 풍요로워질 희망이 보이는 것 같아.<end>"

## 1380153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けど…、けどさ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>姉ちゃん、頼むよ。<line-break>俺の姉ちゃんだってことを、<line-break>いつまでも忘れないでくれよ！<end>"
EN: "But... but, Sis! I'm begging you. Don't ever forget that you're my sister!<end>"
KO: "하지만… 하지만 말이야! 누나, 부탁해. 내 누나라는 걸 언제까지나 잊지 말아 줘!<end>"

## 1380154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなダサい弟だから、<line-break>足手まといになったら、<line-break>捨ててってくれてもかまわない。<line-break>でも、俺、姉ちゃんが<line-break>俺の知らない人間になっちまうなんて<line-break>耐えられそうにないんだよ！<end>"
EN: "I'm such a lame little brother, so if I get in your way, you can leave me behind. But I don't think I could bear it if you became someone I didn't know!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 촌스러운 동생이니 짐이 되면 버려도 괜찮아. 하지만 나, 누나가 내가 모르는 사람이 돼 버리는 건 견딜 수 없을 것 같아!<end>"

## 1380155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…い、いけねえ、明日から<line-break>また忙しくなるんだったよな。<line-break>　<line-break>貴族のフリをするんだから、<line-break>勉強もしなくちゃだめだぜ、<line-break>姉ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "O-oh, crap. We're going to be busy again starting tomorrow, aren't we? If you're going to pretend to be a noble, you've got to study too, Sis.<end>"
KO: "…아, 이런. 내일부터 또 바빠지는 거였지. 귀족인 척하려면 공부도 해야 한다고, 누나.<end>"

## 1380156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "田舎者っぽいボロがひとつでも<line-break>出たら、そこでお芝居は<line-break>おしまいだからな！<end>"
EN: "Let even one bit of your country-bumpkin side show, and the whole act is over!<end>"
KO: "촌놈 티가 하나라도 나면 그 자리에서 연극은 끝장이니까!<end>"

## 1380157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへへっ、その調子、その調子。　　　<line-break><line-break><line-break>一緒に行こうぜ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>一緒に、デカいことを成し遂げよう。<line-break>失敗なんか怖がる必要はないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, that's more like it. Let's go together, Sis. Let's do something big. There's no need to be afraid of failing.<end>"
KO: "헤헤헤, 그래, 그래. 같이 가자, 누나. 함께 큰일을 해내자. 실패 따위 두려워할 필요 없어.<end>"

## 1380158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえ貴族じゃなくなったって、<line-break>姉ちゃんは俺の姉ちゃん<line-break>なんだからさ。<end>"
EN: "Even if you stop being a noble, you'll still be my sister.<end>"
KO: "귀족이 아니게 돼도 누나는 내 누나니까.<end>"

## 1380159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私ですか？　<line-break>復興団の活動で、<line-break>ここの一室を借りたので…　　<line-break>あ、もう用事は済みましたわ。<line-break>戻る前に、一息つこうと<line-break>思っていたところです。<end>"
EN: "Me? I'm using one of the rooms here for the Restoration League's work. Oh, but I'm finished now. I was just thinking I'd catch my breath before heading back.<end>"
KO: "저요? 복흥단 활동 때문에 여기 방 하나를 빌렸거든요… 아, 볼일은 끝났어요. 돌아가기 전에 잠깐 쉬려던 참입니다.<end>"

## 1380162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしければ、<line-break>少しお話していかれませんか？<end>"
EN: "If you have a moment, would you stay and talk with me?<end>"
KO: "괜찮으시면 잠시 이야기하고 가시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1380163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>復興団の活動は、難しい仕事です。<line-break> <line-break>先のクーデターに影響を受けられた<line-break>方々をお助けするのが役割ですが、<line-break>中には、拒絶される方も多くて…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, the Restoration League's work is not easy. Our role is to help those affected by the recent coup, but many refuse our help...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 부흥단 활동은 어려운 일입니다. 지난 쿠데타의 영향을 받은 분들을 돕는 역할이지만 그중에는 거부하는 분도 많아서….<end>"

## 1380164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、無理もありません。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>彼らに打撃を与えたのは、 <line-break>他でもない、<line-break>私たち貴族なのですから…。<end>"
EN: "No, I can hardly blame them. It was we nobles, after all, who brought such suffering upon them...<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 무리도 아닙니다. 그들에게 타격을 준 건 다름 아닌 우리 귀족들이니까요….<end>"

## 1380165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けれど、くじけず、<line-break>何度でも挑戦しようと思っています。<line-break> <line-break>くどくど過去を振り返るより、<line-break>前を向いて進むことこそが、<line-break>明るい未来を作るいしずえとなる…。<end>"
EN: "But I will not lose heart. I mean to keep trying, no matter how many times it takes. Dwelling endlessly on the past will achieve less than facing forward and pressing on. That is how we lay the foundations for a brighter future...<end>"
KO: "하지만 꺾이지 않고 몇 번이고 도전할 생각입니다. 과거만 되씹는 것보다 앞을 보고 나아가는 것이야말로 밝은 미래를 만드는 초석이 되니까요….<end>"

## 1380166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの考えは間違っていますか？<line-break><value:$28>様？<end>"
EN: "Is Tiana wrong to think so, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "티아나의 생각은 틀린 건가요? <value:$28>님?<end>"

## 1380167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad...<end>"
KO: "다행이다….<end>"

## 1380168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、不思議ね。<line-break><value:$28>様と        <line-break>お話ししていると、<line-break>不可能なんてありえない、<line-break>そんな気持ちになってきますわ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. How strange. When I talk with you, <value:$28>, I begin to feel as though nothing is impossible.<end>"
KO: "후후, 신기하네요. <value:$28>님과 이야기하고 있으면 불가능한 일은 없다는 기분이 들어요.<end>"

## 1380169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "明日からまたがんばっていこうと<line-break>思います。<line-break> <line-break>ティアナを、ロストール復興団を<line-break>応援していてくださいね、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Tomorrow, I shall begin again and do my best. Please continue to support Tiana and the Rostorl Restoration League, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "내일부터 다시 힘내려고 해요. 티아나와 로스톨 부흥단을 응원해 주세요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1380170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いてくれ、<value:$28>！<line-break>マジな話、俺たち、とてつもなく<line-break>お前に感謝してるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>! Seriously, we can't thank you enough.<end>"
KO: "들어 줘, <value:$28>! 진심으로 우리 모두 정말 너한테 고마워하고 있어.<end>"

## 1380171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとヴァンは唐突だけどね、<line-break>そういう話をしてたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Van's being a little abrupt, but we were just talking about this.<end>"
KO: "반이 좀 갑작스럽긴 하지만, 그런 이야기를 하고 있었어.<end>"

## 1380172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの時、聖光石の坑道で、<line-break>もしヴァンと<value:$28>が<line-break>止めてくれなかったら…<line-break>たとえ死ななかったとして、<line-break>僕は今頃どうなっていたんだろう。<end>"
EN: "Back then, in the Holy Light Stone mine... if you and Van hadn't stopped me, <value:$28>... Even if I hadn't died, I wonder what would have become of me by now.<end>"
KO: "…그때 성광석 갱도에서 반과 <value:$28>이 막아 주지 않았다면… 죽지 않았더라도 난 지금 어떻게 됐을까.<end>"

## 1380173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ…。<end>"
EN: "Nadge...<end>"
KO: "나지….<end>"

## 1380174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの時、コーンスのためなら<line-break>死んでもいいって言ったのは、<line-break>決して、ハッタリなんかじゃないよ。<end>"
EN: "When I said I was willing to die for the Konsu, I wasn't bluffing.<end>"
KO: "그때 콘스를 위해서라면 죽어도 좋다고 한 건 절대 허세가 아니야.<end>"

## 1380175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、君たちに叩きのめされて、<line-break>それが必ずしも正しいことじゃないって<line-break>気付いたんだ。<line-break>死んじゃったら、何にもならない。<line-break>何にもできやしないんだってね。<end>"
EN: "But after you two beat me senseless, I realized that wasn't necessarily the right thing to do. If I died, it would all be for nothing. I wouldn't be able to do anything at all.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너희에게 호되게 당하고, 그게 반드시 옳은 건 아니라는 걸깨달았어. 죽고 나면 아무 의미도 없어. 아무것도 할 수 없다는 걸 말이야.<end>"

## 1380176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通りだぜ、ナッジ！<line-break>お前の幽霊なんか、見たくもねえ！<line-break><line-break>闇の力なんか手に入れて出てきても、<line-break>それをお前だと認めるなんざ、<line-break>俺はゴメンだからな！<end>"
EN: "Damn right, Nadge! I never want to see you as a ghost! Even if you came back wielding the power of darkness, I'd refuse to accept that thing as you!<end>"
KO: "그 말이 맞아, 나지! 네 유령 따윈 보고 싶지도 않아! 어둠의 힘을 손에 넣고 나타난다 해도 그걸 너라고 인정해 줄 생각은 없어!<end>"

## 1380177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなっちまってたら、<line-break>俺はためらいなくこの聖なる拳を<line-break>振るってやるところだ…。<line-break>俺たちの友情は、鉄より固え！<line-break>聖光石より固えんだと、<line-break>俺は信じてるんだからな！！<end>"
EN: "If that had happened, I'd have let you taste this holy fist without a moment's hesitation... Our friendship is stronger than iron! Stronger than Holy Light Stone! I believe that with all my heart!!<end>"
KO: "그렇게 됐으면 난 망설임 없이 이 성스러운 주먹을 휘둘렀을 거다…. 우리 우정은 철보다 단단해! 성광석보다도 단단하다고 난 믿으니까!!<end>"

## 1380178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、その例え、<line-break>なんだかよくわかんないな、ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I'm not sure that comparison makes much sense, Van.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그 비유는 잘 모르겠는데, 반.<end>"

## 1380179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、俺だって、よくわかんねえよ！<end>"

KO: "나, 나도 잘 모르겠어!<end>"

## 1380180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>このことを僕に気付かせてくれたのは、<line-break>君だよ。<line-break>僕は、僕として、僕自身の力で、<line-break>しっかり生きていく。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you're the one who made me see that. I'm going to live my own life, as myself, on my own two feet.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 이걸 깨닫게 해 준 건 너야. 나는 나로서, 내 힘으로 제대로 살아갈 거야.<end>"

## 1380181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それ以上に大事なことなんて、<line-break>それ以上にすばらしいことなんて、<line-break>他にあるかい？<end>"

KO: "그보다 더 중요한 게, 그보다 더 멋진 게 또 있을까?<end>"

## 1380182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クゥー！　さすが俺のダチだ、　　　<line-break>いいこと言うぜ！<line-break><line-break>よし！　じゃ、晴れてトリオ結成を<line-break>声高らかに宣言しようじゃねえか！<end>"
EN: "Hah! That's my pal―couldn't have said it better! All right! Then let us proudly proclaim the official formation of our trio!<end>"
KO: "크으! 역시 내 친구야, 좋은 말 하는데! 좋아! 그럼 당당히 트리오 결성을 크게 선언해 보자고!<end>"

## 1380183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たち３人の前に敵はねえ！<line-break>どんな巨大な悪も、<line-break>首根っこ洗って待っトリオ！！<end>"
EN: "No enemy can stand against the three of us! Any great evil out there had better get ready for our tri-umph!!<end>"
KO: "우리 셋 앞에 적은 없다! 아무리 거대한 악이라도 목 닦고 기다리트리오!!<end>"

## 1380184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほ、ほんと、君と付き合ってると、<line-break>あらゆる意味で強くなれそうだよ、<line-break>ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "Y-you know, Van, being around you might make me stronger in every possible sense.<end>"
KO: "저, 정말 너랑 어울리다 보면 여러 의미로 강해질 것 같아, 반.<end>"

## 1380185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml

## 1380186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
