## 1380189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、そーよそーよ！<line-break>アタクシは、今、怒ってたのよっ！<line-break>力の限りにねえっ！<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシが！<line-break>どうしてあんな汚いベッドに<line-break>寝なくちゃならないのか！<end>"
EN: "Yes, yes, that's right! I was angry! As angry as I could possibly be! Why should I, a noble Elf, have to sleep in such a filthy bed?!<end>"
KO: "그래, 그래! 나 지금 화나 있었어! 있는 힘껏 말이야! 고귀한 엘프인 내가! 왜 그런 더러운 침대에서 자야 하는 거야!<end>"

## 1380190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてあんな強烈にマズい、<line-break>マメとイモばっかりの夕食を<line-break>食べなくちゃならないのか！<end>"
EN: "Why should I have to eat a dinner of nothing but beans and potatoes that tastes absolutely vile?!<end>"
KO: "왜 그렇게 끔찍하게 맛없는 콩과 감자뿐인 저녁을 먹어야 하는 거야!<end>"

## 1380191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてアンタは、どうしてこんな宿に<line-break>うれしそうに泊まってるのか！<line-break>納得できるように説明してちょうだい！<end>"
EN: "And why are you so delighted to be staying at an inn like this?! Give me an explanation I can accept!<end>"
KO: "그리고 넌 왜 이런 여관에 그렇게 좋아하며 묵고 있는 거야! 내가 납득할 수 있게 설명해 줘!<end>"

## 1380192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "第一、どこのどいつなのよー！<line-break>こんなひどいところに<line-break>泊まろうって言い出したアホウは…<line-break>………………！<end>"
EN: "And just who was the idiot who suggested staying in this dreadful place to begin with...?!<end>"
KO: "애초에 어느 바보야! 이런 형편없는 곳에서 묵자고 한 녀석은… ………………!<end>"

## 1380193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンタね、ド下等生物ッ！！<line-break>キイーーーッ！<line-break>許せない、許せないッ！<line-break>アンタと一緒に旅するように<line-break>なってから、アタクシは<line-break>運から見放されっぱなしなのよ！<end>"
EN: "You! You miserable lower life-form!! Aaargh! I can't forgive you! I simply can't! Ever since I started traveling with you, I've had nothing but bad luck!<end>"
KO: "당신 말이야, 최하등생물!! 키이이익! 용서 못 해, 용서 못 해! 당신과 같이 여행하게 된 뒤로 나는 운이 완전히 떠나 버렸다고!<end>"

## 1380194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつになったら、驚きに満ちた<line-break>世界とやらを見せてくれるつもりっ？<line-break>返答次第によっては、許さなくってよ！<end>"
EN: "Just when do you intend to show me this so-called world of wonders? Your answer had better be good, or I won't forgive you!<end>"
KO: "대체 언제 놀라움으로 가득한 세계라는 걸 보여줄 생각이야? 대답에 따라서는 용서 안 할 거야!<end>"

## 1380195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう少しの辛抱だ<end>"
EN: "Just be patient a little longer.<end>"

## 1380196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "In a way, this place is…<end>"

## 1380197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそくさいわ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break><line-break><line-break>最初に言ったと思うけれど、<line-break>エルフのアタクシが知らないような<line-break>驚異と神秘の世界なんて、<end>"
EN: "I don't believe you. I thought I told you at the outset: a world of wonders and mysteries unknown even to an Elf such as myself<end>"
KO: "수상하네. 처음에도 말했지만 엘프인 내가 모르는 경이와 신비의 세계라니….<end>"

## 1380198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう転がってやしないんだから！<end>"
EN: "is not something one simply stumbles upon!<end>"
KO: "그렇게 흔하게 굴러다니는 게 아니니까!<end>"

## 1380199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここが、ナンだと言うのかしら？<line-break>おびえに満ちた世界、とでも言う気？<end>"
EN: "What, exactly, are you claiming this place is? A world full of fear?<end>"
KO: "여기가 뭐가 어쨌다는 거지? 공포에 가득 찬 세계라고라도 할 셈이야?<end>"

## 1380200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん、滅多なことを<line-break>言うもんじゃなくてよ、下等生物。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. You shouldn't speak so carelessly, lower life-form.<end>"
KO: "흥, 함부로 말하지 마라, 하등생물.<end>"

## 1380201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいこと、これは命令よ！<line-break><line-break><line-break>近いうち、必ず、<line-break>驚きに満ちた世界とやらを<line-break>アタクシに見せないと、<end>"
EN: "Now listen: this is an order! Unless you show me this so-called world of wonders soon,<end>"
KO: "잘 들어, 이건 명령이야! 조만간 반드시 그 놀라움 가득한 세계라는 걸 나한테 보여 줘야 해.<end>"

## 1380202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいことっ！<end>"
EN: "Is that understood?!<end>"
KO: "좋은 일이야!<end>"

## 1380203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>どうして止めるのです。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>. Why are you stopping me?<end>"
KO: "………………. <value:$28>님. 왜 말리시는 겁니까.<end>"

## 1380204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の道具としての使命は<line-break>終わりました。<line-break>いえ…あなたが終わらせました。<line-break>道具として私を欲していた人達も<line-break>あなたが退けました。<end>"
EN: "My mission as a tool is over. No... you ended it. And you drove away those who wanted me as a tool.<end>"
KO: "도구로서의 제 사명은 끝났습니다. 아니… 당신이 끝내 주셨습니다. 저를 도구로 원하던 사람들도 당신이 물리쳤습니다.<end>"

## 1380205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私には、もう何も残っていません。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>しかし、この腕輪を葬らなければ…<line-break>私のまやかしの生命を終わらせる<line-break>ことはできないのです。<end>"
EN: "...I have nothing left. But unless I lay this bracelet to rest... I cannot end this illusory life of mine.<end>"
KO: "…제겐 이제 아무것도 남지 않았습니다. 하지만 이 팔찌를 묻지 않으면… 제 거짓된 생명을 끝낼 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1380206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>束縛の腕輪を私にお譲りください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please give me the Bracelet of Binding.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 속박의 팔찌를 제게 넘겨 주세요.<end>"

## 1380207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。<end>"

KO: "…고마워.<end>"

## 1380210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "束縛の腕輪を失った。<end>"
EN: "Lost the Bracelet of Binding.<end>"
KO: "속박의 팔찌를 잃었다.<end>"

## 1380211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、この命が終わる最期の時まで<line-break>私の果たすべき役目を果たしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Until the final moment of this life, I shall fulfill the duty that remains to me.<end>"
KO: "나는 이 생명이 끝나는 마지막 순간까지 내가 해야 할 역할을 다하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1380212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さようなら…<line-break>私に迷いを与えた人…。<end>"
EN: "Farewell... you who made me waver.<end>"
KO: "안녕히… 나를 망설이게 한 사람….<end>"

## 1380214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうやって…<line-break>私の身も心も縛るのですね。<line-break><line-break>こうした刹那の安らぎと引き換えに…。<end>"
EN: "So this is how you bind me... body and heart alike. And in return, you grant me these fleeting moments of peace.<end>"
KO: "그렇게… 제 몸과 마음까지 묶으시는군요. 이런 찰나의 안식과 맞바꾸어….<end>"

## 1380215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "狂おしいまでの切なさと<line-break>行き所のない恋しさを<line-break>抱えて生きろ…と言うのですね。<line-break>貴方は…卑怯…です…。<end>"
EN: "You would have me live with an ache enough to drive me mad... with a longing that has nowhere to go. You... are unfair...<end>"
KO: "미칠 듯한 애절함과 갈 곳 없는 그리움을 안고 살라는 거군요. 당신은… 비겁해요….<end>"

## 1380216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break><value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "………………. <value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1380217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか…<line-break>おごれし王よ…。<end>"
EN: "So you have come... O arrogant king.<end>"
KO: "왔는가… 오만한 왕이여….<end>"

## 1380218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　忌まわしき古き神は滅びた。　　　　<line-break>　　もはや、人類の革新を阻むものはない。<line-break>　　　　　　　　息子よ…<line-break>　　　　　　我の元へ来るのだ…。<end>"
EN: "The accursed god of old is no more. Nothing now stands in the way of Humanity's Evolution. My son... Come to me...<end>"
KO: "저주스러운 옛 신은 멸망했다. 이제 인류의 혁신을 막는 것은 없다. 아들이여… 내게 오너라….<end>"

## 1380219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、未だついえた夢を追いかけるか？<line-break>醜いものだな。<line-break>人類の革新には貴様など不要だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Still chasing a dream long since shattered? How unsightly. Humanity's Evolution has no need for the likes of you.<end>"
KO: "후후, 아직도 꺾인 꿈을 좇는 건가? 추하군. 인류의 혁신에 네놈 따윈 필요 없다.<end>"

## 1380220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　愚かな…　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　汝の希望…<line-break>　　　　　　ネメアは死んだ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　そして…<line-break>　　　無限のソウルでは人類の革新は<line-break>　　　　　　　　果たせぬ。<end>"
EN: "Fool... Your hope... Nemea is dead. And the Infinite Soul cannot bring about Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나…. 네 희망… 네메아는 죽었다. 그리고… 무한의 소울로는 인류의 혁신을 이룰 수 없다.<end>"

## 1380221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たしかに…今の<value:$28>に<line-break>人類は導けまい。<line-break>しかし…<end>"
EN: "True... As things stand, <value:$28> cannot lead humanity. But...<end>"
KO: "확실히… 지금의 <value:$28>에게 인류를 이끌게 할 수는 없겠지. 하지만….<end>"

## 1380222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王ごときに遅れを取る貴様に<line-break>人類が導けるとも思えぬ。<end>"
EN: "Nor can I believe you capable of leading humanity when even a mere Dragon King has bested you.<end>"
KO: "용왕 따위에게 뒤처지는 네놈이 인류를 이끌 수 있다고는 생각되지 않는다.<end>"

## 1380223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　息子よ。もう、一度だけ言っておこう。　　<line-break>　　　人類を革新に導ける存在は我のみ。<line-break>　　　　　　我と供に歩むのだ。<end>"
EN: "My son. I will tell you this only once more. I alone can lead humanity toward Humanity's Evolution. Walk beside me.<end>"
KO: "아들이여. 다시 한번만 말해 두마. 인류를 혁신으로 이끌 수 있는 존재는 오직 나뿐이다. 나와 함께 걸어라.<end>"

## 1380224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…<line-break>朽ちゆくだけの貴様に<line-break>それができるかな？<end>"
EN: "Heh... Can you truly do that when all you can do is rot away?<end>"
KO: "후후… 썩어 갈 뿐인 네놈에게 그게 가능할까?<end>"

## 1380225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　貴様……　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "You...<end>"
KO: "네놈……<end>"

## 1380226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "去るがいい…<line-break>貴様の口車に乗るほど<line-break>私は愚かではない！<end>"
EN: "Begone. I am not fool enough to be taken in by your honeyed words!<end>"
KO: "물러가라… 네 감언이설에 넘어갈 만큼 나는 어리석지 않다!<end>"

## 1380227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君にも聞こえていたか…<end>"
EN: "You heard it too, then...<end>"
KO: "너한테도 들렸나….<end>"

## 1380228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは…あの男は…。<line-break>あの程度で諦めるような奴ではない。<line-break>君も気をつけておくことだ。<end>"
EN: "That... that man... This will not be enough to make him give up. You would do well to stay on your guard.<end>"
KO: "저건… 그 남자는…. 저 정도로 포기할 녀석이 아니야. 너도 조심해 둬.<end>"

## 1380229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんな顔をするな。<end>"
EN: "Don't make that face.<end>"
KO: "…그런 얼굴 하지 마.<end>"

## 1380230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の傍に私がいる限り、<line-break>奴には指一本触れさせはせん。<end>"
EN: "As long as I am by your side, I will not let him lay a finger on you.<end>"
KO: "내가 네 곁에 있는 한, 그놈이 손가락 하나 대게 두진 않겠다.<end>"

## 1380239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔の血を引くネメアですら<line-break>我が剣を完全に防ぐことはできぬ。<end>"
EN: "Even Nemea, with demon blood in his veins, cannot fully stop my blade.<end>"
KO: "마의 피를 이은 네메아조차 내 검을 완전히 막을 수 없다.<end>"

## 1380240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "脆き人の子のお前が<line-break>我が剣を防ぐことは適わぬはずだ。<end>"
EN: "A frail child of man like you should not be able to block my blade.<end>"
KO: "나약한 인간인 네가 내 검을 막을 수 있을 리 없다.<end>"

## 1380241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "が…<line-break>うぬは我が剣を受けても<line-break>立ち上がってきた。<end>"
EN: "And yet... even after taking a blow from my sword, you rose again.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 너는 내 검을 맞고도 다시 일어섰다.<end>"

## 1380242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が、お前に<line-break>それほどの力をもたらす…。<end>"
EN: "What grants you such strength...?<end>"
KO: "무엇이 너에게 그토록 큰 힘을 주는 거지….<end>"

## 1380243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<end>"
EN: "I see...<end>"
KO: "그런가….<end>"

## 1380244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがうぬの力か…。<end>"
EN: "That is your power...<end>"
KO: "그게 네 힘인가….<end>"

## 1380245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その力が最強なのか…。<end>"
EN: "Is that power the mightiest of all...?<end>"
KO: "그 힘이 최강인 건가….<end>"

## 1380246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、我が力こそ最強なのか…。<end>"
EN: "Or is my power the mightiest...?<end>"
KO: "아니면 내 힘이야말로 최강인가….<end>"

## 1380247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この旅の終わりに答えが出るだろう…。<end>"
EN: "The answer will come at the end of this journey...<end>"
KO: "이 여행의 끝에서 답이 나오겠지….<end>"

## 1380248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し考え事をしていた。<end>"
EN: "I was just thinking.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 생각 좀 하고 있었어.<end>"

## 1380249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが…<line-break>お前達の見ていた世界なのか？<end>"
EN: "Is this... the world you have all seen?<end>"
KO: "이것이…너희가 보고 있던 세계인가?<end>"

## 1380250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰もが、苦しみ、悩み、傷つきながら<line-break>それでも…手を取り合って、<line-break>前に進んでいく。<end>"
EN: "People suffer, struggle, and get hurt. Even so... they join hands and keep moving forward.<end>"
KO: "누구나 괴로워하고, 고민하고, 상처받으면서도… 그래도 손을 맞잡고 앞으로 나아가.<end>"

## 1380251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は王宮を出るまで、そんな世界が<line-break>あるとは信じていなかった。<line-break>いや、信じることができなかった。<end>"
EN: "Until I left the royal palace, I didn't believe a world like that existed. No. I couldn't believe it.<end>"
KO: "나는 왕궁을 나오기 전까지 그런 세계가 있다고 믿지 않았어. 아니, 믿을 수 없었지.<end>"

## 1380252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "権力闘争では、人を信じることは<line-break>死に繋がる。<end>"
EN: "In a struggle for power, trusting someone can mean your death.<end>"
KO: "권력 투쟁에서는 사람을 믿는 일이 죽음으로 이어진다.<end>"

## 1380253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰も信じず、誰も頼りにしない。<line-break>相手の弱みを掴み、脅し、<line-break>そして、消す。<line-break>権力を掴むとはそういうことだ。<end>"
EN: "Trust no one. Rely on no one. Find an adversary's weakness, use it to threaten, and then make the adversary disappear. That is how power is seized.<end>"
KO: "아무도 믿지 않고, 아무에게도 의지하지 않는다. 상대의 약점을 잡고 협박하고, 그리고 없애 버린다. 권력을 잡는다는 건 그런 거다.<end>"

## 1380254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺の手は既に血塗られている。<line-break>そんな俺に…お前達の世界に<line-break>入る資格があるのだろうか？<end>"
EN: "My hands are already stained with blood. Does someone like me... have any right to enter your world?<end>"
KO: "…내 손은 이미 피로 물들었다. 그런 내가… 너희 세계에 들어갈 자격이 있을까?<end>"

## 1380255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は…<end>"
EN: "I...<end>"
KO: "나는…<end>"

## 1380256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<line-break>俺には、お前達がいるのだな。<line-break> <line-break>…これからもよろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "I see. I have you all. I hope I can continue to count on you.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군. 나에겐 너희가 있었구나. …앞으로도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1380257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<line-break>…世界の全てが敵に回ろうとも…<line-break>俺にはお前がいてくれる。<line-break>俺にはそれだけで十分だ。<end>"
EN: "I see. Even if the whole world turns against me... I will still have you. That alone is enough.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. …온 세상이 적이 된다 해도… 내겐 네가 있어. 그것만으로 충분해.<end>"

## 1380258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、<line-break>すまない…忘れてくれ。<end>"
EN: "You're right. I'm sorry... Forget I said anything.<end>"
KO: "그래, 미안하다… 잊어 줘.<end>"

## 1380259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ！<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah! <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "앗! <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、これ？<end>"
EN: "Oh, this?<end>"
KO: "아, 이거?<end>"

## 1380261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レルラのハープだよ<line-break>詩聖レルラの愛器だからね。<line-break>奏でてみようと思ったんだ。<end>"
EN: "It's Relra's Harp―the master poet Relra's cherished instrument. I thought I'd try playing it.<end>"
KO: "렐라의 하프야. 시성 렐라의 애기니까 한번 연주해 보려고 했어.<end>"

## 1380262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…やめたんだ。<end>"
EN: "But... I decided not to.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 그만뒀어.<end>"

## 1380263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくは今まで沢山の詩を作ってきた。<end>"
EN: "I've written so many poems over the years.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금까지 많은 시를 지어왔어.<end>"

## 1380264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "身も焦がれるような切ない恋の詩や。<line-break>血沸き肉躍る冒険の詩とか…。<end>"
EN: "Poems of aching, all-consuming love, and stirring poems of adventure that set the blood racing...<end>"
KO: "가슴이 타들어 갈 듯한 애절한 사랑의 시나, 피가 끓고 살이 뛰는 모험의 시 같은 거….<end>"

## 1380265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、<line-break>僕は満足したことは一度もないんだ。<end>"
EN: "But I've never once been satisfied with any of them.<end>"
KO: "하지만 나는 만족해 본 적이 한 번도 없어.<end>"

## 1380266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あそこで、ああしておけばよかった…<line-break>もっと、うまくできたんじゃないか？<line-break>いつも、いつも、そう思ってる。<end>"
EN: "If only I'd done that differently... Couldn't I have done it better? I always end up thinking that. Always.<end>"
KO: "그때 거기서 그렇게 했어야 했는데… 더 잘할 수 있지 않았을까? 늘, 언제나 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1380267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなぼくに、詩聖レルラのハープを<line-break>奏でる資格があるのかな？<end>"
EN: "Do I really deserve to play Relra's Harp when I'm like that?<end>"
KO: "그런 나에게 시성 렐라의 하프를 연주할 자격이 있을까?<end>"

## 1380268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん…。そうだね。<end>"
EN: "Mm... You're right.<end>"
KO: "응…. 그렇네.<end>"

## 1380269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ぼくは心から満足できる作品が<line-break>作れる日まで楽しみにとって置こう<line-break>って、思ってるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, I think I'll save it until the day I create something I'm truly satisfied with.<end>"
KO: "하지만 나는 마음속 깊이 만족할 작품을 만들 수 있는 날까지 즐거움으로 남겨 두려고 해.<end>"

## 1380270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。<line-break>それはきっと、<line-break>そう遠くない日のことだと思うよ。<end>"
EN: "It's all right. I'm sure that day isn't too far off.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 분명 그리 멀지 않은 날의 일일 거야.<end>"

## 1380271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって。<line-break><value:$28>と<line-break>旅をしてるんだからね！<end>"
EN: "After all, I'm traveling with <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그야 <value:$28>와 여행하고 있으니까!<end>"

## 1380272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん。<line-break>ぼくはやっぱりツイてるなぁ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I really am lucky!<end>"
KO: "음. 역시 나는 운이 좋구나!<end>"

## 1380273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼく、ちょっと近くの森まで行って<line-break>今の気持ちを曲にしてくるよ！<end>"
EN: "I'm going to pop over to the nearby woods and turn this feeling into music!<end>"
KO: "나, 근처 숲까지 가서 지금 기분을 곡으로 만들어올게!<end>"

## 1380274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ない！　ない！　ない！<line-break>レルラのハープがないよー！<end>"
EN: "Gone! Gone! Gone! Relra's Harp is gone!<end>"
KO: "없어! 없어! 없어! 렐라의 하프가 없어!<end>"

## 1380275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>何か知らない？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Do you know anything about it?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 뭐 아는 거 없어?<end>"

## 1380276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな…<line-break>ひ、ひどいや…<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "N-no... That's... that's awful, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가… 너무해… <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、ごめん。<line-break>お金が必要な時も…あるよね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, sorry. I suppose there are times when you need the money...<end>"
KO: "아, 미안. 돈이 필요할 때도… 있지.<end>"

## 1380278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、もういいんだ。<line-break><value:$28>も<line-break>気にしないで…。<end>"
EN: "No, forget it. Don't worry about it, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아니, 이제 됐어. <value:$28>도 신경 쓰지 마….<end>"

## 1380279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、眠るには<line-break>まだ早かったな、セラ。<end>"
EN: "I knew it was too early for you to turn in, Sera.<end>"
KO: "역시 잠들기엔 아직 이르군, 세라.<end>"

## 1380280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん。どうせこの話をし始めたら、<line-break>時間などいくらあっても<line-break>足りないだろうが。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Once we start talking about this, no amount of time will ever be enough.<end>"
KO: "흥. 어차피 이 이야기를 시작하면 시간이 아무리 많아도 모자라겠지만.<end>"

## 1380281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう意固地になるなよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't be so stubborn.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 고집부리지 마.<end>"

## 1380282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、セラと、シェスターのことに<line-break>ついて話していたんだ。<line-break><line-break>いや、正確には、彼女の言った<line-break>ことについて、かな…。<end>"
EN: "We were actually talking about you and Shester, Sera. Or rather, about something she said...<end>"
KO: "사실 세라와 셰스터에 대해 이야기하고 있었어. 아니, 정확히는 그녀가 했던 말에 대해서랄까….<end>"

## 1380283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってのとおり、シェスターは、<line-break>この世界の平穏と同時に、<line-break>精霊との共存を願っていた。<line-break>シェスターの口からそれが出たことで、<line-break>それがいかに重要なことであるかが<line-break>感じられたと思うが…<end>"
EN: "As you know, Shester wished not only for peace in this world, but also for humans and spirits to coexist. Hearing it from Shester herself must have brought home just how important that is...<end>"
KO: "알다시피 셰스터는 이 세계의 평온과 함께 정령과의 공존을 바랐어. 셰스터 입에서 그 말이 나왔다는 점에서 그게 얼마나 중요한 일인지 느꼈을 거라 생각하지만….<end>"

## 1380284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たやすいことではないだろうな。　<line-break><line-break><line-break>俺自身、これまで幾多の<line-break>精霊を苦しめて来たか、<line-break>わからない。<end>"
EN: "It won't be easy. I couldn't begin to say how many spirits I've caused to suffer over the years.<end>"
KO: "쉬운 일은 아니겠지. 나 자신도 지금까지 얼마나 많은 정령을 괴롭혀 왔는지 모르니까.<end>"

## 1380285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も同感だ。それに、残念なことには、<line-break>精霊との共存ということを<line-break>身をもって実践している人間は、<line-break>いまだシェスター<line-break>ただひとりしか知らない。<end>"
EN: "I agree. And regrettably, Shester remains the only person I know who truly puts coexistence with the spirits into practice.<end>"
KO: "나도 동감이다. 게다가 유감스럽게도 정령과의 공존을 몸소 실천하는 인간은 아직 셰스터 단 한 명밖에 모른다.<end>"

## 1380286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイ、やはり俺はもう部屋へ戻る。<end>"
EN: "Roy, I think I'll go back to my room after all.<end>"
KO: "로이, 역시 난 이제 방으로 돌아가겠다.<end>"

## 1380287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉のことを話題にされるのがつらいか？<end>"
EN: "Is it painful to hear us talk about your sister?<end>"
KO: "누나 이야기가 나오는 게 괴로운가?<end>"

## 1380288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう思いたくば、それでもいい。　<line-break><line-break><line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>おまえの力は役に立つはずだ。<line-break>他の無能な連中に比べれば、だがな。<end>"
EN: "If that's what you want to think, so be it. <value:$28>, your power should prove useful. Compared with those other incompetents, at least.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 생각하고 싶다면 그래도 좋다. <value:$28>. 네 힘은 도움이 될 거야. 다른 무능한 녀석들에 비하면 말이지.<end>"

## 1380289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうものの言い方は角が<line-break>立つと、昔から言ってるだろう。<end>"
EN: "I've told you for years that putting things that way only rubs people the wrong way.<end>"
KO: "그런 식으로 말하면 괜히 각이 선다고 옛날부터 말했잖아.<end>"

## 1380290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、事実なのだから仕方なかろう。<line-break>じゃあな、また明日だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. It's the truth, so what else am I supposed to say? See you tomorrow.<end>"
KO: "흥, 사실이니 어쩔 수 없지. 그럼 또 내일 보자.<end>"

## 1380291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "素直じゃないな、まったく。　<line-break>だが、奴の言ったことは<line-break>当たっていると思うよ。<line-break>私も、<value:$28>には、<line-break>何かを変える力を感じるんだ。<end>"
EN: "He can never bring himself to say what he means, can he? Still, I think he was right. I sense in you the power to change something too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "참 솔직하지 못하군. 하지만 녀석 말이 맞다고 생각해. 나도 <value:$28>에게 뭔가를 바꾸는 힘이 느껴져.<end>"

## 1380292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そうだな。<line-break>こんなことを言われるのは、　　　<line-break>確かに重圧だろうな。<line-break><value:$28>は今までどおりの<line-break><value:$28>でいればいい。<line-break>それが皆にとって力になるんだから。<end>"
EN: "Heh, that's true. Hearing all this must put quite a burden on you. <value:$28>, all you need to do is go on being the same <value:$28> you've always been. That alone gives everyone strength.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇지. 이런 말을 들으면 확실히 부담이겠군. <value:$28>은 지금까지처럼 <value:$28>이면 돼. 그게 모두에게 힘이 되니까.<end>"

## 1380293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、ありのままでいいんだ。<line-break>シェスターの夢の実現も、そう遠い<line-break>未来ではないような気がしてくるよ。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. Just be yourself. It makes me feel that the day Shester's dream comes true may not be so far off after all.<end>"
KO: "그래, 있는 그대로면 돼. 셰스터의 꿈이 이루어지는 것도 그리 먼 미래가 아닌 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1380294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "明日からまたお互い力を尽くそう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Starting tomorrow, let's both do all we can, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "내일부터 또 서로 힘내자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380300

## 1380301

## 1380304

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、お腹ぺこぺこ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>この家からいいにおいがするよぅ。<line-break>おじゃましてみようかなぁ。<end>"
EN: "I'm starving. Something smells really good coming from this house. Maybe I'll stop by.<end>"
KO: "나 배고파 죽겠어. 이 집에서 좋은 냄새가 나네. 한번 들어가 볼까.<end>"

## 1380315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。今年の野菜のできはいいぞ。<line-break>やはり、肥料を変えたのは正解だった。<line-break> <line-break>どんな肥料を使ったかって？<line-break>そいつは教えてやれないなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. The vegetables are looking good this year. Changing the fertilizer was definitely the right choice. What kind did I use? Ah, I can't tell you that.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 올해 채소는 잘 자랐군. 역시 비료를 바꾼 게 정답이었어. 무슨 비료를 썼냐고? 그건 알려줄 수 없지.<end>"

## 1380316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぁー。<line-break>退屈だなぁ。<line-break>なにかおもしろいことないかい？<end>"
EN: "Yaaawn. I'm bored. Isn't there anything interesting going on?<end>"
KO: "후아아. 지루하네. 뭔가 재미있는 일 없을까?<end>"

## 1380317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml

## 1380318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふー、やっと着いた。<line-break>毎回、この森を抜けるのは疲れるよ。<line-break>魔物も多いしね。<end>"
EN: "Phew, finally made it. Getting through this forest wears me out every time. So many monsters, too.<end>"
KO: "후우, 드디어 도착했다. 매번 이 숲을 빠져나오는 건 지치네. 마물도 많고.<end>"

## 1380340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0おーい。<line-break>こんなとこで寝てると<line-break>風邪ひいちゃうよ。<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1おーい。<line-break>こんなとこで寝てると<line-break>危険が一杯だよ。女の子なんだから。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey! You'll catch cold sleeping out here.<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1Hey! It's dangerous sleeping out here, especially for a girl.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0이봐. 이런 데서 자면감기 걸려.<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1이봐. 이런 데서 자면위험한 일이 많아. 여자애잖아.<end>"

## 1380341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここ？　ロストール。王国の都だよ。<line-break>なにかワケがありそうだね？<line-break>君の身に何があったんだい？<end>"
EN: "Here? This is Rostorl, the royal capital. It sounds like there's a story behind this. What happened to you?<end>"
KO: "여기? 로스톨. 왕국의 수도야. 뭔가 사정이 있는 것 같네? 네게 무슨 일이 있었어?<end>"

## 1380342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "復讐？　魔人？　故郷？<line-break>竜王の目覚め？<line-break>無限のソウル？<end>"
EN: "Revenge? Demons? Your homeland? The Dragon King's awakening? The Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "복수? 마인? 고향? 용왕의 각성? 무한의 소울?<end>"

## 1380343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "す、すごいや！？<line-break>ねぇ、ぼくと一緒に旅をしようよ。<line-break>こう見えても冒険歴は長いんだ。<line-break>きっと、君の力になれるよ。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's incredible! Hey, why don't you travel with me? I may not look it, but I've been adventuring a long time. I'm sure I can help you.<end>"
KO: "와, 대단하다!? 있지, 나랑 같이 여행하자. 이래 봬도 모험 경력은 길어. 분명 네 힘이 될 수 있어.<end>"

## 1380344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やったぁ！<line-break>君…冒険者じゃないよね。<line-break>なら、冒険者登録しないと。<end>"
EN: "Yes! You're not an adventurer, are you? Then we'll have to get you registered.<end>"
KO: "됐다! 너… 모험자는 아니지? 그럼 모험자 등록부터 해야 해.<end>"

## 1380345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "犯罪登録なし。幻術計振れておらず。<line-break>…はい、あなたは今日から<line-break>冒険者として登録されました。<line-break>がんばって仕事してくれよ。<line-break>有名になったら、そのギルド登録を<line-break>したのは俺だって鼻が高いからね。<end>"
EN: "No criminal record. No reaction from the illusion gauge. Right, as of today, you're officially registered as an adventurer. Work hard out there. If you become famous, I'll be proud to say I was the one who registered you with the Guild.<end>"
KO: "범죄 기록 없음. 환술계 수치 이상 없음. …좋아, 오늘부터 당신은 모험가로 등록됐습니다. 열심히 일해 주세요. 유명해지면 그 길드 등록을 내가 해 줬다고 자랑할 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1380346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！　クリスピー…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right! Crispy...<end>"
KO: "그래! 크리스피….<end>"

## 1380347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁぁぁ！<end>"
EN: "Waaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으아아아!<end>"

## 1380348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ、自己紹介を忘れてた。<line-break>ぼくはレルラ＝ロントン。<line-break><line-break>キレイを探して旅をする<line-break>さすらいの吟遊詩人だよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I forgot to introduce myself. I'm Relra-Lonton, a wandering bard traveling the world in search of beauty.<end>"
KO: "아, 자기소개를 깜빡했네. 나는 렐라=론톤. 아름다움을 찾아 여행하는 방랑 음유시인이야.<end>"

## 1380349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで…なに？<end>"
EN: "So... what is it?<end>"
KO: "그래서… 뭐?<end>"

## 1380350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、その名前で呼んで悪かったよ。<line-break>恐い顔するなよ。<line-break>いい話なんだからよ。<line-break>お前さんが言ってた猫屋敷、<line-break>やっぱり、賢者の森の奥にあるらしい。<line-break>もう一回探して見るかい？<end>"
EN: "S-sorry I called you that. Don't look at me like that. I've got good news. That Cat Manor you mentioned really does seem to be deep in Sage's Forest. Want to look for it again?<end>"
KO: "그, 그 이름으로 불러서 미안해. 무서운 얼굴 하지 마. 좋은 이야기니까. 네가 말했던 고양이 저택, 역시 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있는 모양이야. 한 번 더 찾아볼래?<end>"

## 1380351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん！<line-break><value:$28>となら<line-break>猫屋敷にだっていけそうな気がする。<line-break>ぼくのこういう予感は当たるんだ。<line-break>じゃあ、さっそく出発進行！<end>"
EN: "Of course! With <value:$28>, I feel like I could even reach Cat Manor. My hunches about these things are always right. So let's get going!<end>"
KO: "물론이지! <value:$28>과 함께라면 고양이 저택에도 갈 수 있을 것 같아. 내 이런 예감은 잘 맞거든. 그럼 바로 출발!<end>"

## 1380352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、ここが猫屋敷？<end>"
EN: "I-is this Cat Manor?<end>"
KO: "여, 여기가 고양이 저택?<end>"

## 1380353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？<line-break>お客さんとは珍しい。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Visitors are a rare sight.<end>"
KO: "어라? 손님이라니 드문 일이군.<end>"

## 1380354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あなたが森の賢者様？<end>"
EN: "A-are you the Sage of the Forest?<end>"
KO: "다, 당신이 숲의 현자님?<end>"

## 1380355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、そんな風に私のことを　　<line-break>呼ぶ人もいるようですね。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Some people do call me that.<end>"
KO: "후후, 저를 그렇게 부르는 사람도 있는 모양이네요.<end>"

## 1380356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？<line-break>あなたは…闇の…<end>いえ？　無限のソウル？<end>"
EN: "Oh? You are... of darkness...<end>No... the Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "어라? 당신은… 어둠의…<end>아니? 무한의 소울?<end>"

## 1380357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、なにか事情がありそうですね？<line-break>今日は泊まっていかれてはいかがです？<end>"
EN: "It seems there is more to your story. Why not stay here tonight?<end>"
KO: "아무래도 무슨 사정이 있는 듯하군요? 오늘은 묵고 가시는 게 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1380358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！　いいんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? Are you sure?<end>"
KO: "어! 정말 괜찮은 건가요?<end>"

## 1380359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構いませんとも。<line-break>たいしたおもてなしもできませんがね。<end>"
EN: "Of course. I cannot offer you much in the way of hospitality, though.<end>"
KO: "물론 괜찮습니다. 대단한 대접은 못 해 드리지만요.<end>"

## 1380360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Then we'll take you up on that. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1380361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>そんなことがあったんですか…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So that is what happened...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그런 일이 있었습니까….<end>"

## 1380362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士、<line-break>告げる者アスティアと、<line-break>開け放つものヴァシュタール。<line-break>それに無限のソウルを持つ者。<line-break>運命は…時として皮肉ですね。<end>"
EN: "Astia, the One Who Proclaims, and Vashtar, the One Who Opens: Knights of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction. And the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Fate can be ironic at times.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사, 고하는 자 아스티아와 열어젖히는 자 바슈타르. 그리고 무한의 소울을 지닌 자. 운명은… 때로 얄궂군요.<end>"

## 1380363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の母さんだって、<line-break>円卓の騎士だから…<line-break> <line-break>そう簡単にやられたりしないはずだよ。<line-break>きっと生きてる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>'s mother is a Knight of the Round Table too. She couldn't have been killed that easily. I'm sure she's alive.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 어머니도 원탁기사니까… 그렇게 쉽게 당하진 않았을 거야. 분명 살아 있어.<end>"

## 1380364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっ、どうだかな！<end>"
EN: "Hah! I wouldn't count on it!<end>"
KO: "흥, 글쎄다!<end>"

## 1380365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ！　スゴイや！<line-break>ぼく、しゃべる猫なんて初めてだよ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa! Amazing! I've never seen a talking cat before!<end>"
KO: "와! 대단해! 나 말하는 고양이는 처음 봐!<end>"

## 1380366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は猫じゃない、ネモだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not a cat. I'm Nemo.<end>"
KO: "난 고양이가 아니야, 네모다.<end>"

## 1380367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアや、ヴァシュタールと同じ<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士、ネモ！<end>"
EN: "Nemo, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction, just like Astia and Vashtar!<end>"
KO: "아스티아와 바슈타르와 같은 파괴신의 원탁기사, 네모!<end>"

## 1380368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、心配はいりません。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>昔、勇者ネメアに倒されてからは<line-break>猫の姿に封印されて、<line-break>今はなんの力も残っていませんから。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. There is no cause for concern. Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated him long ago, he has been sealed in the form of a cat. He has no power left now.<end>"
KO: "후후, 걱정하지 마세요. 옛날 용자 네메아에게 쓰러진 뒤로는 고양이 모습에 봉인되어 지금은 아무 힘도 남지 않았으니까요.<end>"

## 1380369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、すごいや！<line-break>猫の姿になってなお、人語を操れる<line-break>なんて…。<end>"
EN: "But that's still amazing! Even trapped in a cat's body, you can speak like a person.<end>"
KO: "그래도 대단해! 고양이 모습이 되고도 인간 말을 할 수 있다니….<end>"

## 1380370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "にしても、アスティアも<line-break>丸くなっちまったもんだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>頭も鈍ったみたいだしな。<line-break>同じ円卓騎士でも、ヴァシュタールと<line-break>アスティアじゃ実力の差があり過ぎだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, Astia's gone soft. Looks like her wits have dulled too. They may both be Knights of the Round Table, but there's a world of difference between Astia and Vashtar.<end>"
KO: "그래도 아스티아도 많이 둥글어졌군. 머리도 둔해진 것 같고. 같은 원탁기사라도 바슈탈과 아스티아는 실력 차이가 너무 커.<end>"

## 1380371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう考えたって、死んだぜ。<line-break>アスティアはよ。<end>"
EN: "No matter how you look at it, Astia's dead.<end>"
KO: "아무리 생각해도 죽었어. 아스티아는.<end>"

## 1380372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙りなさい、このバカネコ。<end>"
EN: "Be quiet, you stupid cat.<end>"
KO: "닥쳐, 이 바보 고양이.<end>"

## 1380373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと！<end>"
EN: "Oops!<end>"
KO: "어이쿠!<end>"

## 1380374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいませんね、<value:$28>。<line-break>何せ、気の利かないネコでね。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, <value:$28>. This cat has no tact whatsoever.<end>"
KO: "미안하네요, <value:$28>. 워낙 눈치 없는 고양이라서요.<end>"

## 1380375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアのことは<line-break>私の口からはなんとも…。<line-break>　<line-break>もう遅いですから、<line-break>今日はこれくらいにして、<line-break>続きは明日にしましょう。<end>"
EN: "I cannot say anything certain about Astia. It is late, so let us leave matters there for tonight and continue tomorrow.<end>"
KO: "아스티아에 대해서는 제 입으로는 뭐라…. 시간이 늦었으니 오늘은 이쯤 하고, 나머지는 내일 이야기하죠.<end>"

## 1380376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね。<line-break>今日は色々あったから<line-break>ぼくも疲れちゃった。<end>"
EN: "Good idea. So much happened today that I'm worn out too.<end>"
KO: "그러게. 오늘 여러 일이 있어서 나도 지쳤어.<end>"

## 1380377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>それでは、おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Good night, then.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그럼 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1380378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ。早いですね。<line-break>昨日はよく眠れましたか？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You're up early. Did you sleep well?<end>"
KO: "후후. 빠르시네요. 어젯밤은 잘 주무셨습니까?<end>"

## 1380379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、昨日の話の続きをしても<line-break>かまいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Now then, may I continue our discussion from yesterday?<end>"
KO: "그럼 어제 이야기의 계속을 해도 괜찮겠습니까?<end>"

## 1380380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっそくですが、先日、ミイスの村が<line-break>焼き払われたことをご存じですか？<end>"
EN: "To come straight to the point, did you know that Miis Village was burned to the ground recently?<end>"
KO: "그럼 바로 묻죠. 얼마 전 미이스 마을이 불타 버린 건 알고 계십니까?<end>"

## 1380381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それって、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "You don't mean...<end>"
KO: "그건 설마….<end>"

## 1380382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。ことはミイスを焼き払った魔人<line-break>アーギルシャイアに関わります。<end>"
EN: "Yes. This concerns Argyleshire, the demon who burned Miis to the ground.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니다. 미이스를 불태운 마인 아르길샤이어와 관련된 일입니다.<end>"

## 1380383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイア…？<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire...?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어…?<end>"

## 1380384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアやヴァシュタールと同じ、<line-break>破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士のひとりです。<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器を１つ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Like Astia and Vashtar, she is one of the Knights of the Round Table serving Ulugh, the God of Destruction. She is a bewitching beauty, cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground for the sake of obtaining a single Dark Artifact.<end>"
KO: "아스티아나 바슈타르와 같은 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 하나입니다. 요염한 미녀이고 잔혹하며 변덕스럽죠. 어둠의 신기 하나를 얻기 위해 미이스를 불태워 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 1380385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器？<end>"
EN: "A Dark Artifact?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기?<end>"

## 1380386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強大で凶々しい力を持つ<line-break>１２の魔道器のことです。<line-break>　<line-break>かつて、破壊神ウルグの<line-break>復活に使われたと言われています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "They are twelve magical artifacts of immense and sinister power. It is said that they were once used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "강대하고 불길한 힘을 지닌 12개의 마도기입니다. 과거 파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰였다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1380387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、彼女は<line-break>その本来の使い方以外のことを　　<line-break>しようとしています。<line-break>彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り、<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を<line-break>破壊しようと考えているのです。<end>"
EN: "But she intends to use one for a different purpose. She plans to create the ultimate composite monster, then use its power to destroy the world for her own amusement.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그녀는 본래의 용도 외의 일을 하려 하고 있습니다. 그녀는 궁극의 합성괴물을 만들어 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려고 생각하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その合成怪物を作るための知識を、<line-break>闇の神器のひとつ、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようとしているのです。<line-break>あなた方には、アーギルシャイアの<line-break>手に渡る前に、その聖杯を<line-break>取り戻してもらいたいのです。<end>"
EN: "She hopes to obtain the knowledge needed to create that monster from the Forbidden Grail, one of the Dark Artifacts. I want you to recover the Grail before it falls into Argyleshire's hands.<end>"
KO: "그 합성괴물을 만들 지식을 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려는 겁니다. 여러분에게는 아르길샤이어 손에 넘어가기 전에 그 성배를 되찾아 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1380389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "取り戻す？<line-break>聖杯は別の誰かに<line-break>盗まれちゃったってこと？<end>"
EN: "Recover it? You mean someone else stole the Grail?<end>"
KO: "되찾는다고? 성배를 다른 누군가에게 도둑맞았다는 뜻이야?<end>"

## 1380391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひょっとして…<line-break>名前を知るもの、ネモ？<end>"
EN: "Could it be... Nemo, the Knower of Names?<end>"
KO: "혹시…이름을 아는 자, 네모?<end>"

## 1380392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすが詩人さんですね。<line-break>ええ、うちにいるネモですよ。<line-break>ご指摘どおり、聖杯は彼の物なのです。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は、<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<line-break>この際、神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from a bard. Yes, I mean the Nemo who lives here. As you surmised, the Grail belongs to him. When he learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he decided to keep his Artifact out of her hands and, while he was at it, use its power to regain his original form...<end>"
KO: "역시 시인분이시군요. 네, 우리 집에 있는 네모입니다. 지적하신 대로 성배는 그의 것입니다. 아르길샤이어의 속셈을 안 그는 자기 신기를 빼앗기고 싶지 않아, 이참에 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가려…<end>"

## 1380393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３匹のゴブリンたちを使って、<line-break>ロストールから神器を盗み出させ、<line-break>自分の所に持ってこさせたのです。<end>"
EN: "He had three Goblins steal the Artifact from Rostorl and bring it to him.<end>"
KO: "세 마리 고블린을 이용해 로스톨에서 신기를 훔쳐 자기에게 가져오게 한 겁니다.<end>"

## 1380394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ネモは猫のまま…<line-break>つまり、失敗した？<end>"
EN: "But Nemo is still a cat... So it didn't work?<end>"
KO: "그런데 네모는 여전히 고양이 모습…. 그러니까 실패한 거야?<end>"

## 1380395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯は持ち主に英知を与えます。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>知恵を身につけたゴブリンたちは、<line-break>ネモを見限ってしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail grants wisdom to its possessor. Once the Goblins gained intelligence, they abandoned Nemo.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 소유자에게 지혜를 줍니다. 지혜를 얻은 고블린들은 네모를 버린 것입니다.<end>"

## 1380396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まるで喜劇だね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's like something out of a comedy, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "마치 희극 같네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1380397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、おかげで、ロストールは<line-break>焼き払われずにすみましたがね。<end>"
EN: "Well, at least it spared Rostorl from being burned to the ground.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 덕분에 로스톨은 불타 없어지지 않았지만요.<end>"

## 1380398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あなたは、<line-break>魔人の恐ろしさを知っているはずです。<line-break><line-break>このまま、アーギルシャイアを<line-break>放っておくと、あなたのように<line-break>大事な人を失う人間がたくさん出ます。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you should know how terrifying demons can be. If Argyleshire is left unchecked, many people will lose someone dear to them, just as you did.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 당신은 마인의 무서움을 알고 있을 겁니다. 이대로 아르길샤이어를 내버려 두면 당신처럼 소중한 사람을 잃는 사람이 많이 생깁니다.<end>"

## 1380399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人と戦えとは言いません。<line-break>彼女の思惑を阻止する…つまり、<line-break>聖杯を先に入手してもらいたいのです。<end>"
EN: "I am not asking you to fight a demon. I only want you to thwart her plans―in other words, obtain the Grail before she does.<end>"
KO: "마인과 싸우라고 하지는 않겠습니다. 그녀의 계획을 막는 것… 즉 성배를 먼저 손에 넣어 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1380400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。よろしく頼みます。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "예. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1380401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、まずは<line-break>そのゴブリンを見つけないと…<line-break>行き先とか心当たりは？<end>"
EN: "Then first we have to find those Goblins. Any idea where they went?<end>"
KO: "그럼 우선그 고블린을 찾아야겠네…어디로 갔는지 짐작 가는 데는?<end>"

## 1380402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンは聖杯の力で人語を話し、<line-break>ゴブゴブ団と名乗ってます。<line-break> <line-break>ネモの話だと、<line-break>エンシャントにいるということでしたが<line-break>急がないと、移動してしまうでしょう。<end>"
EN: "The Grail has given the Goblins the power of human speech, and they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. According to Nemo, they are in Ancient, but if you do not hurry, they may move on.<end>"
KO: "고블린들은 성배의 힘으로 인간의 말을 하고 고브고브단이라 자칭하고 있습니다. 네모 말로는 엔샨트에 있다지만 서두르지 않으면 이동해 버리겠지요.<end>"

## 1380403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人語をしゃべるゴブリン…<line-break>早く会ってみたいな。<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Goblins who can talk... I can't wait to meet them. Come on, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "사람 말을 하는 고블린… 빨리 만나보고 싶어. 그렇지, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1380404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その前に説明したいものがあります。<line-break>こちらに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Before you go, there is something I would like to show you. Come this way.<end>"
KO: "그 전에 설명하고 싶은 게 있습니다. 이쪽으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1380405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん、これは？<end>"
EN: "Orphaus, what's this?<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 이건?<end>"

## 1380406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、これは転送機といいます。<line-break>使ってみましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. This is called a teleportation device. Shall we try it?<end>"
KO: "후후, 이건 전송기라고 합니다. 한번 써 볼까요?<end>"

## 1380407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何しやがる！<line-break>俺を実験台にするな！<end>"
EN: "What the hell are you doing? Don't use me as a test subject!<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야! 날 실험대로 쓰지 마!<end>"

## 1380408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "す、すごいや！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's incredible!<end>"
KO: "대, 대단해!<end>"

## 1380409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じで、<line-break>これは遠くの人を呼び寄せたり…。<line-break>　<line-break>さて、それでは、もう一度。<end>"
EN: "As you can see, it can summon someone from far away. Now then, once more.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 식으로 멀리 있는 사람을 불러오기도 하고…. 자, 그럼 다시 한번.<end>"

## 1380410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら！<line-break>俺を実験台に…<end>"
EN: "Hey! Don't use me as a test subject―<end>"
KO: "야! 나를 실험대로 삼아서….<end>"

## 1380411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな感じで、ここにいる人を<line-break>遠くに送り込んだりできます。<end>"
EN: "And like so, it can send someone who is here to a distant location.<end>"
KO: "이런 식으로 여기 있는 사람을 먼 곳으로 보내기도 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごい、すごいや！<end>"
EN: "Amazing! That's incredible!<end>"
KO: "대단해, 정말 대단해!<end>"

## 1380414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後は、パーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから、<line-break>そのあたりのことはご心配なく。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and from now on, I will obtain your party members' permission before summoning them, so you need not worry about that.<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버들의 동의를 받은 뒤에 부르겠습니다. 그 부분은 걱정하지 않으셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 1380415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんなところですかね？<end>"
EN: "I believe that covers everything.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이 정도인가요?<end>"

## 1380416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん。<line-break>本当にお世話になりました。<line-break> <line-break>す、すごい冒険だよ。<line-break>さっそく出発しよう！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Orphaus, thank you for everything. This is going to be an incredible adventure! Let's set off right away, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨. 정말 신세 많이 졌습니다. 굉장한 모험이야. 바로 출발하자! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1380418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界を巡って、人に出会い、<line-break>仲間を作ってください。<line-break>　<line-break>その中で世界は<line-break>様々な顔を見せてくれるでしょう。<line-break>自由な旅を！<end>"
EN: "Travel the world, meet people, and find companions. Along the way, the world will show you its many faces. Travel freely!<end>"
KO: "세계를 돌아다니며 사람을 만나고 동료를 만드세요. 그 속에서 세계는 여러 얼굴을 보여 줄 겁니다. 자유로운 여행을!<end>"

## 1380419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml

## 1380421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<line-break>しかし、伯爵様、よろしいのですか？<line-break>お兄君のエリエナイ公は…<line-break>い、いえ、出過ぎだ発言でした。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass. But, my lord Count, are you certain? Your elder brother, Duke Elienai... N-no, forgive me. I spoke out of turn.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 들어가십시오. 하지만 백작님, 괜찮으십니까? 형님이신 에리에나이 공은…. 아, 아닙니다. 제가 주제넘은 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 1380422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1380423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様に会うのか。<line-break>よかろう、話は承っている。<line-break>通るとよい。<end>"
EN: "You seek an audience with Her Majesty? Very well. I have been informed. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가는가. 좋다, 이야기는 들었다. 지나가라.<end>"

## 1380424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<line-break>なに、総司令閣下の書状が…。<line-break>あ、それなら、承っております。<line-break>どうぞ、ご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? She is busy. Now off with you! What? You have a letter from the Commander-in-Chief? Ah, yes, we were told to expect you. Allow me to escort you.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 뵙고 싶다고? 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가라! 뭐, 총사령관 각하의 서신이 있다고…. 아, 그거라면 말씀 들었습니다. 이쪽으로 오십시오.<end>"

## 1380425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様はお忙しく、<line-break>どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<line-break>は？　ファーロス総司令閣下の書状…。<line-break>あ！　そういえば…。失礼しました！<line-break>それではご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave. What? A letter from Commander-in-Chief Pharos? Ah! Now that you mention it... Forgive me! I will escort you inside.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구와도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오…. 네? 파로스 총사령관 각하의 서신…. 아! 그러고 보니…. 실례했습니다! 그럼 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1380426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? She is busy. Now off with you!<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 뵙고 싶다고? 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가! 돌아가!<end>"

## 1380427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様はお忙しく、<line-break>どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구와도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 1380428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た、フリントの息子<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>今日は何用か？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>, son of Flint. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, 플린트의 아들 <value:$28>이여. 오늘은 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 1380429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにごとだ。<line-break>今は、王宮は大変なのだ。<line-break>用なら、あとにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? The palace has enough to deal with right now. If you have business here, it will have to wait.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이냐. 지금 왕궁은 매우 바쁘다. 볼일이라면 나중에 와라.<end>"

## 1380430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ファーロス総司令が貴族を召集しています。<line-break>どうぞ、謁見の間へ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Commander-in-Chief Pharos is summoning the nobles. Please proceed to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 파로스 총사령관께서 귀족들을 소집하고 계십니다. 알현실로 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1380431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間へ行く<end>"
EN: "Go to the audience chamber<end>"
KO: "알현실로 간다<end>"

## 1380432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間へ行かない<end>"
EN: "Don't go to the audience chamber<end>"
KO: "알현실에는 가지 않는다<end>"

## 1380433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、どうぞ<line-break>お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Very well. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "그럼 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1380434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<end>"
EN: "...I see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1380435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王宮に何か用かね？<line-break>王宮に用事のない者がうろつくのは<line-break>感心できぬな。<end>"
EN: "Do you have business at Rostorl Palace? I cannot approve of people loitering here without cause.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕궁에 무슨 용무인가? 왕궁에 볼일 없는 자가 어슬렁거리는 건 좋지 않군.<end>"

## 1380436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、王宮内の雰囲気が変わった…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>あんなことがあったから、<line-break>当然のことかもしれないが…。<end>"
EN: "The mood inside the palace has changed lately. After what happened, perhaps that is only natural.<end>"
KO: "최근 왕궁 안 분위기가 달라졌어…. 그런 일이 있었으니 당연할지도 모르지만….<end>"

## 1380437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>恐れながら、お通しできません。<line-break>陛下はご気分が優れないとかで…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I regret that I cannot let you pass. I am told that Her Majesty is unwell.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 송구하지만 들여보내 드릴 수 없습니다. 폐하께서 몸이 편찮으시다고….<end>"

## 1380438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女王陛下が即位なされ、<line-break>これからはよいことが<line-break>続くとよいのですが。<end>"
EN: "Now that Her Majesty the Queen has ascended the throne, I only hope better days lie ahead.<end>"
KO: "여왕 폐하께서 즉위하셨으니 앞으로는 좋은 일이 계속되면 좋겠군요.<end>"

## 1380439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮の怪異を調べに？<line-break>しかし…<line-break>陛下からは誰も通すなと…。<end>"
EN: "You are here to investigate the strange occurrences in the palace? But Her Majesty ordered us not to let anyone through.<end>"
KO: "왕궁의 괴이를 조사하러? 하지만… 폐하께선 아무도 들이지 말라고…<end>"

## 1380440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説得を続ける<end>"
EN: "Keep making your case<end>"
KO: "설득을 계속한다<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380442

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그, 그럴 리가!? 그건 그냥 소문 아니었어!?<end>"

## 1380445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml

## 1380447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380448

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダム解放軍への援助要請か。<line-break>あいにくと事態はさほど悠長ではない。<line-break>あまり興味はないな。<end>"
EN: "You have come to request aid for the Liberdam Liberation Army? I am afraid matters are far too urgent for that. It holds little interest for me.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 해방군에 대한 지원 요청인가. 유감이지만 상황은 그렇게 한가하지 않아. 별로 관심 없군.<end>"

## 1380471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それよりも、ものは相談だが、<line-break>次の戦に参戦してはくれぬか？<line-break>カルラ進軍のこと、聞いていよう？<line-break>そなたが、こちらの言うことを<line-break>聞いてくれるなら、こちらも<line-break>援助の件、引き受けよう。いかがか？<end>"
EN: "Instead, I have a proposal for you. Will you join us in the coming battle? You have heard of Karula's Advance, I presume. Agree to our request, and I shall grant yours and provide the aid you seek. What say you?<end>"
KO: "그보다 상담할 게 있는데 다음 전투에 참가해 주지 않겠나? 카를라 진군 이야기는 들었겠지? 네가 우리 말을 들어준다면 우리도 지원 건을 받아들이겠다. 어떤가?<end>"

## 1380472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1380473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1380474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、引き受けてくれるか。　<line-break>ありがたい。<line-break>今回の戦、かなり分が悪くてな。<line-break>その上、レムオン坊やが、<line-break>貴族をまとめて、今回の戦には<line-break>参加せぬと申しておる。<end>"
EN: "So you will accept? You have my thanks. The odds are very much against us in this battle. To make matters worse, young Lemghon has rallied the nobles and declared that they will take no part in it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 맡아 주겠나. 고맙군. 이번 전투는 꽤 불리해서 말이야. 게다가 레무온 도련님이 귀족들을 모아 이번 전쟁엔 참가하지 않겠다고 하고 있어.<end>"

## 1380475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆえに、ファーロス家の軍だけで<line-break>立ち向かう。しかし、将星も乏しい。<line-break>そなたのような人材を欲していた。<line-break>頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>我が甥、ゼネテスの力になってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thus, the forces of House Pharos must face the enemy alone, and we have precious few capable commanders. We need someone of your ability. I am counting on you, <value:$28>. Lend your strength to my nephew Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "그러므로 파로스 가문의 군대만으로 맞선다. 하지만 장수도 부족하다. 너 같은 인재가 필요했다. 부탁한다, <value:$28>. 내 조카 제네테스에게 힘이 되어 다오.<end>"

## 1380476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、残念だな。<line-break>それならば、こちらも援助できぬ。<line-break>下がるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. That is unfortunate. In that case, we cannot provide aid either. You may go.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 유감이군. 그렇다면 이쪽도 지원할 수 없다. 물러가라.<end>"

## 1380477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、あの神速の戦術家カルラが<line-break>勇猛なディンガル兵をわんさか連れて<line-break>こっちに向かってるそうだ。<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんとの戦いで<line-break>こっちは兵力をあらかた失ってる上に<line-break>レムオンのダンナがな…。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Word is Karula, that lightning-fast tactician, is headed this way with a whole horde of fierce Dyneskal soldiers. We already lost most of our forces fighting old Angeerdan, and now Lord Lemghon has gone and...<end>"
KO: "이봐, 그 신속의 전략가 카를라가 용맹한 딘갈 병사들을 잔뜩 끌고 이쪽으로 온다는군. 앙길단 영감과의 싸움으로 우린 병력을 거의 다 잃은 데다 레무온 나리까지….<end>"

## 1380478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族を集めて、兵を出さねえってさ。<line-break>んで、俺はファーロス家の兵だけで<line-break>カルラを迎え撃つことにした。<line-break>状況は最悪だ…つっても、<line-break>もっと悪くなるかもしれねえから<line-break>最悪じゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "Got the nobles together and says they won't send any troops. So I've decided to meet Karula with only the soldiers of House Pharos. Things are as bad as they can get... though they might still get worse, so I guess they're not.<end>"
KO: "귀족들을 모아도 병력을 안 낸대. 그래서 나는 파로스 가문의 병사들만으로 카를라를 맞기로 했어. 상황은 최악이야… 라고 해도 더 나빠질 수 있으니 최악은 아닌가.<end>"

## 1380491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、レルラのハープを<line-break>売らずにいてくれたじゃない。<line-break>お金はいくらあったって足りないのに。<line-break>フゴーが広告出してたの、見たよ。<line-break>買い取りたいって。<line-break>それも結構なお値段でさ。<end>"
EN: "You didn't sell Relra's Harp, even though no amount of money ever seems enough. I saw Hugo's advertisement. He said he wanted to buy it, and he was offering quite a price, too.<end>"
KO: "렐라의 하프를 팔지 않고 있어 줬잖아. 돈은 아무리 많아도 부족할 텐데. 휴고가 광고 낸 거 봤어. 사고 싶다고. 그것도 꽤 높은 값으로.<end>"

## 1380492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、うれしいなあ。<line-break>ぼくがとっておきたいって言ったの、<line-break>ちゃんと覚えていてくれたんだね。<line-break>やさしいなあ、<value:$28>は。<line-break>気まぐれなリルビーの言うことなんて<line-break>人間は本気にしてくれないと思ってた。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, I'm so happy. You remembered that I wanted you to keep it. You're so kind, <value:$28>. I never thought a human would take anything a fickle Lilubee said seriously.<end>"
KO: "후후, 기쁘다. 내가 갖고 싶다고 했던 거, 제대로 기억해 줬구나. 상냥하네, <value:$28>는. 변덕스러운 릴비의 말 같은 건 인간이 진지하게 받아들이지 않을 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 1380493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レルラのハープ、<line-break>売ってもいいよ。<end>"
EN: "You can sell Relra's Harp.<end>"
KO: "레르라의 하프, 팔아도 돼.<end>"

## 1380494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのときには、も～っとすばらしい<line-break>レルラ＝ロントンのハープが<line-break>ぼくの手にはあるはずだから。<line-break>とぉ～ってもやさしい<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主の歌の作者である<line-break>偉大な詩人の楽器がね！<end>"
EN: "Because by then, I should have an even more magnificent harp in my hands: Relra-Lonton's harp! The instrument of the great poet who wrote the song of the ever-so-kind bearer of the Infinite Soul!<end>"
KO: "그때쯤이면 훨씬 더 멋진 렐라=론톤의 하프가 내 손에 있을 테니까. 아주아주 상냥한 무한의 소울 소유자의 노래를 만든 위대한 시인의 악기 말이야!<end>"

## 1380495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のぼくには<line-break>レルラのハープを奏でる資格は<line-break>まだまだないなあって思ってさ。<line-break>そして、ぼくにその資格ができたとき、<line-break>ぼくにはレルラのハープは<line-break>もう、いらないと思うから。<end>"
EN: "I've realized I'm still nowhere near worthy of playing Relra's Harp. And by the time I am worthy, I don't think I'll need it anymore.<end>"
KO: "지금의 나한테는 렐라의 하프를 연주할 자격이 아직 멀었다고 생각했어. 그리고 내가 그 자격을 갖게 될 때쯤이면 렐라의 하프는 더 이상 필요 없을 것 같아서.<end>"

## 1380496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（早く師匠のところに<line-break>　向かわなければ…。）<end>"
EN: "(I have to get to my master, quickly...)<end>"
KO: "(빨리 스승님께 가야 해….)<end>"

## 1380498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<line-break><value:$28>さん…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨….<end>"

## 1380499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ、<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>いたじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Of course! You're here, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "맞아, <value:$28> 씨가 있었잖아.<end>"

## 1380500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、実は私、これから<line-break>酒場の用事でお使いに<line-break>行かなければならないんです。<line-break>この先のスラムにある<line-break>酒場なのですが…。<end>"
EN: "Well, I actually have to run an errand for the tavern. I need to go to a tavern in the Slums up ahead...<end>"
KO: "저기, 사실 저는 지금부터 술집 심부름을 가야 합니다. 이 앞 빈민가에 있는 술집인데요….<end>"

## 1380501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、そうですね。<line-break>お酒を飲まれない<line-break><value:$28>さんには<line-break>あんまりなじみが<line-break>ないかもしれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, I suppose so. You don't drink, <value:$28>, so you may not know the place.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그렇네요. 술을 마시지 않는 <value:$28> 씨에겐 그다지 익숙하지 않을지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1380502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スラムには、私が働いている<line-break>この酒場とは別にもう一軒、<line-break>酒場があるんです。<line-break>今日はそこへ届けものを<line-break>しなくちゃならないんですが…<line-break>ちょっと怖くて。<end>"
EN: "There's another tavern in the Slums, apart from the one where I work. I have to deliver something there today, but... I'm a little frightened.<end>"
KO: "빈민가에는 제가 일하는 이 술집 말고도 한 곳 더 술집이 있어요. 오늘은 거기에 물건을 전해 줘야 하는데… 조금 무서워서요.<end>"

## 1380503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、このロストールでも、<line-break>女の人を狙う<line-break>変質者が出るらしいんです。<line-break><value:$28>さんは、<line-break>冒険者志望でしたよね？<end>"
EN: "They say there's been a pervert preying on women here in Rostorl lately. You wanted to become an adventurer, didn't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "최근 이 로스톨에도 여성을 노리는 변태가 나타난다고 해요. <value:$28>님은 모험자 지망생이셨죠?<end>"

## 1380504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、<line-break>スラムの酒場まで、<line-break>つきあってください。<end>"
EN: "Please, come with me as far as the tavern in the Slums.<end>"
KO: "부탁입니다, 슬럼가의 술집까지 함께 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1380505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった！<line-break>じゃ、よろしくお願いします！<end>"
EN: "That's a relief! Thank you. I'm counting on you!<end>"
KO: "다행이다! 그럼 잘 부탁드립니다!<end>"

## 1380506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうだったんですか。<line-break>オッシ先生と鍛冶屋さんで<line-break>酒代をワリカンに…。<line-break>ふふふ、確かに鍛冶屋さんも、<line-break>オッシ先生に負けないくらい<line-break>よくお飲みになりますからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see. Master Ossi and the blacksmith are splitting the drinks bill... Hee hee. The blacksmith certainly drinks just as much as Master Ossi.<end>"
KO: "아, 그랬군요. 오시 선생님과 대장장이가 술값을 반씩…. 후후, 확실히 대장장이도 오시 선생님 못지않게 술을 잘 드시니까요.<end>"

## 1380507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃまず、<line-break>鍛冶屋さんに寄りましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>ご存知かもしれませんが、<line-break>この街の鍛冶屋さんは、<line-break>道具屋さんの地下にありますよ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's stop by the blacksmith's first. You may already know this, but the town smithy is beneath the item shop.<end>"
KO: "그럼 먼저 대장간에 들르죠. 아실지도 모르지만 이 도시 대장간은 도구점 지하에 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやじ、剣が古くなってきたから　　<line-break>新しいのを買いたいんだ。<line-break>いい剣はないか？<end>"
EN: "Old man, my sword's getting worn out, so I want to buy a new one. Got any good swords?<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 검이 낡아 와서 새 걸 사고 싶어. 좋은 검 없어?<end>"

## 1380509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、新米冒険者だね？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're a novice adventurer, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "당신, 신참 모험자지?<end>"

## 1380510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？<line-break>なんで新米だってわかったんだ？　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? How did you know I was a novice?<end>"
KO: "어? 내가 신참인 걸 어떻게 알았지?<end>"

## 1380511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器はな、新しく買うよりも、<line-break>強化していったほうが強くなるのさ。　<line-break>だから、そう思ったわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Weapons get stronger if you improve them instead of buying new ones. That's how I knew.<end>"
KO: "무기는 새로 사는 것보다 강화해 나가는 편이 더 강해져. 그래서 그렇게 생각한 거야.<end>"

## 1380512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なるほど。<line-break>それなら、強化してくれないか。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I-I see. Then would you improve mine for me?<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇군. 그렇다면 강화해 주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1380513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Improving a weapon takes Tempering Stone. Have you got any?<end>"
KO: "무기 강화에는 연강석이 필요한데, 가지고 있나?<end>"

## 1380514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그러니 연강석이 나는 산을 찾으면 몇 번쯤 찾아가 보는 게 좋겠지.<end>"

## 1380517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그렇군. 모험자는 뭐든 모험에서 시작한다는 거네. 그럼 아저씨, 연강석을 손에 넣으면 다시 올게.<end>"

## 1380518

## 1380519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰だって最初は新米だからな。<line-break>知ってる奴が、教えてやらんとな。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、高いとこから低いとこへ<line-break>水が流れてくのと同じ道理だわな。<line-break>はっはっは。<end>"
EN: "Everyone starts out a novice. Those who know have to teach those who don't. It's only natural, like water running downhill. Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "누구나 처음에는 신참이니까. 아는 사람이 가르쳐 줘야지. 높은 곳에서 낮은 곳으로 물이 흐르는 것과 같은 이치야. 하하하.<end>"

## 1380524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がはは、遠慮すんなって！<line-break>俺がその気じゃないときに来ても、<line-break>教えてやらねえぜ。<line-break>それに、覚えといてくれたほうが、<line-break>仕事を頼まれる側としても<line-break>話が早いってもんだ。<end>"

KO: "가하하, 사양하지 마! 내가 내킬 때 찾아오지 않으면 가르쳐 주지 않을 거라고. 게다가 기억해 두는 편이 일을 맡기는 쪽에서도 얘기가 빠르지.<end>"

## 1380533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "疲れたときはいつでも来るといい。<line-break>王宮気分…とはいかないが、<line-break> <line-break>うまい家庭料理と<line-break>ふかふかのベッドで歓迎するよ。<end>"

## 1380536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1380542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
