KO: "전부 이해하고 있다.<end>"

## 1380548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、さっきの新米の時も言ったがな、<line-break>武器や防具の頑丈さのことだわな。<line-break>　<line-break>金属って奴は、俺ら鍛冶屋が<line-break>ぶっ叩けば叩くほど、<line-break>ねばりが出て頑丈になりやがる。<end>"
EN: "Like I told that novice earlier, it's how sturdy a weapon or piece of armor is. The more we blacksmiths pound metal, the tougher and sturdier it gets.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아까 신참 때도 말했지만 무기나 방어구의 튼튼함 이야기야. 금속은 우리 대장장이가 두드리면 두드릴수록 질겨지고 단단해지지.<end>"

## 1380549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強度が高いほうが、<line-break>攻撃力や防御力が増すってのは<line-break>言うまでもねえ。<line-break>で、そこに、錬剛石って特別な鉱石を<line-break>あわせて叩き直す…言うなれば<line-break>ドワーフ流が今の主流だ。<end>"
EN: "Needless to say, greater strength means greater Attack Power or defense. The method most widely used now is what you might call the Dwarf style: reforging the piece together with a special ore called Tempering Stone.<end>"
KO: "강도가 높을수록 공격력과 방어력이 올라가는 건 말할 필요도 없지. 거기에 연강석이라는 특별한 광석을 섞어 다시 두드려 만드는… 이른바 드워프식이 지금의 주류야.<end>"

## 1380550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただし、鍛えれば鍛えるほど、<line-break>武器の重量が重くなって扱いが<line-break>難しくなることも知っておいてくれ。<line-break>ま、買ったままの武器でも<line-break>そこそこ戦えるようには鍛えてある。<end>"
EN: "But remember, the more you improve a weapon, the heavier it gets, and that can make it harder to handle. Mind you, even a weapon straight from the shop has been forged well enough to hold its own.<end>"
KO: "다만 단련할수록 무기 무게가 늘어서 다루기 어려워질 수도 있다는 건 알아 둬. 뭐, 산 그대로의 무기도 어느 정도 싸울 수 있게 단련돼 있어.<end>"

## 1380551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、さらに鍛えたかったら、<line-break>あとで錬剛石と代金をそろえてきな。<line-break>仕事は、保証するぜ。<end>"
EN: "If you want to improve it further, come back with Tempering Stone and the money. I guarantee my work.<end>"
KO: "그래도 더 단련하고 싶으면 나중에 연강석이랑 대금을 갖춰서 와. 솜씨는 보장하지.<end>"

## 1380552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、効果のことな。<line-break>お前さん、<line-break>ハチに刺されたことがあるだろ？<end>"
EN: "Ah, effects. You've been stung by a bee before, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "아, 효과 말이지. 너 벌에 쏘여 본 적 있지?<end>"

## 1380553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かゆくなったり痛くなったり…<line-break>そうなるのは、<line-break>ハチの針に毒があるからだな。<line-break>武器に効果をつけるってのは、<line-break>例えるなら、お前さんの剣が<line-break>ハチの針みてえになるってことよ。<end>"
EN: "It itches or hurts because a bee's stinger carries poison. Giving a weapon an effect is like turning your sword into a bee's stinger.<end>"
KO: "가렵거나 아프거나… 그런 건 벌침에 독이 있기 때문이야. 무기에 효과를 붙인다는 건 쉽게 말하면 네 검이 벌침처럼 되는 거지.<end>"

## 1380554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "刺したと同時に敵を眠らせちまう槍や、<line-break>斬ることで相手を麻痺させる剣、<line-break>なんてのも作れる。<line-break>防具はその逆だ。<line-break>石化や呪いからお前さんを<line-break>守ってくれる防具も作れる。<end>"
EN: "I can make a spear that puts an enemy to sleep when it strikes, or a sword that inflicts Paralysis when it cuts. Armor works the other way around. I can make armor that protects you from petrification or curses.<end>"
KO: "찌르는 동시에 적을 잠재우는 창이나, 베면 상대를 마비시키는 검 같은 것도 만들 수 있어. 방어구는 그 반대지. 석화나 저주로부터 너를 지켜 주는 방어구도 만들 수 있다.<end>"

## 1380555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつは難しい仕事だから、<line-break>それなりのギアをいただくが、<line-break>重宝するぜ。<line-break>強度や形状を変えてもまだ…<line-break>って時には、<line-break>ぜひ試してみてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "It's difficult work, so it'll cost you a fair amount of Gea, but it comes in handy. If changing the strength or shape still isn't enough, give it a try.<end>"
KO: "이건 어려운 일이니 그만큼 기어를 받지만 아주 쓸모 있어. 강도나 형상을 바꿔도 아직 부족하다 싶을 때 꼭 시험해 봐.<end>"

## 1380556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちにとっても、<line-break>いい稼ぎになるしな。<end>"
EN: "Makes us a tidy profit too.<end>"
KO: "우리한테도 좋은 벌이가 되니까.<end>"

## 1380557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははは！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "하하하!<end>"

## 1380558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ズバリ、エモノの形のことだな。<line-break>使ってる武器がいまいち好みじゃない<line-break>…そんな時は、鍛冶屋の出番だ。<line-break>代金さえもらえりゃ、ある程度、<line-break>お前さんの好みに従って<line-break>仕立て直すことが可能だ。<end>"
EN: "Simply put, it means the shape of your weapon. If the weapon you're using isn't quite to your liking, that's where a blacksmith comes in. Pay the price and I can reshape it to suit your tastes, within reason.<end>"
KO: "딱 잘라 말하면 무기의 모양 얘기야. 쓰는 무기가 영 마음에 안 든다… 그럴 땐 대장장이 차례지. 돈만 받으면 어느 정도는 네 취향에 맞춰 다시 손봐 줄 수 있어.<end>"

## 1380559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ剣にしても、もっと細身がいいとか<line-break>まっすぐなのがいいとか、あるだろ？<line-break>そういうことだよ。<line-break>もちろん、<line-break>昔の形に戻すことだってできるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Even with swords, you might want one slimmer or straighter, right? That's what I mean. And of course, I can restore its old shape too.<end>"
KO: "같은 검이라도 좀 더 가느다란 게 좋다거나 곧은 게 좋다거나 있잖아? 그런 거야. 물론 예전 형태로 되돌릴 수도 있어.<end>"

## 1380560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊ＬＶのことか。<line-break>確かにそれも装備品まわりの<line-break>ことではあるが、ちょいと…<line-break>それは鍛冶屋じゃなくて、<line-break>神殿の仕事なんでな。<end>"
EN: "You mean Spirit ＬＶ? It does concern equipment, but... that's temple work, not a blacksmith's.<end>"
KO: "정령 LV 말인가. 그것도 분명 장비 쪽 일이긴 하지만… 그건 대장간이 아니라 신전의 일이거든.<end>"

## 1380561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の出番ですね。<end>"
EN: "Then this is where I come in.<end>"
KO: "제 차례군요.<end>"

## 1380562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっ、ありがてえ。<line-break>神官さん、精霊属性のことを<line-break>説明してやってくださいよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, much obliged. Priest, would you explain Spirit Attributes?<end>"
KO: "오, 고맙군. 신관님, 정령 속성에 대해 설명해 주세요.<end>"

## 1380563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の杖の修理代、<line-break>安くしてくれるなら、<line-break>考えてもいいですよ。<end>"
EN: "I might consider it if you'll give me a discount on repairing my staff.<end>"
KO: "제 지팡이 수리비를 싸게 해 주신다면 생각해 볼게요.<end>"

## 1380564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うおっ、神様のしもべが俺の足元を！<line-break>天空神様へのお布施ってやつですかい？<end>"
EN: "Whoa! A servant of the gods, taking advantage of me! Is that what you call an offering to the Sky God?<end>"
KO: "우왓, 신의 하인이 내 발밑에! 천공신께 드리는 헌금이란 겁니까?<end>"

## 1380565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、冗談ですよ、冗談。<line-break>で、精霊属性のことですね？<line-break>　<line-break>装備品の精霊属性を変更する仕事は、<line-break>鍛冶屋ではなく、<line-break>神と人とをつなぐ神殿の領分なのです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, it was only a joke. Now, Spirit Attributes, was it? Altering the Spirit Attribute of equipment falls not to blacksmiths, but to the temples that serve as a bridge between gods and mortals.<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담입니다, 농담. 정령 속성 이야기죠? 장비의 정령 속성을 바꾸는 일은 대장간이 아니라 신과 사람을 잇는 신전의 영역입니다.<end>"

## 1380566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールには神殿がないからな。<line-break>ちなみにこの方はたまたま用事があって<line-break>エンシャントからいらっしゃってんだ。<end>"
EN: "There isn't a temple in Rostorl. This priest just happens to be visiting from Ancient on other business.<end>"
KO: "로스톨엔 신전이 없으니까. 참고로 이분은 마침 볼일이 있어서 엔샨트에서 오신 거야.<end>"

## 1380567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊属性の変更とは、すなわち、<line-break>火や水といった精霊の力を<line-break>武器や防具に宿すことを意味します。<line-break>精霊の属性については<line-break>もうご存知ですか？<end>"
EN: "Changing a Spirit Attribute means imbuing a weapon or piece of armor with the power of a spirit, such as fire or water. Are you already familiar with the Spirit Attributes?<end>"
KO: "정령 속성 변경이란 불이나 물 같은 정령의 힘을 무기나 방어구에 깃들이는 것을 뜻합니다. 정령의 속성은 이미 알고 계십니까?<end>"

## 1380568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火、水、土、風…<line-break>この四種類が精霊の属性です。<line-break>　<line-break>聖母神がお作りになったこの四属性が<line-break>互いに干渉しあい、循環することで<line-break>世界は成り立っています。<end>"
EN: "Fire, water, earth, and wind: these are the four Spirit Attributes. The world exists through the interplay and circulation of these four attributes, created by the Mother Goddess.<end>"
KO: "불, 물, 땅, 바람…. 이 네 종류가 정령의 속성입니다. 성모신께서 만드신 이 네 속성이 서로 간섭하고 순환함으로써 세계는 이루어져 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火は水に強く、水は土に強く、<line-break>土は風に、風は火に強い。<line-break>　<line-break>おわかりでしょう？<line-break>どれが圧倒的に優位ということはなく、<line-break>うまくバランスをとっているのです。<end>"
EN: "Fire is strong against water, water against earth, earth against wind, and wind against fire. You see? None holds an overwhelming advantage. They maintain a careful balance.<end>"
KO: "불은 물에 강하고, 물은 흙에 강하며, 흙은 바람에, 바람은 불에 강합니다. 아시겠죠? 어느 하나가 압도적으로 우세한 게 아니라 절묘하게 균형을 이루고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1380570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "装備に精霊属性を宿すということは<line-break>そのまま、この四属性の性質を<line-break>盛り込むことになるのです。<line-break>例えば、あなたが火属性の剣を持ち、<line-break>火属性の鎧を身につけたとしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Imbuing equipment with a Spirit Attribute gives it the qualities of one of those four elements. Suppose, for example, that you carried a Fire-element sword and wore Fire-element armor.<end>"
KO: "장비에 정령 속성을 깃들이는 건 그대로 이 네 속성의 성질을 넣는다는 뜻입니다. 예를 들어 당신이 불 속성 검을 들고 불 속성 갑옷을 입었다고 해 보죠.<end>"

## 1380571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは水属性の相手に対して<line-break>有利に戦うことができ、<line-break>　<line-break>火属性のダメージを<line-break>大幅に軽減することができます。<line-break>これは大変有益なことです。<end>"
EN: "You would have an advantage when fighting a Water-element opponent, and Fire-element damage against you would be greatly reduced. This can be extremely useful.<end>"
KO: "당신은 수속성 상대에게 유리하게 싸울 수 있고 화속성 피해를 크게 줄일 수 있습니다. 매우 유익한 효과입니다.<end>"

## 1380572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらにこの精霊属性には、<line-break>装備品ごと、最初から決まった強弱…<line-break>レベルというものが存在します。<line-break>同じ火属性を宿したとしても、<line-break>片手剣なら４、槍なら２、<line-break>ナックルだと６…となります。<end>"
EN: "Spirit Attributes also have a predetermined strength, or level, for each type of equipment. Even with the same Fire element, a one-handed sword has a level of 4, a spear 2, and a knuckle weapon 6.<end>"
KO: "또 이 정령 속성에는 장비마다 처음부터 정해진 강약, 즉 레벨이 있습니다. 같은 불 속성을 품어도 한손검은 4, 창은 2, 너클은 6… 식입니다.<end>"

## 1380573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、同時に、忘れないでください。<line-break>精霊ＬＶが高いということは、<line-break>それだけ純粋に近いという証。<line-break>火属性の剣の攻撃では、<line-break>同じ火属性の敵を<line-break>傷つけることは難しく…<end>"
EN: "But do not forget: a high Spirit ＬＶ is proof that the attribute is closer to purity. Attacks from a Fire-element sword will have difficulty harming an enemy of the same element...<end>"
KO: "하지만 동시에 잊지 마세요. 정령 레벨이 높다는 건 그만큼 순수한 속성에 가깝다는 증거입니다. 불 속성 검의 공격으로 같은 불 속성 적을 상처 입히기는 어려워지고…<end>"

## 1380574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火属性の鎧は、風属性の攻撃を、<line-break>時に倍にもして受けてしまう。<line-break> <line-break>その効果が、さらに、<line-break>属性のレベルによって変動します。<line-break>臨機応変に考えねばならないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "And Fire-element armor may sometimes double the damage taken from Wind-element attacks. The extent of these effects also varies with the attribute level. You must adapt to the situation.<end>"
KO: "화속성 갑옷은 풍속성 공격을 때로는 두 배로 받을 수 있습니다. 그 효과는 속성 레벨에 따라 더 달라집니다. 상황에 맞게 생각해야겠지요.<end>"

## 1380575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふうむ、さすが神官様は説明がうまい。<line-break>口下手な俺なんかが<line-break>でしゃばらなくてよかったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. A priest certainly knows how to explain things. Good thing a poor talker like me didn't butt in.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 역시 신관님은 설명을 잘하는군. 말주변 없는 내가 괜히 나서지 않길 잘했어.<end>"

## 1380576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "装備に精霊の力を宿したくなったら、<line-break>神殿へいらっしゃい。<line-break>お待ちしてますよ。<end>"
EN: "If you ever wish to imbue your equipment with the power of spirits, come to the temple. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "장비에 정령의 힘을 깃들게 하고 싶다면 신전으로 오세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 1380577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、説明はこの辺で<line-break>終わりにしちまって大丈夫かい？<end>"
EN: "All right, is it okay if we end the explanation here?<end>"
KO: "그럼 설명은 이쯤에서 끝내도 괜찮겠지?<end>"

## 1380578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ<end>"
EN: "No<end>"
KO: "아니요<end>"

## 1380579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "네<end>"

## 1380580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　オッシの奴が？<line-break>酒場のツケを、<line-break>俺のと奴のとでワリカンに？<line-break>そうかー！　思い出したぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Ossi said that? Split the tavern tab between the two of us? Oh! Now I remember!<end>"
KO: "응? 오시 녀석이? 술집 외상을 나랑 자기 거랑 반씩 내기로 했다고? 아! 생각났다.<end>"

## 1380581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前、あいつと飲んでて、<line-break>何か約束したような気になってたのは、<line-break>そのことだったか！<line-break>くっ、あいつのほうが飲んでるってのに<line-break>ワリカンとは…。オッシの野郎、<line-break>こっちがあぜんとするほど飲むんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "When we were drinking the other day, I had a feeling I'd promised him something. So that was it! Damn it, he drank more than I did, and we're splitting the bill? That bastard Ossi drinks enough to leave me speechless.<end>"
KO: "전에 그 녀석이랑 술 마시면서 뭔가 약속한 기분이 들었는데 그거였나! 젠장, 자기가 더 마셨으면서 더치페이라니…. 오시 녀석, 어이가 없을 만큼 마셔 대거든.<end>"

## 1380582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おまけにその酒癖ときちゃあ…。<line-break>ま、約束したなら仕方がねえな。<line-break>頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "And don't get me started on what he's like when he's drunk... Well, a promise is a promise. Take care of it for me.<end>"
KO: "게다가 그 술버릇까지…. 뭐, 약속했다면 어쩔 수 없지. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1380583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いな。<line-break>よし、じゃ、あんたには、<line-break>駄賃といったらなんだが…<end>"
EN: "Sorry about that. Here, let me give you something for your trouble...<end>"
KO: "미안하군. 좋아, 그럼 자네에겐 품삯이라 하기엔 뭐하지만….<end>"

## 1380584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつを持ってきな。<end>"
EN: "Take this with you.<end>"
KO: "이걸 가져와.<end>"

## 1380585
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何って、錬剛石だよ、錬剛石！<line-break>そいつがありゃ、お前さんの装備も<line-break>少しはマシになるだろ。<line-break>がんばれよ、ヒヨッコ。<line-break>お前さんが装備を鍛えにくるのを<line-break>楽しみに待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "What, this? It's a Tempering Stone, a Tempering Stone! With that, you can make your equipment a little less pathetic. Keep at it, fledgling. I'll be looking forward to seeing you when you're ready to improve your equipment.<end>"
KO: "뭐긴, 연강석이지 연강석! 그게 있으면 네 장비도 조금은 나아질 거야. 힘내라, 풋내기. 네가 장비를 단련하러 오는 걸 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 1380586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、私たち行きますね。<line-break>またお店で待ってますので<line-break>よろしくお願いしますね。<end>"
EN: "Well, we'd better be going. I'll be waiting for you back at the tavern, so please take care of it.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저희는 가 볼게요. 또 가게에서 기다리고 있을 테니 잘 부탁드려요.<end>"

## 1380587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>まずはスラムの酒場まで<line-break>連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "First, please take me to the tavern in the Slums, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 우선 빈민가 술집까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1380588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒代をマスターに渡すのは<line-break>そのあとにしてください。<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Please give the money for the drinks to the tavern keeper after that. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "술값을 마스터에게 건네는 건 그 뒤에 해 주세요. 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1380599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンタを一生、<line-break>コキ使ってあげるから！<end>"
EN: "I'll work you to the bone for the rest of your life!<end>"
KO: "널 평생, 부려 먹어 줄 테니까!<end>"

## 1380618
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec138-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ、ボリューム５終端<end>"

## 1390001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　広大な大地、バイアシオン大陸。<line-break>　　　　　人と、人ならざる者とが　　　 <line-break>　　　　　　それぞれの生を営み　　　　 <line-break>　　　光と闇がせめぎあう神秘の世界。　　 <line-break>　　古き遺跡の眠る小さな町、オズワルド。 <line-break>　　この穏やかな町の奥で、底知れぬ闇が　 <line-break>　　　　　目覚めようとしていた。　　　 <line-break>　大いなる魂の物語は、ここから始まる……。<end>"
EN: "A vast land―the Vyashion Continent. A mysterious world where humans and nonhuman beings each go about their lives, while light and darkness struggle against one another. Oswald, a small town where ancient ruins lie dormant. Deep within this peaceful town, a fathomless darkness was about to awaken. Here begins the tale of a great soul...<end>"
KO: "광대한 대지 바이아시온 대륙. 인간과 인간 아닌 자들이 각자의 삶을 살아가며 빛과 어둠이 맞부딪치는 신비로운 세계. 오래된 유적이 잠든 작은 마을 오즈월드. 이 평온한 마을 깊은 곳에서 끝을 알 수 없는 어둠이 깨어나려 하고 있었다. 위대한 혼의 이야기는 여기서 시작된다…….<end>"

## 1390002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラは、ゼネテスの機略の前に敗れた。<line-break>だが、青竜軍の兵力は、依然として<line-break>ロストール軍を大きく上回っていた。<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテスの勝利とは関係なく、<line-break>勝機は、いまだカルラにあった。<line-break>しかし、カルラ軍は進軍しなかった。<end>"
EN: "Karula had been defeated by Xenetes's stratagem. Yet the Azure Dragon Army still vastly outnumbered Rostorl's forces. Despite Xenetes's victory, Karula still had a chance to win. But Karula's Army did not advance.<end>"
KO: "카를라는 제네테스의 계략 앞에 패했다. 하지만 청룡군의 병력은 여전히 로스톨군을 크게 웃돌았다. 제네테스의 승리와 관계없이 승기는 아직 카를라에게 있었다. 그러나 카를라군은 진군하지 않았다.<end>"

## 1390003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア死す。<line-break>ゼネテスの放った<line-break>偽情報を裏付けるかのごとく、<line-break>　<line-break>ゼグナ鉱山で<line-break>闇の神器を探索した後、<line-break>カルラ軍に合流するはずだった<line-break>ネメアが、ついに戻らなかったのである。<end><end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead. As if confirming the false report spread by Xenetes, Nemea, who was supposed to join Karula's Army after searching Zegna Mine for the Artifact of Darkness, never returned.<end><end>"
KO: "네메아 사망. 제네테스가 흘린 거짓 정보를 뒷받침하듯, 제그나 광산에서 어둠의 신기를 탐색한 뒤 카를라군에 합류할 예정이던 네메아는 끝내 돌아오지 않았다.<end><end>"

## 1390004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一方、ロストールでは、<line-break>ファーロス家の勢力拡大を恐れた<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンが、<line-break>ファーロス家に反旗を翻した。<end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, in Rostorl, Lemghon, Duke of Elienai, feared the growing power of House Pharos and rose against it.<end>"
KO: "한편 로스톨에서는 파로스 가문의 세력 확대를 두려워한 엘리에나이 공 레무온이 파로스 가문에 반기를 들었다.<end>"

## 1390005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これにより、王妃エリスは殺害され、<end>"
EN: "As a result, Queen Eris was murdered,<end>"
KO: "이로 인해 왕비 에리스는 살해되고,<end>"

## 1390006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帰還の途についていたゼネテスも<line-break>無実の罪で処刑された。<end>"
EN: "and Xenetes, who was on his way home, was executed for a crime he did not commit.<end>"
KO: "귀환하던 제네테스도 억울한 죄로 처형되었다.<end>"

## 1390007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "政敵を葬り去り、ロストールを<line-break>掌中に収めたレムオンであったが<line-break>そのレムオンも、謎の失踪を遂げた。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon had eliminated his political rivals and brought Rostorl under his control. Yet he, too, mysteriously disappeared.<end>"
KO: "정적을 제거하고 로스톨을 손에 넣은 레무온이었지만, 그 레무온마저 의문의 실종을 맞았다.<end>"

## 1390008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　指導者を失った両国は<line-break>　　　一時的な停戦状態に陥るのだった。<end>"
EN: "With both nations left leaderless, a temporary ceasefire took hold.<end>"
KO: "지도자를 잃은 두 나라는 일시적인 휴전 상태에 들어갔다.<end>"

## 1390009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　青竜将軍カルラ率いるディンガル軍と<line-break>　　　ゼネテス率いるロストール軍は<line-break>　　　竜骨の砂漠南の街道で対峙した。<line-break>　<line-break>　　レムオンら有力貴族の出兵拒否により<line-break>　　　　ロストール軍は、青竜軍の<line-break>　　　５分の１ほどの戦力だったが…、<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Army, led by Azure Dragon General Karula, faced the Rostorl Army under Xenetes on the road south of the Dragonbone Desert. Because Lemghon and other powerful nobles had refused to send troops, the Rostorl Army had only about one-fifth the strength of the Azure Dragon Army. But...<end>"
KO: "청룡 장군 카를라가 이끄는 딘갈군과 제네테스가 이끄는 로스톨군은 용골의 사막 남쪽 가도에서 대치했다. 레무온 등 유력 귀족들이 출병을 거부해 로스톨군의 전력은 청룡군의 5분의 1 정도였지만….<end>"

## 1390010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　足止めのために空の本陣を敷き、併せて<line-break>　ネメア死すという偽情報を流すことで青竜軍の<line-break>　動揺を誘い、そこに奇襲をかけるという機略で<line-break>　　　　辛くも緒戦の勝利をものにした。<end>"
EN: "An empty command camp was established to hold the enemy back, and a false report of Nemea's death was spread to unsettle the Azure Dragon Army. A surprise attack amid the resulting confusion narrowly secured victory in the opening battle.<end>"
KO: "발을 묶기 위해 빈 본진을 설치하고, 네메아가 죽었다는 거짓 정보를 흘려 청룡군의 동요를 유도한 뒤 기습을 가하는 계략으로 가까스로 첫 전투의 승리를 거머쥐었다.<end>"

## 1390011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　だが、青竜軍の兵力は、依然として<line-break>　ロストール軍を大きく上回っており、緒戦の<line-break>勝敗に関係なく、勝機は、いまだカルラにあった。<line-break>　　しかし、カルラ軍は、進軍しなかった。<end>"
EN: "Even so, the Azure Dragon Army still vastly outnumbered the Rostorl Army. Regardless of the opening battle's outcome, the advantage still lay with Karula. Yet Karula's Army did not advance.<end>"
KO: "하지만 청룡군의 병력은 여전히 로스톨군을 크게 웃돌았고, 첫 전투의 승패와 관계없이 승기는 아직 카를라에게 있었다. 그러나 카를라군은 진군하지 않았다.<end>"

## 1390018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　こうして……<line-break>　　後にリューガの変と呼ばれることになる<line-break>　　　　　　騒動は終わりを告げた。<line-break> <end>"

## 1390019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この混乱の最中、シャリと<line-break>闇の力に魅入られた王女アトレイアが<line-break>現れ、王宮に殺戮の嵐を巻き起こした。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Amid the chaos, Shalli and Princess Atleia, who had fallen under the spell of the power of darkness, appeared and unleashed a storm of slaughter in the royal palace.<end>"
KO: "이 혼란 속에서 샤리와 어둠의 힘에 매혹된 아트레이아 공주가 나타나 왕궁에 살육의 폭풍을 일으켰다.<end>"

## 1390022

## 1390024

KO: "엘리에나이 공 레무온,<end>"

## 1390025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 軍の主戦力であった多くの竜騎士……<line-break>　　　失われたものはあまりにも大きく、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　 ロストールの国力は著しく低下した。<end>"
EN: "and the many Dragon Knights who had formed the army's main fighting force... The losses were far too great, and Rostorl's national strength declined sharply.<end>"
KO: "군의 주전력이었던 수많은 용기사…. 잃은 것이 너무 컸고, 로스톨의 국력은 크게 약화되었다.<end>"

## 1390026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　一連の事件は、大いなる闇の力の復活に<line-break>　深く関与していたのだが、それを知るものは<line-break>　　　わずかに生き残った当事者以外には<line-break>　　　　　　　　 いなかった。<end>"
EN: "These events were deeply connected to the resurgence of the great power of darkness, but no one knew of that connection except the few directly involved who survived.<end>"
KO: "일련의 사건은 거대한 어둠의 힘의 부활과 깊이 관련되어 있었지만, 그 사실을 아는 이는 살아남은 당사자 몇 명뿐이었다.<end>"

## 1390027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 一方、ゼネテスに敗れたカルラ軍には<line-break>　　　　　兵の動揺が広がっていた。<end>"

KO: "한편 제네테스에게 패한 카를라군에는 병사들의 동요가 퍼지고 있었다.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公レムオン、<end>"
EN: "Lemghon, Duke of Elienai,<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공 레무온,<end>"

## 1390037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 軍の主戦力であった多くの竜騎士……<line-break>　　　失われたものはあまりにも大きく、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　 ロストールの国力は著しく低下した。<end>"
EN: "and the many Dragon Knights who had formed the army's main fighting force... The losses were far too great, and Rostorl's national strength declined sharply.<end>"
KO: "군의 주전력이었던 수많은 용기사…. 잃은 것이 너무 컸고, 로스톨의 국력은 크게 약화되었다.<end>"

## 1390038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　一連の事件は、大いなる闇の力の復活に<line-break>　深く関与していたのだが、それを知るものは<line-break>　　　わずかに生き残った当事者以外には<line-break>　　　　　　　　 いなかった。<end>"
EN: "These events were deeply connected to the resurgence of the great power of darkness, but no one knew of that connection except the few directly involved who survived.<end>"
KO: "일련의 사건은 거대한 어둠의 힘의 부활과 깊이 관련되어 있었지만, 그 사실을 아는 이는 살아남은 당사자 몇 명뿐이었다.<end>"

## 1390039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 一方、ゼネテスに敗れたカルラ軍には<line-break>　　　　　兵の動揺が広がっていた。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, unrest was spreading among the soldiers of Karula's Army after its defeat by Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "한편 제네테스에게 패한 카를라군에는 병사들의 동요가 퍼지고 있었다.<end>"

## 1390040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア死す。<line-break>ゼネテスの放った<line-break>偽情報を裏付けるかのごとく、<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead. As if confirming the false report spread by Xenetes,<end>"
KO: "네메아 사망. 제네테스가 퍼뜨린 거짓 정보를 뒷받침하듯….<end>"

## 1390041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼグナ鉱山で<line-break>闇の神器を探索した後、<line-break>カルラ軍に合流するはずだった<line-break>ネメアがついに戻らなかったのである。<end>"
EN: "Nemea, who was supposed to join Karula's Army after searching Zegna Mine for the Artifact of Darkness, never returned.<end>"
KO: "제그나 광산에서 어둠의 신기를 탐색한 뒤 카를라군에 합류하기로 했던 네메아는 끝내 돌아오지 않았다.<end>"

## 1390042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　指導者を失った両国は<line-break>　　　一時的な停戦状態に陥るのだった。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "With both nations left leaderless, a temporary ceasefire took hold.<end>"
KO: "지도자를 잃은 두 나라는 일시적인 휴전 상태에 들어갔다.<end>"

## 1390043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　こうして……<line-break>　　後にリューガの変と呼ばれることになる<line-break>　　　　　　騒動は終わりを告げた。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "And so... the turmoil that would later become known as the Ryuga Incident came to an end.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 해서…… 훗날 류가의 변이라 불리게 될 소동은 막을 내렸다.<end>"

## 1390044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この混乱の最中、シャリと<line-break>闇の力に魅入られた王女アトレイアが<line-break>現れ、王宮に殺戮の嵐を巻き起こした。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Amid the chaos, Shalli and Princess Atleia, who had fallen under the spell of the power of darkness, appeared and unleashed a storm of slaughter in the royal palace.<end>"
KO: "이 혼란 속에서 샤리와 어둠의 힘에 매혹된 아트레이아 공주가 나타나 왕궁에 살육의 폭풍을 일으켰다.<end>"

## 1390045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国王セルモノー、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "King Selmono,<end>"
KO: "국왕 셀모노,<end>"

## 1390046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女ティアナ、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Princess Tiana,<end>"
KO: "왕녀 티아나,<end>"

## 1390047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令ゼネテスは<line-break>無事だったものの…<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Though Xenetes, commander-in-chief of Rostorl's army, escaped unharmed...<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령 제네테스는 무사했지만…<end>"

## 1390048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃エリス、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Queen Eris,<end>"
KO: "왕비 에리스,<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네메아 사망. 제네테스가 흘린 거짓 정보를 뒷받침하듯 제그나 광산에서 어둠의 신기를 탐색한 뒤 카를라군에 합류할 예정이던 네메아가 끝내 돌아오지 않은 것이다.<end>"

## 1390079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　指導者を失った両国は<line-break>　　　一時的な停戦状態に陥るのだった。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "With both nations left leaderless, a temporary ceasefire took hold.<end>"
KO: "지도자를 잃은 두 나라는 일시적인 휴전 상태에 들어갔다.<end>"

## 1390080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　こうして……<line-break>　　後にリューガの変と呼ばれることになる<line-break>　　　　　　騒動は終わりを告げた。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "And so... the turmoil that would later become known as the Ryuga Incident came to an end.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 해서…… 훗날 류가의 변이라 불리게 될 소동은 막을 내렸다.<end>"

## 1390081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この混乱の最中、シャリと<line-break>闇の力に魅入られた王女ティアナが<line-break>現れ、王宮に殺戮の嵐を巻き起こした。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Amid the chaos, Shalli and Princess Tiana, who had fallen under the spell of the power of darkness, appeared and unleashed a storm of slaughter in the royal palace.<end>"
KO: "이 혼란 속에서 샤리와 어둠의 힘에 매혹된 공주 티아나가 나타나 왕궁에 살육의 폭풍을 일으켰다.<end>"

## 1390082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国王セルモノー、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "King Selmono,<end>"
KO: "국왕 셀모노,<end>"

## 1390083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女アトレイア、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia,<end>"
KO: "왕녀 아트레이아,<end>"

## 1390085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオンは、ダルケニスであることを<line-break>暴露され、表舞台から身を引くことを<line-break>余儀なくされた。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Lemghon was exposed as a Darkenith and forced to withdraw from public life.<end>"
KO: "레무온은 다르케니스라는 사실이 폭로되어, 무대에서 물러날 수밖에 없었다.<end>"

## 1390086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃エリス、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Queen Eris,<end>"
KO: "왕비 에리스,<end>"

## 1390087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令ゼネテス、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Xenetes, commander-in-chief of Rostorl's army,<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령관 제네테스,<end>"

## 1390088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 軍の主戦力であった多くの竜騎士……<line-break>　　　失われたものはあまりにも大きく、<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　 ロストールの国力は著しく低下した。<end>"
EN: "and many of the Dragon Knights who had formed the army's main fighting force... The losses were far too great, and Rostorl's national power declined sharply.<end>"
KO: "군의 주전력이었던 수많은 용기사…. 잃은 것이 너무 컸고, 로스톨의 국력은 크게 약화되었다.<end>"

## 1390089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　一連の事件は、大いなる闇の力の復活に<line-break>　深く関与していたのだが、それを知るものは<line-break>　　　わずかに生き残った当事者以外には<line-break>　　　　　　　　 いなかった。<end>"
EN: "These events were closely tied to the revival of a great power of darkness, but no one knew this except the few surviving people directly involved.<end>"
KO: "일련의 사건은 거대한 어둠의 힘의 부활과 깊이 관련되어 있었지만, 그 사실을 아는 이는 살아남은 당사자 몇 명뿐이었다.<end>"

## 1390090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 一方、ゼネテスに敗れたカルラ軍には<line-break>　　　　　兵の動揺が広がっていた。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, unrest was spreading among the soldiers of Karula's Army after its defeat by Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "한편 제네테스에게 패한 카를라군에는 병사들의 동요가 퍼지고 있었다.<end>"

## 1390091

## 1390119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラの侵攻、リューガの変、<line-break>そして、セルモノーの死。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Karula's invasion, the Ryuga Incident, and the death of Selmono.<end>"
KO: "카를라의 침공, 류가의 변, 그리고 셀모노의 죽음.<end>"

## 1390120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　国政の中心人物を次々と失った<line-break>　　 ロストールの混迷はここに極まった。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　　　　　　深刻な人材不足…<line-break>　　　　　壊滅的な被害を受けた軍…<line-break>　　　　　重く圧し掛かる財政負担…<line-break>　　不気味な沈黙を続けるディンガル帝国…<end>"
EN: "Having lost one central figure in government after another, Rostorl's turmoil now reached its height. A severe shortage of capable personnel... An army devastated... A crushing financial burden... The Dyneskal Empire maintaining an ominous silence...<end>"
KO: "국정의 중심 인물들을 잇달아 잃은 로스톨의 혼란은 극에 달했다. 심각한 인재 부족… 괴멸적인 피해를 입은 군대… 무겁게 짓누르는 재정 부담… 불길한 침묵을 이어 가는 딘갈 제국….<end>"

## 1390121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残された貴族達に山積した問題を<line-break>解決する力はなく、王女ティアナが<line-break>ロストールの王位を継承することに<line-break>異を唱えるものもいなかった。<end>"
EN: "The nobles who remained had no power to resolve the mounting crises, and none opposed Princess Tiana's succession to the throne of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "남은 귀족들에게 산적한 문제를 해결할 힘은 없었고, 티아나 왕녀가 로스톨 왕위를 계승하는 데 이의를 제기하는 자도 없었다.<end>"

## 1390122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 ティアナの王位継承は、ロストールの<line-break>　　　 貴族制の終焉を暗に示していた。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Tiana's accession was an implicit sign of the end of Rostorl's aristocratic system.<end>"
KO: "티아나의 왕위 계승은 로스톨 귀족제의 종말을 암시하고 있었다.<end>"

## 1390123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラの侵攻、リューガの変、<line-break>そして、セルモノーの死。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Karula's invasion, the Ryuga Incident, and the death of Selmono.<end>"
KO: "카를라의 침공, 류가의 변, 그리고 셀모노의 죽음.<end>"

## 1390124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　国政の中心人物を次々と失った<line-break>　　 ロストールの混迷はここに極まった。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　　　　　　深刻な人材不足…<line-break>　　　　　壊滅的な被害を受けた軍…<line-break>　　　　　重く圧し掛かる財政負担…<line-break>　　不気味な沈黙を続けるディンガル帝国…<end>"
EN: "Having lost one central figure in government after another, Rostorl's turmoil now reached its height. A severe shortage of capable personnel... An army devastated... A crushing financial burden... The Dyneskal Empire maintaining an ominous silence...<end>"
KO: "국정의 중심 인물들을 잇달아 잃은 로스톨의 혼란은 극에 달했다. 심각한 인재 부족… 괴멸적인 피해를 입은 군대… 무겁게 짓누르는 재정 부담… 불길한 침묵을 이어 가는 딘갈 제국….<end>"

## 1390125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残された貴族達に山積した問題を<line-break>解決する力はなく、王女アトレイアが<line-break>ロストールの王位を継承することに<line-break>異を唱えるものもいなかった。<end>"
EN: "The nobles who remained had no power to resolve the mounting crises, and none opposed Princess Atleia's succession to the throne of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "남은 귀족들에게 산적한 문제를 해결할 힘은 없었고, 아트레이아 왕녀가 로스톨의 왕위를 잇는 데 반대하는 자도 없었다.<end>"

## 1390126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　アトレイアの王位継承は、ロストールの<line-break>　　　 貴族制の終焉を暗に示していた。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Atleia's accession was an implicit sign of the end of Rostorl's aristocratic system.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아의 왕위 계승은 로스톨 귀족제의 종말을 암시하고 있었다.<end>"

## 1390127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア死す。<line-break>この一報がディンガル帝国に与えた<line-break>打撃ははかりしれないものがあった。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead. The blow this news dealt the Dyneskal Empire was beyond measure.<end>"
KO: "네메아 사망. 이 소식이 딩갈 제국에 준 충격은 헤아릴 수 없을 정도였다.<end>"

## 1390128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア亡きディンガルに種族平等を<line-break>実現する力なし…<line-break><line-break><line-break>そう考えたジラーク将軍は、制圧した<line-break>神聖都市アルノートゥンにコーンス族の<line-break>国を起こそうと、ディンガル帝国に<line-break>反旗をひるがえした。<end>"
EN: "Without Nemea, Dyneskal has no power to achieve equality among the races... Convinced of this, General Zsilak rebelled against the Dyneskal Empire, seeking to establish a nation for the Konsu in the conquered holy city of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "네메아가 없는 딩갈에는 종족 평등을 실현할 힘이 없다…. 그렇게 생각한 지라크 장군은 제압한 성도 알노툰에 콘스족의 나라를 세우려 딩갈 제국에 반기를 들었다.<end>"

## 1390129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを予期していたディンガル帝国宰相<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ・ベルラインは、<line-break>ロストール攻略から帰還させていた<line-break>青竜軍にジラークの討伐を命じた。<end>"
EN: "Having foreseen this, Beelzeva Berline, Chancellor of the Dyneskal Empire, ordered the Azure Dragon Army, recalled from the Rostorl Campaign, to eliminate Zsilak.<end>"
KO: "이를 예견한 딘갈 제국 재상 벨제바 벨라인은 로스톨 공략에서 귀환시킨 청룡군에 지라크 토벌을 명했다.<end>"

## 1390130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国政の中心人物を失い、形骸化したロストール…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Rostorl, hollowed out by the loss of its leading figures in government...<end>"
KO: "국정의 중심인물을 잃고 유명무실해진 로스톨….<end>"

## 1390131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　英雄を失い、迷走を始めたディンガル…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Dyneskal, deprived of its hero and beginning to lose its way...<end>"
KO: "영웅을 잃고 방황하기 시작한 딘갈…<end>"

## 1390132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　そして、<line-break>種族平等をかざし反乱を起こしたコーンス族…<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "And the Konsu, who rose in rebellion under the banner of racial equality...<end>"
KO: "그리고 종족 평등을 내걸고 반란을 일으킨 콘스족….<end>"

## 1390133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終わらぬ戦乱はバイアシオンの大地を赤く染め、<line-break>　　　混乱と恐怖のみを拡大させていく。<line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "The unending wars stained the lands of Vyashion red, spreading only chaos and fear.<end>"
KO: "끝나지 않는 전란은 바이아시온의 대지를 붉게 물들이고 혼란과 공포만 키워 갔다.<end>"

## 1390134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アルノートゥンから帰還したカルラは、<line-break>今回の反乱はジラーク個人の罪であると、<line-break>宰相ベルゼーヴァに報告した。<line-break><line-break>それと同時に、コーンスに対する<line-break>あらゆる迫害を禁じるよう提案した。<end>"
EN: "Upon returning from Alnortun, Karula reported to Chancellor Beelzeva that Zsilak alone was responsible for the rebellion. At the same time, she proposed a ban on all persecution of the Konsu.<end>"
KO: "알노툰에서 귀환한 카를라는 이번 반란은 지라크 개인의 죄라고 재상 벨제바에게 보고했다. 동시에 콘스에 대한 모든 박해를 금지하도록 제안했다.<end>"

## 1390135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　 ベルゼーヴァはこれを承認。<line-break>　　　　　　捕虜の解放を命じた。<line-break><line-break><line-break>　　　　コーンス達は、傷ついた身体を<line-break>　　 引きずりながら、それぞれの故郷へと<line-break>　　　　　　　 去っていった。<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva approved the proposal and ordered the prisoners released. Dragging their wounded bodies, the Konsu departed for their respective homelands.<end>"
KO: "벨제바는 이를 승인하고 포로의 석방을 명했다. 콘스들은 상처 입은 몸을 끌면서 저마다의 고향으로 떠나갔다.<end>"

## 1390136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ここにコーンスによる世界統一の野望は<line-break>　　　　　　　潰えたのだった。<end>"
EN: "Thus ended the ambition of the Konsu to unite the world.<end>"
KO: "이로써 콘스의 세계 통일 야망은 무너졌다.<end>"

## 1390137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アルノートゥンのコーンスの反乱は、<line-break>首謀者であるジラークの死によって<line-break>その幕を閉じた。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "The Konsu Rebellion in Alnortun came to an end with the death of its ringleader, Zsilak.<end>"
KO: "알노툰의 콘스 반란은 주모자 지라크의 죽음으로 막을 내렸다.<end>"

## 1390138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　残されたコーンス達は離散し、<line-break>　　　ディンガル軍も<line-break>　　　これを追撃することは無かった。<line-break><line-break>　　　<value:$2B>の<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　その活躍により、人類対コーンスの<line-break>　　　全面戦争は回避されたのだった。<end>"
EN: "The surviving Konsu scattered, and the Dyneskal Army did not pursue them. The deeds of <value:$2B> <value:$28> averted an all-out war between humanity and the Konsu.<end>"
KO: "남겨진 콘스들은 흩어졌고, 딩갈군도 그들을 추격하지 않았다. <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>. 그 활약으로 인류와 콘스의 전면전은 피할 수 있었다.<end>"

## 1390139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神聖都市アルノートゥン……<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <end>"
EN: "Alnortun, the holy city...<end>"
KO: "신성도시 알노툰……<end>"

## 1390140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ率いる討伐軍は、<line-break>この神聖都市に<line-break>驚くべき速さで進軍した。<line-break><line-break>精鋭中の精鋭でかためられた討伐軍は、<line-break>ジラーク達に守備を固める暇さえ<line-break>与えなかった。<end>"
EN: "The punitive force led by Karula advanced upon the holy city with astonishing speed. Composed of the finest troops, it gave Zsilak and his followers no time even to fortify their defenses.<end>"
KO: "카를라가 이끄는 토벌군은 이 성스러운 도시에 놀라운 속도로 진군했다. 정예 중의 정예로 구성된 토벌군은 지라크 일행에게 수비를 굳힐 틈조차 주지 않았다.<end>"

## 1390142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　叛乱鎮圧後、ディンガル帝国は<line-break>　　 ただちにコーンス族討伐令を発した。<line-break>　　　　　　 ここにコーンス族は<line-break>　　　　　 追われる存在となった…<end>"
EN: "Once the rebellion had been suppressed, the Dyneskal Empire immediately issued an edict ordering a campaign against the Konsu. From that moment, the Konsu became a hunted people...<end>"
KO: "반란 진압 후 딘갈 제국은 즉시 콘스족 토벌령을 내렸다. 이로써 콘스족은 쫓기는 존재가 되었다…<end>"

## 1390143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神聖都市アルノートゥン……<line-break><line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Alnortun, the holy city...<end>"
KO: "신성도시 알노툰……<end>"

## 1390144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ率いる討伐軍は、<line-break>この神聖都市に<line-break>驚くべき速さで進軍した。<line-break><line-break>精鋭中の精鋭でかためられた討伐軍は、<line-break>ジラーク達に守備を固める暇さえ<line-break>与えなかった。<end>"
EN: "The punitive force led by Karula advanced upon the holy city with astonishing speed. Composed of the finest troops, it gave Zsilak and his followers no time even to fortify their defenses.<end>"
KO: "카를라가 이끄는 토벌군은 이 성스러운 도시에 놀라운 속도로 진군했다. 정예 중의 정예로 구성된 토벌군은 지라크 일행에게 수비를 굳힐 틈조차 주지 않았다.<end>"

## 1390146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークの考えに賛同し、<line-break>その旗の元に参集していたコーンス達も<line-break>ジラークと運命を供にした。<line-break><line-break>その中には<line-break><value:$28>の友、<line-break>ナッジの姿もあった。<end>"
EN: "The Konsu who had embraced Zsilak's cause and rallied beneath his banner shared his fate. Among them was Nadge, a friend of <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 생각에 동의해 그 깃발 아래 모였던 콘스들도 지라크와 운명을 함께했다. 그중에는 <value:$28>의 친구 나지의 모습도 있었다.<end>"

## 1390147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　叛乱鎮圧後、ディンガル帝国は<line-break>　　 ただちにコーンス族討伐令を発した。<line-break>　　　　　　 ここにコーンス族は<line-break>　　　　　 追われる存在となった…<end>"
EN: "Once the rebellion had been suppressed, the Dyneskal Empire immediately issued an edict ordering a campaign against the Konsu. From that moment, the Konsu became a hunted people...<end>"
KO: "반란 진압 후 딘갈 제국은 즉시 콘스족 토벌령을 내렸다. 이로써 콘스족은 쫓기는 존재가 되었다…<end>"

## 1390149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　 だが…<line-break><line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "But...<end>"
KO: "하지만…<end>"

## 1390150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　 姿を消した人々が戻ってくることはなく…<line-break>　　魔物達の数は増えていくばかりだった。<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Those who had vanished never returned... and the monsters only continued to multiply.<end>"
KO: "사라진 사람들은 돌아오지 않았고… 마물의 수만 계속 늘어 갔다.<end>"

## 1390151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　英雄の力を持ってしても、闇の勢力を<line-break>　　　　 滅ぼすことはできなかった。<line-break>　　　この事実は、戦乱に疲れた人々を<line-break>　　 　さらに打ちのめすこととなった。<line-break>　　　　　　深まりゆく闇の気配…。<line-break>　まことしやかに伝えられる破壊神復活の噂。<line-break><end>"
EN: "Not even the hero's power could destroy the forces of darkness. This truth dealt yet another blow to a people already exhausted by war. The presence of darkness grew ever stronger... Rumors of the God of Destruction's return circulated as though they were true.<end>"
KO: "영웅의 힘으로도 어둠의 세력을 멸망시키지는 못했다. 이 사실은 전란에 지친 사람들을 더욱 절망에 빠뜨렸다. 짙어져 가는 어둠의 기운…. 사실처럼 퍼지는 파괴신 부활의 소문.<end>"

## 1390152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　すべての人々が終末への予感を<line-break>　　　　　　抱き始めていた…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone began to sense that the end was near...<end>"
KO: "모든 사람들이 종말의 예감을 품기 시작했다….<end>"

## 1390153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec139-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"
